# Review (backend)

How each rule from `../CLAUDE.md` is enforced, and the test that proves it. Verify with `pnpm test`; the invariants live in
`tests/integration/invariants.test.ts`, one test per rule, and `scripts/gate.sh` fails if any is missing or skipped.

| # | Rule | Enforced in | Proven by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Stamp only inside the geofence; exempt accounts are visible only to themselves | `services/geofence.ts`, `stamps.ts#createStamp`, `canSeeStamp` (countable) | invariants "rule 1"; `stamps.test.ts` per-place radius; `compliance.test.ts` exempt accounts |
| 2 | Private account's stamps and places reach only full access, in every payload | `privacy.ts` (`profileAccess`, `canSeeStamp`), `stamps.ts#assertVisible`, feed query | invariants "rule 2"; `privacy.test.ts` (8 viewers x 6 payloads, private and public); `tests/unit/privacy.test.ts` |
| 3 | Blocks override everything except staff | `blocks.ts`, `privacy.ts`, feed `blocking` / `blockedBy` filters | invariants "rule 3"; `privacy.test.ts` "blocking by handle" |
| 4 | Hidden, never deleted; the moderation log is append-only | DB triggers (`photo_no_delete`, `moderation_event_*`, `photo_immutable`), `removeStamp` | invariants "rule 4"; `db-guards.test.ts`; `moderation.test.ts` |
| 5 | EXIF and GPS stripped from every upload | `services/image.ts#processImage` (full re-encode) | invariants "rule 5" (direct and through the stamp path) |
| 6 | No user coordinates, storage keys or user ids in payloads | explicit selects in `stamps.ts`, signed URLs only | invariants "rule 6" (every payload scanned); contract test (`.strict()` schemas) |
| 7 | Reputation visible only to the user and moderators | `people.ts#getProfile`, `me.ts` | invariants "rule 7" |
| 8 | Under-21s blocked at signup, nothing stored | `auth.ts#signup` (first statement), `lib/age.ts`, no birthdate column | invariants "rule 8"; `age-gate.test.ts` (row counts before and after); `tests/unit/age.test.ts` |
| 9 | Feed is chronological from people you follow | `stamps.ts#listFeed` (order by `created_at desc, id desc`, no other input) | invariants "rule 9"; `stamps.test.ts` paging |
| 10 | Likes and badges never affect anything else | no code reads likes or `verified_at` outside their own payloads | invariants "rule 10" |

| 11 | With `SOCIAL_ENABLED` off, nothing of another person's passport reaches anyone but its owner | `privacy.ts` (`profileAccess`, `canSeeStamp`), `http.ts` (social routes 404) | invariants "rule 11"; `personal-mode.test.ts` |

## Other things worth checking

- The contract test (`contracts.test.ts`) calls every route and fails if a route exists without a schema in
  `src/contracts/api.ts`, or a response drifts from it.
- `seed.test.ts` runs the real seed and checks it: deterministic, anchored to now, geofence-valid, privacy and block rules
  hold on the seeded data.
- Migrations in `prisma/migrations/2026100*`: read `strip_to_nostos` (drops), `stamps` (triggers, CHECKs), `flag_category`,
  `stamp_photos`, `pinned_stamps`, `neighborhoods`.
- Session 2 data features: up to six photos per stamp (`stamp-photos.test.ts`), pinned stamps (`pins.test.ts`), neighborhoods and
  collections (`neighborhoods.test.ts`), the place page (`stamps.test.ts` "my place page"), oldest-first page and slot
  (`tests/unit/layout.test.ts`, `stamps.test.ts`).
- The server saves the phone's coordinates with each stamp (`photo.lat`, `photo.lng`) as the record of the geofence check. They are in
  no payload (invariant 6). Whether that is acceptable is OPEN-QUESTIONS L4 and L6.
- Open questions that affect the backend: `../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` (L1 songs terms, L2 age gate, L3 SAFE for Kids Act, L4
  geolocation disclosures).

## Codex lane review, 2026-10-02

The default is personal mode. Social-mode rules in the table above apply only with SOCIAL_ENABLED enabled.
Personal moderation/admin callers are 404s, foreign mutations have identical status/body/headers, photos are creation-only,
and deferred live-content constraints protect edit/removal races, including repeatable-read snapshots.
Live songs must have non-whitespace titles and artists at the database boundary.

Regression coverage: personal-moderation.test.ts, stamp-mutation-visibility.test.ts, stamp-photo-creation.test.ts,
stamp-content.test.ts, backend-audit.test.ts and personal-staff-pages.test.ts. Invariant rule 11 is stronger.

====================================================================================================
== DECISIONS.md
====================================================================================================

# Decisions (backend)

Judgment calls made while building Nostos from the Last Call social layer. The Last Call history (consensus, accuracy,
owners, promos, featuring) is in the archive repo and is not repeated here. Open product and legal questions are in
`../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.

## Data model

- **Stamp = `photo`, place = `venue`.** The tables keep their names to avoid migration churn; the API, contract and
  UI say stamp and place. Migrations are forward-only (`strip_to_nostos`, `stamps`, `flag_category`), so the
  hand-written trigger history stays valid. No `migrate reset` outside development.
- **`ContentType` keeps its COMMENT and REPORT values.** The append-only moderation log may reference them, so the enum is
  not rewritten. The API only accepts STAMP and USER and maps STAMP to the database's PHOTO.
- **Stamps are immutable except caption, song, moderation state and `removed_at`** (a trigger). The optional `rating` is
  stored but immutable and never shown in v1.
- **A stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song** (CHECK, and a service check with a clear message). A photo has both key
  and type or neither. A song needs title and artist; art must be an https link (the service narrows it to Apple's
  `mzstatic.com` CDN so a stamp can't embed a tracking pixel).
- **Per-place geofence** (`geofence_meters`, default 100). The server checks the place's own radius.
- **Cooldown**: one stamp per person per place per 15 minutes, plus 20 stamps an hour. Likes 200 an hour, song search 120 an hour.

## Privacy

- **Visibility is the account's privacy, nothing else.** No per-stamp toggle, so the `shared` column is gone.
- **A stamp you may not see is a 404, never a 403,** so existence isn't revealed. Lists of a private account's stamps,
  places and followers answer 403 `private_account` (the account exists and is findable by handle anyway).
- **Reporting and liking require visibility.** A stranger can't probe a private account's stamps through `/api/flags` or
  the like endpoint.
- **A pending follow request grants nothing.** Staff see everything, including blocked pairs and hidden stamps.
- **The limited profile** is handle, verified badge and a Follow button. Search matches a private account's display name
  only for people who may see it.
- **No "Anonymous" anywhere.** A stamp is only returned with its author's full profile view, because it is only returned
  to people who may see that profile. Block-by-content is gone; blocks are by handle.
- **Reputation** stays as a column (default 0.5), visible only to the user and moderators. Nothing recomputes it now; it
  gates eligibility for the 3-flag auto-hide.

## Safety

- **The 21+ gate is read-once.** The signup body carries a birthdate; `signup` checks it first (before any database access),
  throws 403 `underage` if needed, and the value goes out of scope. There is no birthdate column. A refused attempt writes
  nothing, and the refusal precedes the duplicate-email check so it reveals nothing about accounts. "Today" is the New York
  date. The mobile app also checks and never sends an under-21 birthdate.
- **Report categories** (`flag.category`): underage and stalking reports sort to the top of the queue. The reason text is
  still free (3 to 300 characters).
- **Song search** (`/api/songs/search`) is a proxy to Apple's iTunes Search API: signed-in only, rate limited, 3 second
  timeout, only the search term goes upstream (no cookies, no user id), results with objectionable text are dropped, and a
  failure is a 503. The terms question is open (OPEN-QUESTIONS L1).
- **Moderation** keeps its rules: three flags from three eligible accounts auto-hide a stamp, the log is append-only,
  nothing is deleted. Stamps of deleted accounts stay hidden for good.
- **No error-reporting SDK on the backend.** Unhandled errors log to the console. Sentry is wired only in the app, off by
  default.

## Web

The web side keeps only what the iPhone app and App Review need: sign-in, signup (with the birthdate field), the legal
pages (drafts, rewritten to describe Nostos, banner says so), account deletion, `/mod` and `/admin` (verified badge tools).
The Last Call web map, venue pages, owner dashboards and people pages are gone, and so is Leaflet.

## Seed

`prisma/seed.ts` exports `seed(db, options)` so tests can run it. Song seeds carry title and artist only (no art URL and no
source id), so no made-up links exist. Seeded stamps use generated placeholder images through the configured storage driver.

## Collections and places (session 2)

- **Neighborhood boundaries: NYC's Neighborhood Tabulation Areas (2020), NYC Open Data dataset 9nt8-h7nd** (public domain city
  data, `https://data.cityofnewyork.us/resource/9nt8-h7nd.geojson`). Chosen over OSM relations because it is one consistent,
  complete, city-maintained partition with stable codes (BK0101 and so on), where OSM's neighborhood relations overlap and
  leave gaps. We keep residential areas (ntatype 0) and parks and open space (ntatype 9), the outer ring of each polygon only,
  simplified with Douglas-Peucker to about 6 m and rounded to 5 decimals: 237 areas in 400 KB (`prisma/data/neighborhoods.json`,
  built by `scripts/build-neighborhoods.mjs`, loaded by `pnpm neighborhoods:import`). Holes and slivers are dropped, so a place
  within 6 m of a border can land on the wrong side. Cemeteries, airports and other types are left out (a place there has no neighborhood).
- **Progress** = distinct places you stamped in a neighborhood, over places in it. Only live, in-geofence stamps count (App Review
  stamps and removed stamps don't). **A set is at least 3 places**: finishing a neighborhood of 1 or 2 places is not an achievement.
  **A completion is earned once and never taken back** (`neighborhood_completion`), even if the neighborhood gains places later
  or you remove a stamp. Progress is computed in the stamp's own transaction so the marker and the stamp commit together.
- **The importer now takes any place worth a stamp**: bars, pubs, nightclubs, restaurants, cafes, theatres, cinemas, arts centres,
  libraries, parks, gardens, playgrounds, museums, galleries, attractions, viewpoints, bookshops. The default area is Manhattan from
  the Battery to 59th St (`--bbox S,W,N,E` for more). With neighborhoods loaded, a place is kept only if it is inside NYC and is
  filed under its neighborhood; without them it falls back to the old East Village and Lower East Side outlines.
- **Page and slot**: the oldest live stamp is page 1 slot 1, three slots to a page. Removing a stamp closes the gap behind it
  (later stamps move up); the oldest never move. One function (`src/lib/layout.ts`), mirrored in the app, same test vectors.
- **Multi-photo**: all of a stamp's photos live in `stamp_photo`; `photo.storage_key` stays as the first photo at creation for
  immutable audit history. Live stamp_photo rows drive display and content constraints. The in-app camera rule is a client rule: the server cannot tell a camera from a
  library, it only strips and limits.
- **Pins** are their own table, owner only, up to 4, unpinning deletes the row (a layout choice, not content).
- **Social off**: with `SOCIAL_ENABLED` off the privacy functions themselves treat every viewer but the owner as a stranger
  (staff too), so even a forgotten route check cannot leak; social routes 404.

## Codex lane: reviewer fixes

- Personal moderation/admin/social services reject the disabled flag before reading records. Foreign passport services
  return 404, including staff, so legacy records cannot reveal handles through a direct service or web caller.
- Photo sets are creation-only. A forced, immutable creation-transaction marker gates database child inserts; the
  service rejects later append before image processing or storage. Seed parents and children are one transaction.
- Empty-content checks use live stamp_photo rows. Parent locks serialize service edits/removals; deferred constraint
  triggers validate committed content. Photo updates advance the parent row version to invalidate stale repeatable-read snapshots.
- Historical empty live stamps are not assigned invented content or automatically removed. The migration preflight
  stops for explicit review. See OPEN-QUESTIONS.md, Codex lane.
- Summary photos are selected from live rows in layout order, including place, activity and moderation previews.
- Tests and exact source references are in the Codex lane review. No mobile visual or device claim is made here.
- A live song needs a non-whitespace title and artist even through direct SQL; removed historical metadata remains final.


## 2026-10-02 combined Codex continuation

The user authorized finishing both lanes in build-codex and requested all integration work remain uncommitted.
Claude's incoming e639860 is combined with Codex HEAD 42a6c0d. Existing privacy and database fixes are reused; no new
backend migration or reset is necessary. Backend configuration remains project nostos-codex, Postgres 5438 and dev port 3001.
The mobile development API fallback now matches that port. The final combined gate passed 292 backend/407 mobile tests
and the backend production build passed. Bundle headers explicitly distinguish dirty snapshots from committed HEAD.

# Open questions

Decisions Nostos needs from the user (or from the lawyer session). Each has a default already built, so nothing is
blocked. Legal questions are never decided by the builder.

## Product

| # | Question | Default built | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Ratings.** `photo.rating` stays in the table and becomes optional. It is not shown in the UI for v1. Do you want it back, or dropped in a later migration? | Stored when sent, never shown. | `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` |
| 2 | **Follow requests show requesters in the limited view** (handle and badge, no name or photo) when they are private and you do not follow them. Show name and photo? | Limited, per the privacy rules. | `backend/src/server/services/people.ts#listFollowRequests` |
| 3 | **GPS spoofing.** A stamp is only as trustworthy as the phone's reported location. Worth a server-side signal later (velocity checks)? | Geofence on the submitted coordinates only. | `backend/src/server/services/photos.ts` |
| 4 | **DESIGN.md section 8 was not in the build prompt** ("DESIGN.md: see section 8"). DESIGN.md was written from the prompt's other sections, with an empty "Chosen reference". Fill that section to enable phase 5. | Phase 5 skipped until filled. | `DESIGN.md` |
| 5 | **Source repo location.** `~/Downloads/lastcall-feature-social` did not exist. The fork was made from `/Users/charlesjinn/lastcall` (branch main, clean, has backend/, mobile/, HANDOFF.md). Confirm that is the right source. | Forked from `/Users/charlesjinn/lastcall`. | n/a |
| 6 | **Reputation.** The accuracy score and consensus are removed, so nothing recomputes reputation. The column stays (default 0.5), visible only to the user and moderators. Keep it for moderation use or drop it? | Kept, moderator-only, no job. | `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` |
| 7 | **The place catalog.** `pnpm venues:import` still imports only bars, pubs and nightclubs from OpenStreetMap in the East Village and Lower East Side. Nostos is about any place in NYC (parks, cafes, landmarks, shops). Which categories and which area should the real catalog cover, and who may add a place? The seed includes six landmarks by hand. | Bars only through the importer; manual or seeded places otherwise. | `backend/src/lib/osm.ts` |
| 8 | **Stamp frequency.** One stamp per person per place per 15 minutes, 20 an hour. For a passport you may want "once per place per day", or repeat visits shown differently. | The old 15 minute cooldown. | `backend/src/server/services/geofence.ts` |

## Legal (for the lawyer session; nothing here is decided by the builder)

| # | Question | Default built |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | **Song metadata licensing.** Stamps store song title, artist and album art URL from Apple's public iTunes Search API, proxied through our backend, no audio playback. Are the iTunes Search API terms and artwork usage acceptable for an app that stores and displays this metadata? Is attribution required? | Metadata only, proxied, no user identifiers sent, short timeout and rate limit. |
| L2 | **Age gate.** A birthdate check blocks under-21s at signup and guest browsing and stores nothing about them. What exactly may and must we do (storing a refusal flag to stop retries, COPPA and state rules, wording)? | Nothing stored about a refused attempt, so a retry is possible. |
| L3 | **NY SAFE for Kids Act.** The feed is strictly chronological from followed accounts, no ranking, no personalization, to stay outside "addictive feed". Is that sufficient? | Chronological feed only. |
| L4 | **Geolocation data.** Coordinates are used for the geofence check and stripped from uploads; stamp coordinates are not shown to viewers. What disclosures does the privacy policy need? | Policy text carried over from Last Call and needs a rewrite for Nostos. |
| L2b | **Age gate retries.** Nothing is stored about someone who fails the 21+ check, so they can try again with a different birthdate. Is that acceptable, or must we remember a refusal (for example a flag on the device)? The app stores nothing and never sends an under-21 birthdate to the server. | No memory of a refusal. |
| L5 | **Sentry and privacy labels.** Error reporting is built but off (needs `EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1` and a DSN at build time). If you turn it on, does the App Store privacy label need "Diagnostics", is a data processing agreement needed, and where should the Sentry project live (EU or US)? Events carry no user, request or breadcrumbs. | Off. No DSN in the repo. |

| L6 | **What the stamp screen says about your position.** The server saves the phone's coordinates with each stamp (columns `lat`, `lng` on the `photo` table) as the record of the geofence check; they are never in any payload. The screen says: "When you stamp, our server checks where your phone is. That position is saved with the stamp and is never shown in the app." Is that wording enough, how long may the coordinates be kept, and should they be dropped after the check instead? (Related to L4. The nearby-places and place-page endpoints do not store the position.) | Saved with the stamp, never shown. Text in `mobile/src/features/stamps/stamp-flow.ts` (`HONESTY`). |
| L7 | **The privacy policy and terms still describe the social layer** (private and public accounts, follow requests, a feed, likes, blocking). Nostos is strictly personal for this version: with `SOCIAL_ENABLED` off none of that exists and only you can see your passport. The pages were only recolored to black and brass this session; the text is unchanged because it is a legal text. It needs a rewrite for personal mode, and the "Who can see what" section should say only you. | Text unchanged. Pages: `backend/src/app/privacy/page.tsx`, `terms/page.tsx`. |

## Codex lane

- If a data-bearing environment contains existing empty live stamps or invalid song metadata, who should review their disposition before the
  live-content migration is deployed? The migration stops without inventing content or changing those records
  (`backend/prisma/migrations/20261003140000_live_stamp_content/migration.sql:42`). The isolated Codex databases migrated
  successfully; this is not a current implementation blocker. No legal decision is made here.

- Combined working-tree fixes and merge remain uncommitted by explicit user request. A physical iPhone/Expo Go run is still required for native interaction and visual verification; no device evidence is available in this session.

== API.md
====================================================================================================

# Nostos API

The single source of truth for the iOS app. Every response shape below is a zod schema in
[`src/contracts/api.ts`](src/contracts/api.ts). Copy that file verbatim into the app, and parse responses with it.
It depends only on **zod v3**, so install `zod@^3.25` in the app, not v4, whose API differs.
`tests/integration/contracts.test.ts` calls every route and fails if a response drifts from those schemas, or if a
route exists without one.

A **stamp** is one visit to a **place**: a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix (at least one). Your **passport** is
your profile, a collection of stamps. (In the database a stamp is a row in `photo` and a place is a row in `venue`; the
API and the app say stamp and place.)

## Personal mode (default)

With `SOCIAL_ENABLED=false`, only the owner can read a passport, its stamps or its visited places. Foreign and missing
stamp mutation IDs return the same 404 status, body and headers. Feed, follows, likes, activity, blocks, people search,
moderation and admin tools return 404, including service and web callers. Existing social and moderation records stay stored.
The social behavior documented below applies only when the flag is explicitly enabled.

Photos are attached only when creating a stamp. `POST /api/stamps/:id/photos` rejects later additions with 400 for the
owner and 404 for foreign/missing stamps. Caption/song edits and final photo removal must leave actual live content;
the original immutable storage key does not count. Layout edits remain supported.

## Conventions

| | |
|---|---|
| Base URL | `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL`: local LAN URL in development, the Vercel preview URL for TestFlight, production for release |
| Format | JSON in and out, except stamp and avatar uploads (`multipart/form-data`) |
| Time | ISO-8601 UTC strings |
| Errors | `ErrorResponse` = `{ error: string, message: string }`. `message` is a human sentence written to be shown inline, as is |
| Paging | Lists return `nextBefore`, an opaque cursor. Pass it as `?before=` for the next page. `null` = the end |

### Authentication (iOS)

1. `POST /api/auth/signup` or `/api/auth/login` with header **`X-Client: ios`** returns `{ token, expiresAt, user }`.
2. Store `token` in the iOS keychain (`expo-secure-store`). It lasts 90 days (fixed; not extended by use).
3. Send `Authorization: Bearer <token>` on every request.
4. If any response is **401 with `error: "invalid_token"`** (expired, revoked, signed out elsewhere, or the account was
   deleted or suspended), clear the token and return to sign-in. Public endpoints also return this 401 when a dead token is
   sent, so the app never silently shows signed-out data.

Bearer requests are exempt from the web's Origin/CSRF check. An iOS token doesn't work as a web cookie, and a web cookie doesn't work as a bearer token.

### Rules the app must not re-implement

The server decides. Show its `message` on failure:

- **Geofence**: a stamp needs the phone within the place's `geofenceMeters` (default **100 m**). Send current `lat`/`lng` with
  every stamp. Outside it you get 403 `forbidden`: "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)".
- **Cooldown**: one stamp per place every **15 minutes** (429 `rate_limited`), and **20 stamps an hour** across places.
- **Privacy**: a stamp or place of a private account is only returned to people with full access (see below). A stamp you
  may not see is a 404, never a 403, so its existence isn't revealed.
- **Feed**: chronological, newest first, from people you follow. No ranking, no personalization, no suggestions.
- **Objectionable text** returns 400 `objectionable_content` ("Your caption contains language we don't allow...").

### Who can see a person's stamps and places ("full access")

The author; staff (moderators and admins); anyone, when the account is public; an approved follower, when it is private.
**A pending follow request is not access.** A blocked pair (either direction) sees nothing of each other, staff excepted.
Accounts are private by default. Going public changes what strangers can see: **every stamp and place** becomes visible
to anyone, signed out included. The app must confirm that before switching.

`GET /api/users/:handle` returns `access: "limited"` (handle and badge only) or `access: "full"`. The stamp and place
lists return **403 `private_account`** without full access, and 404 when blocked.

### Attribution (ODbL)

Places imported from OpenStreetMap must be credited wherever they're shown. When `GET /api/places` returns a non-null
`attribution`, show `attribution.text` ("© OpenStreetMap contributors") on the map, linking to `attribution.url`.

### Photos and songs

Photo URLs are short-lived signed URLs (15-20 minutes): refetch rather than caching them long-term. EXIF and GPS are
stripped from every upload. A song is **metadata only** (title, artist, art URL); there is no audio. Song search is
proxied through the backend to Apple's public iTunes Search API with no user identifiers sent.

## Endpoints

### Auth and account

| Method and path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/auth/signup` | `SignupRequest` | `TokenResponse` (with `X-Client: ios`), else cookie + `WebAuthResponse` |
| `POST /api/auth/login` | `LoginRequest` | same |
| `POST /api/auth/logout`, `/logout-all` | none | `OkResponse`, `LogoutAllResponse` |
| `GET /api/me` | | `MeResponse`: identity, role, pending follow requests, terms status |
| `POST /api/me/terms` | `AcceptTermsRequest` | `AcceptTermsResponse` |
| `POST /api/me/delete` | `DeleteAccountRequest` | `DeleteAccountResponse`: anonymizes the account, hides its stamps, removes the files |
| `GET`, `PATCH /api/me/profile` | `ProfileSettingsRequest` | `ProfileSettingsResponse` |
| `POST`, `DELETE /api/me/avatar` | multipart `photo` | `AvatarResponse` |
| `GET /api/me/neighborhoods` | | `NeighborhoodIndexResponse`: every neighborhood with places and your visited/total, a completion marker once a set (3+ places) is finished. Owner only |
| `GET`, `PUT /api/me/pins` | `PinsRequest` `{ stampIds }` (up to 4 of your own) | `PinsResponse`: pinned stamps, owner only |
| `GET /api/me/activity` | | `ActivityResponse`: follow requests, new followers, likes on your stamps |
| `GET /api/me/following` | | `FollowingResponse` |
| `GET /api/me/blocks` | | `BlocksResponse` |

### Places

| Method and path | Response |
|---|---|
| `GET /api/places` | `PlacesResponse`: name, location, geofence. No live data |
| `GET /api/places/:id` | `PlaceResponse` (id or slug) |
| `GET /api/places/nearby?lat=&lng=` | `NearbyPlacesResponse`: inside or near the geofence, nearest first. The position is never stored |
| `GET /api/places/:id/mine?lat=&lng=` | `MyPlacePageResponse`: your own stamps there (stacked by night, oldest first) and `inside`, whether the position is within the geofence now (null without one). The position is not stored |
| `POST /api/places/:id/stamps` | `CreateStampResponse` (201): `{ stamp, progress }`, where `progress` is your standing in the place's neighborhood (null if it has none). multipart: optional `photos` (up to 6, repeated), `caption`, `songTitle`, `songArtist`, `songArtUrl`, `songSourceId`, `lat`, `lng`, optional `rating` |

### Stamps, feed and passports

| Method and path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/stamps/:id` | | `StampResponse` (404 unless visible) |
| `PATCH /api/stamps/:id` | `StampEditRequest`: caption and song only; frozen while a flag is open | `StampResponse` |
| `POST /api/stamps/:id/photos` | multipart `photos` (repeated) | Creation-only: returns 400 for the owner; foreign/missing stamps return the same 404 |
| `PATCH /api/stamps/:id/photos` | `PhotoLayoutRequest`: `{ photos: [{ id, position?, x?, y?, rotation? }] }` | `StampResponse`: the scrapbook layout and order, owner only |
| `POST /api/stamps/:id/photos/:photoId/remove` | none | `StampResponse`: hides one photo for good, never deletes |
| `POST /api/stamps/:id/remove` | none | `RemoveStampResponse`: "delete from profile", final, never a hard delete |
| `PUT`, `DELETE /api/stamps/:id/like` | none | `LikeResponse`: one per person, not your own, not on hidden or removed stamps |
| `GET /api/feed?before=` | | `FeedResponse`: stamps from people you follow, newest first, 30 per page |
| `GET /api/users/:handle/stamps?before=` | | `UserStampsResponse` (403 `private_account`) |
| `GET /api/users/:handle/places` | | `UserPlacesResponse`: distinct places with stamp count and latest stamp |
| `GET /api/songs/search?q=` | | `SongSearchResponse`: signed in, rate limited (120 an hour) |

Photos can only be attached in the stamp creation request, never added later. A stamp holds up to 6 photos (`photos`, in page order, each with an optional scrapbook `x`, `y` as 0..1 of the page and `rotation` in degrees; `photoUrl` is the first). Photos are re-encoded, so no EXIF or GPS survives, and they are hidden, never deleted.

A `StampView` carries the author (always a full profile view), the place, the song, likes and `mine`. It never carries
coordinates, storage keys or user ids. `hidden` and `removed` are true only for staff.

### People

| Method and path | Response |
|---|---|
| `GET /api/users?q=` | `UserSearchResponse` (handle search; display names only where visible) |
| `GET /api/users/:handle` | `ProfileResponse` (counts: stamps, places, followers, following) |
| `PUT`, `DELETE /api/users/:handle/follow` | `FollowResponse`: public account -> `following`, private -> `requested` |
| `POST`, `DELETE /api/users/:handle/block` | `BlockResponse` |
| `GET /api/users/:handle/followers`, `/following` | `PeopleListResponse` (403 `private_account`) |
| `GET /api/me/follow-requests`, `POST /api/me/follow-requests/:handle`, `POST .../approve-all` | requests, decisions |
| `DELETE /api/me/followers/:handle` | `RemoveFollowerResponse` |

### Reports and moderation

| Method and path | Notes |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/flags` | `FlagRequest`: `targetType` `STAMP` or `USER`. You can only report a stamp you can see |
| `GET /api/mod/queue`, `/hidden`, `/log` | moderators only |
| `POST /api/mod/actions` | `HIDE`, `DISMISS`, `RESTORE` (stamps); `SUSPEND`, `UNSUSPEND`, `DISMISS` (accounts) |

Three flags from three eligible people (a regular account, 7+ days old, reputation 0.5+) auto-hide a stamp pending
review. Hidden stamps are never deleted, and every action is in the append-only moderation log.

### Web only

`PATCH /api/admin/users/:handle` (geofence-exempt flag), `GET /api/admin/users`, `POST /api/admin/users/:handle/verification`,
`GET /api/admin/verification-log`, `GET|POST /api/cron/maintenance` (daily housekeeping, `Bearer $CRON_SECRET`),
`GET /api/health`, `GET /api/uploads/*` (signed media URLs).


====================================================================================================
== DEPLOY.md
====================================================================================================

# Deploying Nostos

**Status: not deployed.** There is no git remote, no Vercel project, no Neon database and no R2 bucket for Nostos, and
nothing here creates one for you. This is the plan, written so each step is a command or a value to paste. Legal and
policy questions are in `../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.

| Environment | App | Database | Photos | Data |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| development | `pnpm dev` | Docker Postgres :5435 | local disk (`uploads/`) | demo seed |
| preview | Vercel, `preview` branch | Neon branch `preview` | R2 `nostos-photos-preview` | demo seed + `@appreview` |
| production | Vercel, `main` branch | Neon branch `main` | R2 `nostos-photos-prod` | imported places, no fake data |

## 1. Accounts and resources

Vercel (the **Root Directory is `backend/`**), Neon (Postgres 16; a pooled URL for `DATABASE_URL` with
`&pgbouncer=true&connect_timeout=15`, a direct URL for `DIRECT_URL`), and Cloudflare R2 (two private buckets, one
read/write API token scoped to them).

## 2. Environment variables (Vercel)

| Variable | Value |
|---|---|
| `DATABASE_URL`, `DIRECT_URL` | Neon pooled and direct URLs for that branch |
| `APP_ENV` | `production` or `preview` |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER` | `r2` |
| `R2_ACCOUNT_ID`, `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `R2_BUCKET` | from Cloudflare |
| `CRON_SECRET` | random, 32+ characters (`openssl rand -base64 32`) |
| `UPLOAD_URL_SECRET` | random, 32+ characters |

Turn off Vercel Authentication for the preview branch, or the app gets a login wall instead of the API.

## 3. Deploy

Every build runs `prisma migrate deploy` over `DIRECT_URL` before `next build` (`pnpm vercel-build`). The one cron
(`vercel.json`) runs `/api/cron/maintenance` daily at 09:00 UTC: it prunes day-old login attempts and rate-limit rows. It
needs only the Hobby plan. Smoke test: `curl -s https://<host>/api/health` returns `{"ok":true,"db":"up",...}`.

## 4. Production data

No fake data. Import real places from OpenStreetMap (idempotent; re-run any time):

```bash
DATABASE_URL='<pooled>' DIRECT_URL='<direct>' APP_ENV=production pnpm venues:import --dry-run
DATABASE_URL='<pooled>' DIRECT_URL='<direct>' APP_ENV=production pnpm venues:import
```

The demo seed refuses to run against production. The first admin signs up normally, then:
`DATABASE_URL='<pooled>' DIRECT_URL='<direct>' pnpm user:role you@example.com ADMIN`.

## 5. Preview data and the App Review account

```bash
DATABASE_URL='<preview pooled>' DIRECT_URL='<preview direct>' APP_ENV=preview STORAGE_DRIVER=r2 R2_ACCOUNT_ID='<id>' \
R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID='<key>' R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY='<secret>' R2_BUCKET=nostos-photos-preview \
DEV_SEED_PASSWORD='<16+ chars>' APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD='<12+ chars>' pnpm db:seed
```

This creates places, 17 demo accounts and the geofence-exempt `appreview@lastcall.dev`. The review account can stamp from
anywhere, and only it can see those stamps. Re-run before a review submission so the feed looks recent.

## 6. Point the app at it

`../mobile/eas.json` sets `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL` for the `preview` and `production` profiles (placeholders:
`https://preview.nostos.app` and `https://nostos.app`). App Store Connect needs `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/support` and
`/delete-account` on that host. The policy pages are drafts from Last Call and need a rewrite and a lawyer.

## 7. Ongoing

Commit migrations (forward only, never `migrate reset` outside development). Neon keeps point-in-time restore; raise the
retention before launch. Rotate `CRON_SECRET` or the R2 keys in Vercel and redeploy.


====================================================================================================
== src/contracts/api.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Nostos HTTP API contract: the single source of truth for clients (the Expo iOS app copies this file
 * verbatim into src/api/contracts.ts). Depends only on `zod` **v3** (written against 3.25; install `zod@^3.25`,
 * not v4). Every response schema is `.strict()`, and
 * tests/integration/contracts.test.ts calls every route and fails if a response drifts from these schemas.
 * Human-readable reference: API.md.
 *
 * Conventions: JSON bodies; timestamps are ISO-8601 UTC strings. Errors are `{ error: <code>, message: <human sentence> }`.
 * A stamp is one visit to a place: a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix. Nothing here is ever "Anonymous":
 * a stamp is only returned to people who may see its author's full profile.
 */
import { z } from "zod";

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Primitives and enums
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const IsoDate = z.string().datetime({ offset: true });
export const Role = z.enum(["USER", "MODERATOR", "ADMIN"]);
/** What can be reported or moderated. The database calls a stamp "PHOTO"; the API says STAMP. */
export const TargetType = z.enum(["STAMP", "USER"]);
/** Why something is reported. Moderators read UNDERAGE and STALKING first. */
export const FlagCategory = z.enum(["SPAM", "HARASSMENT", "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT", "NOT_AT_PLACE", "STALKING", "UNDERAGE", "PRIVATE_INFO", "OTHER"]);
export const ModerationAction = z.enum(["FLAGGED", "AUTO_HIDE", "HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "ACCOUNT_DELETED", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]);

export const ErrorResponse = z
  .object({
    /** Machine-readable code, e.g. "unauthorized", "invalid_token", "forbidden", "rate_limited", "objectionable_content", "private_account". */
    error: z.string(),
    /** Human sentence, safe to show inline (e.g. "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)"). */
    message: z.string(),
  })
  .strict();

/** ODbL attribution; show wherever OpenStreetMap-sourced place data appears. */
export const Attribution = z.object({ text: z.literal("© OpenStreetMap contributors"), url: z.string().url(), license: z.literal("ODbL-1.0") }).strict();

export const PublicUser = z.object({ id: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role }).strict();

/** "requested" = a pending request to a private account. */
export const FollowState = z.enum(["none", "requested", "following"]);
/**
 * A person in a list (search, followers, following, requests). The handle and verified badge always; displayName
 * and avatarUrl are null when the account is private and the viewer doesn't follow it (show a generic avatar).
 */
export const PersonView = z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string().nullable(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** A person plus the viewer's follow state toward them (drives Follow / Requested / Following buttons). */
export const PersonRow = PersonView.extend({ follow: FollowState, isSelf: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const PlaceRef = z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth and account
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const SignupRequest = z
  .object({
    email: z.string().trim().email().max(200),
    password: z.string().min(10).max(200),
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60),
    handle: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().regex(/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/),
    acceptTerms: z.literal(true),
    /** YYYY-MM-DD. Checked once for 21+ (403 `underage` if not) and never stored. */
    birthdate: z.string().refine((v) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v), "Enter your birthdate as YYYY-MM-DD"),
  })
  .strict();
export const LoginRequest = z.object({ email: z.string().trim().email().max(200), password: z.string().max(200) }).strict();
/** POST /api/auth/login and /api/auth/signup with header `X-Client: ios`. */
export const TokenResponse = z.object({ token: z.string(), expiresAt: IsoDate, user: PublicUser }).strict();
/** Same endpoints without X-Client (web): the session is set as an httpOnly cookie. */
export const WebAuthResponse = z.object({ user: PublicUser }).strict();
export const OkResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }).strict();
export const LogoutAllResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true), revoked: z.number().int() }).strict();

export const MeResponse = z
  .object({
    user: z
      .object({
        id: z.string(), email: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role, memberSince: IsoDate, reputation: z.number(),
        isPrivate: z.boolean(), bio: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean(),
      })
      .strict(),
    /** Badge count for the Follow requests entry. */
    social: z.object({ pendingFollowRequests: z.number().int() }).strict(),
    /** geofenceExempt: App Review / QA account that can stamp anywhere; its stamps are visible only to itself. */
    settings: z.object({ geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    terms: z
      .object({ acceptedVersion: z.string().nullable(), acceptedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), currentVersion: z.string(), needsAcceptance: z.boolean() })
      .strict(),
  })
  .strict();
export const AcceptTermsRequest = z.object({ version: z.string() }).strict();
export const AcceptTermsResponse = z.object({ acceptedVersion: z.string(), acceptedAt: IsoDate }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountRequest = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1).max(200) }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountResponse = z.object({ deleted: z.literal(true) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Places
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** A place: name, location and geofence. No live data. */
export const Place = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(),
    /** May be "" for imported places without a mapped street address; show the neighborhood instead. */
    address: z.string(),
    neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number(),
    /** Radius in meters within which a phone may stamp this place (the server checks it again on every stamp). */
    geofenceMeters: z.number().int(),
    /** The NYC neighborhood the place is in, when known. */
    neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable(),
    fromOsm: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const PlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place), attribution: Attribution.nullable() }).strict();
export const PlaceResponse = z.object({ place: Place }).strict();
/** GET /api/places/nearby?lat=&lng=: nearest first. `inside` = within its geofence right now. The position is never stored. */
export const NearbyPlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place.extend({ distanceM: z.number().int(), inside: z.boolean() }).strict()) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stamps
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Like count (App Review likes excluded) and whether you liked it. */
export const LikeSummary = z.object({ count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Album art only from Apple's artwork CDN, over https (the server enforces the same rule). */
export const AppleArtUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(500)
  .refine((u) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(u);
      return url.protocol === "https:" && /(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(url.hostname);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "Song art must be an https link from Apple's artwork servers");
/** Song metadata only (no audio). */
export const Song = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
/**
 * A stamp. Photo URLs are short-lived signed URLs (15-20 min): refetch rather than caching URLs. There are no
 * coordinates, storage keys or user ids anywhere in it. `hidden` / `removed` are true only for staff viewers.
 */
/** One photo on a stamp. x and y are 0..1 of the page and rotation is degrees: null until the owner lays them out. */
export const StampPhotoView = z
  .object({ id: z.string(), url: z.string(), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5), x: z.number().nullable(), y: z.number().nullable(), rotation: z.number().nullable() })
  .strict();
export const StampView = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(),
    /** The first photo, or null. */
    photoUrl: z.string().nullable(),
    /** Up to 6 photos in page order. */
    photos: z.array(StampPhotoView).max(6),
    caption: z.string(),
    song: Song.nullable(),
    /** Page and slot in the owner's passport, oldest first, three slots to a page (src/lib/layout.ts). null if hidden or removed. */
    page: z.number().int().min(1).nullable(),
    slot: z.number().int().min(1).max(3).nullable(),
    createdAt: IsoDate,
    editedAt: IsoDate.nullable(),
    place: PlaceRef,
    author: z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    mine: z.boolean(),
    likes: LikeSummary,
    hidden: z.boolean(),
    removed: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const StampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView }).strict();
/**
 * POST /api/places/:id/stamps (multipart/form-data): optional `photo` file, `caption`, `songTitle`, `songArtist`,
 * `songArtUrl`, `songSourceId`, `lat`, `lng` (and an optional `rating` 1-10 that v1 doesn't show). At least one of
 * photo, caption or song. 403 outside the geofence, with a message that says how far away you are.
 */
/** Progress through a neighborhood: visited places over total, and whether this stamp finished the set (a set is at least 3 places). */
export const NeighborhoodProgress = z
  .object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), justCompleted: z.boolean(), newPlace: z.boolean() })
  .strict();
/** `progress` is null when the place has no neighborhood (or the stamp is an App Review stamp). */
export const CreateStampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView, progress: NeighborhoodProgress.nullable() }).strict();
/** GET /api/me/neighborhoods: the passport's index. Started first. Owner only. */
export const NeighborhoodIndexResponse = z
  .object({
    neighborhoods: z.array(z.object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), completedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), percent: z.number().int() }).strict()),
    totals: z.object({ neighborhoodsStarted: z.number().int(), neighborhoodsComplete: z.number().int() }).strict(),
  })
  .strict();
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id: caption and song; send only changed fields. `song: null` removes the song. */
export const StampEditRequest = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(280),
    song: z.object({ title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artist: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artUrl: AppleArtUrl.nullish(), sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).nullish() }).strict().nullable(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id/photos: the scrapbook layout. Owner only. POST rejects later additions (400 for the owner, 404 for foreign/missing stamps); POST .../photos/:photoId/remove removes one. */
export const PhotoLayoutRequest = z
  .object({
    photos: z
      .array(z.object({ id: z.string().min(1).max(64), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5).optional(), x: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), y: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), rotation: z.number().min(-45).max(45).nullable().optional() }).strict())
      .min(1)
      .max(6),
  })
  .strict();
/** GET and PUT /api/me/pins: your pinned stamps (up to 4), owner only. PUT body is `{ stampIds }`. */
export const PinsRequest = z.object({ stampIds: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(64)).max(4) }).strict();
export const PinsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView).max(4) }).strict();
export const RemoveStampResponse = z.object({ removed: z.literal(true) }).strict();
/** PUT/DELETE /api/stamps/:id/like. */
export const LikeResponse = z.object({ liked: z.boolean(), count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Feed and passport grid, newest first. `nextBefore` = an opaque cursor: pass it as ?before= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const StampsPageResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextBefore: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const FeedResponse = StampsPageResponse;
/** The passport, OLDEST first so page 1 stays page 1. `nextAfter` = pass it as ?after= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const UserStampsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextAfter: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const UserPlacesResponse = z
  .object({
    places: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          place: z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number() }).strict(),
          stamps: z.number().int(),
          latestStamp: z.object({ id: z.string(), createdAt: IsoDate, photoUrl: z.string().nullable(), caption: z.string() }).strict(),
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();

/** GET /api/songs/search?q= : metadata from Apple's iTunes Search API, proxied. */
export const SongResult = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), album: z.string().nullable(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string() }).strict();
export const SongSearchResponse = z.object({ songs: z.array(SongResult) }).strict();

/** GET /api/places/:id/mine?lat=&lng=: your own page for a place. `visits` stack your stamps by night; `inside` is null without a position. */
export const MyPlacePageResponse = z
  .object({
    place: Place.omit({ fromOsm: true }).extend({ fromOsm: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    stamps: z.array(StampView),
    visits: z.array(z.object({ day: z.string(), stamps: z.array(StampView) }).strict()),
    inside: z.boolean().nullable(),
    distanceM: z.number().int().nullable(),
  })
  .strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// People
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const UserSearchResponse = z.object({ users: z.array(PersonRow) }).strict();
export const Relationship = z
  .object({
    isSelf: z.boolean(),
    /** The viewer's follow state toward this account. */
    follow: FollowState,
    followsYou: z.boolean(),
    mutual: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
/**
 * `access: "limited"`: a private account the viewer doesn't follow. Show the handle, the badge, a generic avatar,
 * "This account is private" and Follow (Request) / Requested. Everything else, including every stamp and place, is
 * only in `access: "full"`.
 */
export const ProfileResponse = z.discriminatedUnion("access", [
  z
    .object({
      access: z.literal("full"),
      user: z
        .object({
          handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), bio: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), isPrivate: z.boolean(),
          memberSince: IsoDate,
          /** Private: present for yourself and for moderators, otherwise null. */
          reputation: z.number().nullable(),
          counts: z.object({ stamps: z.number().int(), places: z.number().int(), followers: z.number().int(), following: z.number().int() }).strict(),
        })
        .strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
  z
    .object({
      access: z.literal("limited"),
      user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), isPrivate: z.literal(true) }).strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
]);
export const FollowingResponse = z
  .object({ following: z.array(z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean(), mutual: z.boolean() }).strict()) })
  .strict();
/** PUT follow: public account -> "following"; private account -> "requested". DELETE (unfollow or cancel) -> "none". */
export const FollowResponse = z.object({ state: FollowState }).strict();
const StampRef = z.object({ id: z.string(), url: z.string().nullable(), place: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict() }).strict();
/** GET /api/me/activity: newest first, last 30 days (pending requests of any age). In-app only. */
export const ActivityResponse = z
  .object({
    items: z.array(
      z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("follow_request"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView }).strict(),
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("new_follower"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView }).strict(),
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("like"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView, stamp: StampRef }).strict(),
      ]),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
/** GET /api/users/:handle/followers and /following (403 `private_account` without full access). */
export const PeopleListResponse = z.object({ people: z.array(PersonRow) }).strict();
export const FollowRequestsResponse = z.object({ requests: z.array(z.object({ person: PersonView, requestedAt: IsoDate }).strict()) }).strict();
export const FollowRequestDecisionRequest = z.object({ approve: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const FollowRequestDecisionResponse = z.object({ approved: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const ApproveAllResponse = z.object({ approved: z.number().int() }).strict();
export const RemoveFollowerResponse = z.object({ removed: z.literal(true) }).strict();
/** PATCH /api/me/profile: send only changed fields. */
export const ProfileSettingsRequest = z
  .object({ displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60), bio: z.string().trim().max(150), isPrivate: z.boolean() })
  .partial()
  .strict();
export const ProfileSettingsResponse = z
  .object({
    profile: z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), bio: z.string(), isPrivate: z.boolean(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    /** After switching to public, offer "Approve N pending requests?" when this is above 0. */
    pendingRequests: z.number().int(),
  })
  .strict();
/** POST /api/me/avatar (multipart `photo`) and DELETE /api/me/avatar. */
export const AvatarResponse = z.object({ avatarUrl: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const BlockResponse = z.object({ blocked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Your blocks, newest first. Unblock with DELETE /api/users/:handle/block. */
export const BlocksResponse = z.object({ blocks: z.array(z.object({ handle: z.string(), blockedAt: IsoDate }).strict()) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Flags and moderation

/** targetId: the stamp id; for USER, the account id or handle. */
export const FlagRequest = z.object({ targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64), reason: z.string().trim().min(3).max(300), category: FlagCategory.default("OTHER") }).strict();
export const FlagResponse = z.object({ flag: z.object({ id: z.string() }).strict(), autoHidden: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const ModActionRequest = z
  .object({ targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64), action: z.enum(["HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]), note: z.string().trim().max(300).optional() })
  .strict();
export const ModActionResponse = z.object({ target: z.object({ id: z.string(), hidden: z.boolean() }).strict().nullable() }).strict();
export const ModQueueItem = z
  .object({
    targetType: TargetType,
    targetId: z.string(),
    /** The place of a stamp; null for USER (account) reports. */
    venue: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict().nullable(),
    content: z
      .object({
        kind: TargetType,
        preview: z.string(),
        /** Signed URL for photos (moderators can see hidden ones). */
        url: z.string().optional(),
        author: z.string(),
        authorRep: z.number(),
        /** Hidden (stamp) or suspended (account) time. */
        hiddenAt: IsoDate.nullable(),
        createdAt: IsoDate,
      })
      .strict()
      .nullable(),
    flags: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), reason: z.string(), category: FlagCategory, createdAt: IsoDate, by: z.string(), byRole: Role }).strict()),
    history: z.array(z.object({ action: ModerationAction, by: z.string(), note: z.string().nullable(), at: IsoDate }).strict()),
  })
  .strict();
export const ModQueueResponse = z.object({ items: z.array(ModQueueItem) }).strict();
/** Hidden stamps and suspended accounts (newest first) for the Restore / Unsuspend screen. */
export const ModHiddenResponse = z
  .object({
    items: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string(), venue: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict().nullable(),
          preview: z.string(), url: z.string().optional(), author: z.string(), hiddenAt: IsoDate,
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
export const ModLogResponse = z
  .object({
    events: z.array(
      z.object({ id: z.string(), action: ModerationAction, targetType: z.enum(["PHOTO", "COMMENT", "REPORT", "USER"]), targetId: z.string(), by: z.string(), note: z.string().nullable(), createdAt: IsoDate }).strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();

export const AdminUserFlagsResponse = z.object({ user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict() }).strict();
export const AdminUserSearchResponse = z
  .object({
    users: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), email: z.string(), role: Role, isPrivate: z.boolean(),
          verified: z.boolean(), verifiedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), suspended: z.boolean(), memberSince: IsoDate,
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
/** POST /api/admin/users/:handle/verification. The note is required and goes into the append-only log. */
export const VerificationRequest = z.object({ verified: z.boolean(), note: z.string().trim().min(3).max(300) }).strict();
export const VerificationResponse = z.object({ user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), verifiedAt: IsoDate.nullable() }).strict() }).strict();
export const VerificationLogResponse = z
  .object({ events: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), handle: z.string(), action: z.enum(["GRANT", "REVOKE"]), note: z.string(), by: z.string(), at: IsoDate }).strict()) })
  .strict();

  .strict();

== ../mobile/src/api/contracts.ts

/**
 * COPIED VERBATIM from backend/src/contracts/api.ts (this repo, commit a8fd412).
 * Do not edit here: change the backend contract, then re-copy with `npm run sync:contracts`.
 */
/**
 * Nostos HTTP API contract: the single source of truth for clients (the Expo iOS app copies this file
 * verbatim into src/api/contracts.ts). Depends only on `zod` **v3** (written against 3.25; install `zod@^3.25`,
 * not v4). Every response schema is `.strict()`, and
 * tests/integration/contracts.test.ts calls every route and fails if a response drifts from these schemas.
 * Human-readable reference: API.md.
 *
 * Conventions: JSON bodies; timestamps are ISO-8601 UTC strings. Errors are `{ error: <code>, message: <human sentence> }`.
 * A stamp is one visit to a place: a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix. Nothing here is ever "Anonymous":
 * a stamp is only returned to people who may see its author's full profile.
 */
import { z } from "zod";

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Primitives and enums
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const IsoDate = z.string().datetime({ offset: true });
export const Role = z.enum(["USER", "MODERATOR", "ADMIN"]);
/** What can be reported or moderated. The database calls a stamp "PHOTO"; the API says STAMP. */
export const TargetType = z.enum(["STAMP", "USER"]);
/** Why something is reported. Moderators read UNDERAGE and STALKING first. */
export const FlagCategory = z.enum(["SPAM", "HARASSMENT", "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT", "NOT_AT_PLACE", "STALKING", "UNDERAGE", "PRIVATE_INFO", "OTHER"]);
export const ModerationAction = z.enum(["FLAGGED", "AUTO_HIDE", "HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "ACCOUNT_DELETED", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]);

export const ErrorResponse = z
  .object({
    /** Machine-readable code, e.g. "unauthorized", "invalid_token", "forbidden", "rate_limited", "objectionable_content", "private_account". */
    error: z.string(),
    /** Human sentence, safe to show inline (e.g. "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)"). */
    message: z.string(),
  })
  .strict();

/** ODbL attribution; show wherever OpenStreetMap-sourced place data appears. */
export const Attribution = z.object({ text: z.literal("© OpenStreetMap contributors"), url: z.string().url(), license: z.literal("ODbL-1.0") }).strict();

export const PublicUser = z.object({ id: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role }).strict();

/** "requested" = a pending request to a private account. */
export const FollowState = z.enum(["none", "requested", "following"]);
/**
 * A person in a list (search, followers, following, requests). The handle and verified badge always; displayName
 * and avatarUrl are null when the account is private and the viewer doesn't follow it (show a generic avatar).
 */
export const PersonView = z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string().nullable(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** A person plus the viewer's follow state toward them (drives Follow / Requested / Following buttons). */
export const PersonRow = PersonView.extend({ follow: FollowState, isSelf: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const PlaceRef = z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth and account
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const SignupRequest = z
  .object({
    email: z.string().trim().email().max(200),
    password: z.string().min(10).max(200),
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60),
    handle: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().regex(/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/),
    acceptTerms: z.literal(true),
    /** YYYY-MM-DD. Checked once for 21+ (403 `underage` if not) and never stored. */
    birthdate: z.string().refine((v) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v), "Enter your birthdate as YYYY-MM-DD"),
  })
  .strict();
export const LoginRequest = z.object({ email: z.string().trim().email().max(200), password: z.string().max(200) }).strict();
/** POST /api/auth/login and /api/auth/signup with header `X-Client: ios`. */
export const TokenResponse = z.object({ token: z.string(), expiresAt: IsoDate, user: PublicUser }).strict();
/** Same endpoints without X-Client (web): the session is set as an httpOnly cookie. */
export const WebAuthResponse = z.object({ user: PublicUser }).strict();
export const OkResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }).strict();
export const LogoutAllResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true), revoked: z.number().int() }).strict();

export const MeResponse = z
  .object({
    user: z
      .object({
        id: z.string(), email: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role, memberSince: IsoDate, reputation: z.number(),
        isPrivate: z.boolean(), bio: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean(),
      })
      .strict(),
    /** Badge count for the Follow requests entry. */
    social: z.object({ pendingFollowRequests: z.number().int() }).strict(),
    /** geofenceExempt: App Review / QA account that can stamp anywhere; its stamps are visible only to itself. */
    settings: z.object({ geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    terms: z
      .object({ acceptedVersion: z.string().nullable(), acceptedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), currentVersion: z.string(), needsAcceptance: z.boolean() })
      .strict(),
  })
  .strict();
export const AcceptTermsRequest = z.object({ version: z.string() }).strict();
export const AcceptTermsResponse = z.object({ acceptedVersion: z.string(), acceptedAt: IsoDate }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountRequest = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1).max(200) }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountResponse = z.object({ deleted: z.literal(true) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Places
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** A place: name, location and geofence. No live data. */
export const Place = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(),
    /** May be "" for imported places without a mapped street address; show the neighborhood instead. */
    address: z.string(),
    neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number(),
    /** Radius in meters within which a phone may stamp this place (the server checks it again on every stamp). */
    geofenceMeters: z.number().int(),
    /** The NYC neighborhood the place is in, when known. */
    neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable(),
    fromOsm: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const PlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place), attribution: Attribution.nullable() }).strict();
export const PlaceResponse = z.object({ place: Place }).strict();
/** GET /api/places/nearby?lat=&lng=: nearest first. `inside` = within its geofence right now. The position is never stored. */
export const NearbyPlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place.extend({ distanceM: z.number().int(), inside: z.boolean() }).strict()) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stamps
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Like count (App Review likes excluded) and whether you liked it. */
export const LikeSummary = z.object({ count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Album art only from Apple's artwork CDN, over https (the server enforces the same rule). */
export const AppleArtUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(500)
  .refine((u) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(u);
      return url.protocol === "https:" && /(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(url.hostname);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "Song art must be an https link from Apple's artwork servers");
/** Song metadata only (no audio). */
export const Song = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
/**
 * A stamp. Photo URLs are short-lived signed URLs (15-20 min): refetch rather than caching URLs. There are no
 * coordinates, storage keys or user ids anywhere in it. `hidden` / `removed` are true only for staff viewers.
 */
/** One photo on a stamp. x and y are 0..1 of the page and rotation is degrees: null until the owner lays them out. */
export const StampPhotoView = z
  .object({ id: z.string(), url: z.string(), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5), x: z.number().nullable(), y: z.number().nullable(), rotation: z.number().nullable() })
  .strict();
export const StampView = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(),
    /** The first photo, or null. */
    photoUrl: z.string().nullable(),
    /** Up to 6 photos in page order. */
    photos: z.array(StampPhotoView).max(6),
    caption: z.string(),
    song: Song.nullable(),
    /** Page and slot in the owner's passport, oldest first, three slots to a page (src/lib/layout.ts). null if hidden or removed. */
    page: z.number().int().min(1).nullable(),
    slot: z.number().int().min(1).max(3).nullable(),
    createdAt: IsoDate,
    editedAt: IsoDate.nullable(),
    place: PlaceRef,
    author: z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    mine: z.boolean(),
    likes: LikeSummary,
    hidden: z.boolean(),
    removed: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const StampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView }).strict();
/**
 * POST /api/places/:id/stamps (multipart/form-data): optional `photo` file, `caption`, `songTitle`, `songArtist`,
 * `songArtUrl`, `songSourceId`, `lat`, `lng` (and an optional `rating` 1-10 that v1 doesn't show). At least one of
 * photo, caption or song. 403 outside the geofence, with a message that says how far away you are.
 */
/** Progress through a neighborhood: visited places over total, and whether this stamp finished the set (a set is at least 3 places). */
export const NeighborhoodProgress = z
  .object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), justCompleted: z.boolean(), newPlace: z.boolean() })
  .strict();
/** `progress` is null when the place has no neighborhood (or the stamp is an App Review stamp). */
export const CreateStampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView, progress: NeighborhoodProgress.nullable() }).strict();
/** GET /api/me/neighborhoods: the passport's index. Started first. Owner only. */
export const NeighborhoodIndexResponse = z
  .object({
    neighborhoods: z.array(z.object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), completedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), percent: z.number().int() }).strict()),
    totals: z.object({ neighborhoodsStarted: z.number().int(), neighborhoodsComplete: z.number().int() }).strict(),
  })
  .strict();
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id: caption and song; send only changed fields. `song: null` removes the song. */
export const StampEditRequest = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(280),
    song: z.object({ title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artist: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artUrl: AppleArtUrl.nullish(), sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).nullish() }).strict().nullable(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id/photos: the scrapbook layout. Owner only. POST rejects later additions (400 for the owner, 404 for foreign/missing stamps); POST .../photos/:photoId/remove removes one. */
export const PhotoLayoutRequest = z
  .object({
    photos: z
      .array(z.object({ id: z.string().min(1).max(64), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5).optional(), x: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), y: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), rotation: z.number().min(-45).max(45).nullable().optional() }).strict())
      .min(1)
      .max(6),
  })
  .strict();
/** GET and PUT /api/me/pins: your pinned stamps (up to 4), owner only. PUT body is `{ stampIds }`. */
export const PinsRequest = z.object({ stampIds: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(64)).max(4) }).strict();
export const PinsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView).max(4) }).strict();
export const RemoveStampResponse = z.object({ removed: z.literal(true) }).strict();
/** PUT/DELETE /api/stamps/:id/like. */
export const LikeResponse = z.object({ liked: z.boolean(), count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Feed and passport grid, newest first. `nextBefore` = an opaque cursor: pass it as ?before= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const StampsPageResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextBefore: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const FeedResponse = StampsPageResponse;
/** The passport, OLDEST first so page 1 stays page 1. `nextAfter` = pass it as ?after= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const UserStampsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextAfter: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const UserPlacesResponse = z
  .object({
    places: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          place: z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number() }).strict(),
          stamps: z.number().int(),
          latestStamp: z.object({ id: z.string(), createdAt: IsoDate, photoUrl: z.string().nullable(), caption: z.string() }).strict(),
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();

/** GET /api/songs/search?q= : metadata from Apple's iTunes Search API, proxied. */
export const SongResult = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), album: z.string().nullable(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string() }).strict();

/** GET /api/places/:id/mine?lat=&lng=: your own page for a place. `visits` stack your stamps by night; `inside` is null without a position. */
export const MyPlacePageResponse = z

        })
        .strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
  z
    .object({
      access: z.literal("limited"),
      user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), isPrivate: z.literal(true) }).strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
]);
export const FollowingResponse = z

/** PUT follow: public account -> "following"; private account -> "requested". DELETE (unfollow or cancel) -> "none". */
export const FollowResponse = z.object({ state: FollowState }).strict();

== ../mobile/src/auth/auth-context.tsx
====================================================================================================

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { api } from "@/api";
import { qk } from "@/api/keys";
import type { TokenResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { tokenStore } from "./token-store";

/**
 * - signedOut: first launch or after sign-out; shows the welcome screen.
 * - guest: browsing the public map without an account (Guideline 5.1.1(v)); posting asks to sign in.
 * - signedIn: has a token.
 */
export type AuthStatus = "loading" | "signedOut" | "guest" | "signedIn";

type AuthContextValue = {
  status: AuthStatus;
  signIn: (res: TokenResponse) => Promise<void>;
  /** Forget the token locally at once, then revoke it on the server (best effort). */
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  /** Forget the token without calling the server (after account deletion). */
  forget: () => Promise<void>;
  browseAsGuest: () => void;
};

const GUEST_KEY = "nostos.guest";

// A one-time message for the welcome screen after the account is deleted.
let farewell: string | null = null;
export const farewellNotice = {
  set: (m: string) => {
    farewell = m;
  },
  /** Read once; later reads return null. */
  take: (): string | null => {
    const m = farewell;
    farewell = null;
    return m;
  },
};
const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [hasToken, setHasToken] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [guest, setGuest] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void Promise.all([tokenStore.load(), SecureStore.getItemAsync(GUEST_KEY).catch(() => null)]).then(([token, g]) => {
      if (!alive) return;
      setGuest(g === "1");
      setHasToken(!!token);
    });
    const unsubscribe = tokenStore.subscribe((token) => {
      setHasToken(!!token);
      // Never show one account's cached data to the next.
      if (!token) queryClient.clear();
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [queryClient]);

  const signIn = useCallback(
    async (res: TokenResponse) => {
      // Load the account first, so the app stays mounted
      // instead of passing through the account gate. If it fails, the gate handles it.
      const me = await api.me.get(res.token).catch(() => null);
      queryClient.clear();
      if (me) queryClient.setQueryData(qk.me, me);
      await tokenStore.set(res.token);
    },
    [queryClient],
  );

  const endGuest = useCallback(() => {
    setGuest(false);
    void SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(GUEST_KEY).catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  const forget = useCallback(async () => {
    endGuest();
    await tokenStore.clear();
  }, [endGuest]);

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    endGuest();
    const previous = await tokenStore.clear();
    if (previous) void api.auth.logout(previous).catch(() => undefined);
  }, [endGuest]);

  const browseAsGuest = useCallback(() => {
    setGuest(true);
    void SecureStore.setItemAsync(GUEST_KEY, "1").catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  const status: AuthStatus = hasToken === null ? "loading" : hasToken ? "signedIn" : guest ? "guest" : "signedOut";
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ status, signIn, signOut, forget, browseAsGuest }), [status, signIn, signOut, forget, browseAsGuest]);
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider");
  return ctx;
}

  "crons": [{ "path": "/api/cron/maintenance", "schedule": "0 9 * * *" }]
