== prisma/schema.prisma
====================================================================================================

// Nostos data model.
// Conventions: snake_case tables/columns, all timestamps stored in UTC (timestamptz).
// A stamp is a row in `photo` (the table name is kept to avoid migration churn; API and UI say "stamp").
// A place is a row in `venue` (same reason). There is deliberately NO accuracy or ranking data anywhere.

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider  = "postgresql"
  /// Pooled connection for the app (Neon: the "-pooler" host with ?pgbouncer=true). Local: same as DIRECT_URL.
  url       = env("DATABASE_URL")
  /// Direct (unpooled) connection, used by `prisma migrate`.
  directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")
}

enum Role {
  USER
  MODERATOR
  ADMIN
}

enum SessionClient {
  WEB
  IOS
}

enum ContentType {
  PHOTO
  COMMENT
  REPORT
  /// A user account (profile reports; suspension decisions).
  USER
}

/// Why something was reported. UNDERAGE and STALKING are read first by moderators.
enum FlagCategory {
  SPAM
  HARASSMENT
  SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT
  NOT_AT_PLACE
  STALKING
  UNDERAGE
  PRIVATE_INFO
  OTHER
}

enum FlagStatus {
  OPEN
  RESOLVED
}

enum ModerationAction {
  FLAGGED
  AUTO_HIDE
  HIDE
  DISMISS
  /// Moderator reviewed hidden content (e.g. auto-hidden after 3 flags) and restored it.
  RESTORE
  /// System: the author deleted their account, so their personal photos were hidden.
  ACCOUNT_DELETED
  /// Moderator removed an abusive account (can't sign in; sessions revoked).
  SUSPEND
  UNSUSPEND
}

enum VerificationAction {
  GRANT
  REVOKE
}

model User {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  email           String    @unique
  handle          String    @unique
  displayName     String    @map("display_name")
  passwordHash    String    @map("password_hash")
  role            Role      @default(USER)
  /// Moderation reputation in [0.2, 1.5]; new accounts start at 0.5. Visible only to the user and moderators.
  reputation      Float     @default(0.5)
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Set when the account was deleted (anonymized). The row stays so stamps and the moderation log keep their history.
  deletedAt       DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsAcceptedAt DateTime? @map("terms_accepted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsVersion    String?   @map("terms_version")
  /// App Review / QA accounts: may stamp from anywhere. Their stamps are stored but visible only to themselves.
  /// Settable only by an admin or the seed.
  geofenceExempt  Boolean   @default(false) @map("geofence_exempt")
  /// Set by a moderator for abusive accounts: can't sign in, all sessions revoked. Content stays until moderated.
  suspendedAt     DateTime? @map("suspended_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Private accounts (the default for every account): only approved followers see the profile, stamps and places.
  /// See src/server/privacy.ts.
  isPrivate       Boolean   @default(true) @map("is_private")
  /// Max 150 characters, content-filtered.
  bio             String    @default("")
  /// Profile photo (re-encoded square JPEG). Shown only to viewers allowed to see the full profile.
  avatarKey       String?   @map("avatar_key")
  /// Verified badge, handpicked by an admin. Every grant and revoke is in the append-only verification_event log.
  /// Badge only: it never changes visibility, ordering or anything else.
  verifiedAt      DateTime? @map("verified_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  sessions               Session[]
  photos                 Photo[]
  following              Follow[]                 @relation("follower")
  followers              Follow[]                 @relation("followee")
  flags                  Flag[]
  moderationEvents       ModerationEvent[]
  blocking               Block[]                  @relation("blocker")
  blockedBy              Block[]                  @relation("blocked")
  followRequestsSent     FollowRequest[]          @relation("requester")
  followRequestsReceived FollowRequest[]          @relation("requestTarget")
  likes                  PhotoLike[]
  verificationEvents     VerificationEvent[]      @relation("verifiedUser")
  verificationActions    VerificationEvent[]      @relation("verifier")
  rateEvents             RateEvent[]
  pinned                 PinnedStamp[]
  completions            NeighborhoodCompletion[]

  @@map("app_user")
}

model Session {
  /// sha256 of the random token stored in the cookie; the raw token never touches the DB.
  id        String        @id
  userId    String        @map("user_id")
  /// WEB sessions ride in an httpOnly cookie; IOS sessions are bearer tokens kept in the iOS keychain.
  client    SessionClient @default(WEB)
  expiresAt DateTime      @map("expires_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  createdAt DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@map("session")
}

/// A place (the table keeps its old name). Name, location and geofence only: no live data.
model Venue {
  id             String        @id @default(cuid())
  slug           String        @unique
  name           String
  address        String
  neighborhood   String
  lat            Float
  lng            Float
  /// Radius in meters within which a phone may stamp this place (server-checked).
  geofenceMeters Int           @default(100) @map("geofence_meters")
  /// The NYC neighborhood the place is in (assigned from the boundaries), for the passport's collections.
  neighborhoodId String?       @map("neighborhood_id")
  hood           Neighborhood? @relation(fields: [neighborhoodId], references: [id])
  /// OpenStreetMap element ("node/123", "way/456") for imported places (ODbL: attribute "© OpenStreetMap contributors").
  osmId          String?       @unique @map("osm_id")
  createdAt      DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  updatedAt      DateTime      @updatedAt @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  photos Photo[]
  flags  Flag[]

  @@map("venue")
}

/// A stamp: one visit to a place, with a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix (a CHECK requires at least one).
/// Stamps are immutable except caption, song, moderation state (hidden_at) and removed_at (final); a trigger enforces it.
model Photo {
  /// Immutable database transaction of creation; photos may only be inserted in that transaction.
  creationTransaction String @default(dbgenerated("txid_current()::text")) @map("creation_transaction")
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String        @map("user_id")
  venueId      String        @map("venue_id")
  /// null = a stamp without a photo.
  storageKey   String?       @unique @map("storage_key")
  mimeType     String?       @map("mime_type")
  /// Up to 280 characters (CHECK). Empty string when there is no caption.
  caption      String
  /// Song metadata only (no audio): from Apple's iTunes Search API, via our backend.
  songTitle    String?       @map("song_title")
  songArtist   String?       @map("song_artist")
  songArtUrl   String?       @map("song_art_url")
  songSourceId String?       @map("song_source_id")
  /// Where the phone was when it stamped (kept for auditing the geofence check; never sent to viewers).
  lat          Float
  lng          Float
  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  hiddenAt     DateTime?     @map("hidden_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Optional 1 to 10 (CHECK). Not shown in the UI for v1. Immutable.
  rating       Int?          @db.SmallInt
  editedAt     DateTime?     @map("edited_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// The author removed it from their profile ("delete" means hide). Final: a trigger refuses to clear it.
  removedAt    DateTime?     @map("removed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Snapshot at write time: false when stamped by a geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) account: visible to its author only.
  countable    Boolean       @default(true)
  user         User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue        Venue         @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  likes        PhotoLike[]
  photos       StampPhoto[]
  pins         PinnedStamp[]

  @@index([venueId, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt, id])
  @@map("photo")
}

/// A neighborhood (NYC Neighborhood Tabulation Area, 2020; public NYC Open Data). `boundary` is an array of outer rings,
/// each an array of [lng, lat]; the bbox columns make point lookups cheap. Loaded by `pnpm neighborhoods:import`.
model Neighborhood {
  id          String                   @id
  name        String
  borough     String
  boundary    Json
  minLat      Float                    @map("min_lat")
  maxLat      Float                    @map("max_lat")
  minLng      Float                    @map("min_lng")
  maxLng      Float                    @map("max_lng")
  places      Venue[]
  completions NeighborhoodCompletion[]

  @@map("neighborhood")
}

/// Earned once, when a person has stamped every place in a neighborhood (a set of at least 3). Never taken back, even if
/// places are added later. Owner only.
model NeighborhoodCompletion {
  userId         String       @map("user_id")
  neighborhoodId String       @map("neighborhood_id")
  completedAt    DateTime     @map("completed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  stampId        String       @map("stamp_id")
  user           User         @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  neighborhood   Neighborhood @relation(fields: [neighborhoodId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, neighborhoodId])
  @@map("neighborhood_completion")
}

/// One photo on a stamp (up to 6 live ones, CHECK position 0 to 5 and a trigger). The scrapbook layout (x, y as 0..1 of the
/// page, rotation in degrees) is the owner's to change; the file itself never changes. Removing a photo sets removed_at
/// (final); rows are never deleted (trigger).
model StampPhoto {
  id         String    @id @default(cuid())
  stampId    String    @map("stamp_id")
  storageKey String    @unique @map("storage_key")
  mimeType   String    @map("mime_type")
  position   Int       @db.SmallInt
  x          Float?
  y          Float?
  rotation   Float?
  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  removedAt  DateTime? @map("removed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  stamp      Photo     @relation(fields: [stampId], references: [id])

  @@index([stampId, position])
  @@map("stamp_photo")
}

/// The owner's pinned stamps (up to 4), shown as ribbons on the first spread of the passport. Owner only: nothing
/// outside /api/me reads this table. Unpinning deletes the row (a pin is a layout choice, not content).
model PinnedStamp {
  userId   String   @map("user_id")
  stampId  String   @map("stamp_id")
  position Int      @db.SmallInt
  pinnedAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("pinned_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user     User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  stamp    Photo    @relation(fields: [stampId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, stampId])
  @@unique([userId, position])
  @@map("pinned_stamp")
}

model Follow {
  followerId String   @map("follower_id")
  followeeId String   @map("followee_id")
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  follower   User     @relation("follower", fields: [followerId], references: [id])
  followee   User     @relation("followee", fields: [followeeId], references: [id])

  @@id([followerId, followeeId])
  @@index([followeeId])
  @@map("follow")
}

/// A pending request to follow a private account. Approving it creates the follow row (every follow row is an
/// approved follow), declining or cancelling deletes the request.
model FollowRequest {
  requesterId String   @map("requester_id")
  targetId    String   @map("target_id")
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  requester   User     @relation("requester", fields: [requesterId], references: [id])
  target      User     @relation("requestTarget", fields: [targetId], references: [id])

  @@id([requesterId, targetId])
  @@index([targetId, createdAt])
  @@map("follow_request")
}

/// One like per user per stamp. Likes are reactions, not content: unliking deletes the row. Likes never affect anything else.
model PhotoLike {
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  photoId   String   @map("photo_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Write-time snapshot: false for geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) accounts, whose likes never count.
  countable Boolean  @default(true)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  photo     Photo    @relation(fields: [photoId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, photoId])
  @@index([photoId, createdAt])
  @@map("photo_like")
}

/// Append-only log of verified-badge grants and revokes (trigger rejects UPDATE and DELETE). A note is required.
model VerificationEvent {
  id        String             @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String             @map("user_id")
  action    VerificationAction
  note      String
  /// null = system (e.g. the account was deleted).
  actorId   String?            @map("actor_id")
  createdAt DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User               @relation("verifiedUser", fields: [userId], references: [id])
  actor     User?              @relation("verifier", fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@map("verification_event")
}

/// Rate-limit ledger for social actions (follows and requests, likes, posts). Counted under a per-user advisory
/// lock; rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model RateEvent {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  kind      String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])

  @@index([userId, kind, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("rate_event")
}

model Flag {
  id         String       @id @default(cuid())
  targetType ContentType  @map("target_type")
  targetId   String       @map("target_id")
  /// null for USER targets (profile reports).
  venueId    String?      @map("venue_id")
  flaggerId  String       @map("flagger_id")
  reason     String
  category   FlagCategory @default(OTHER)
  status     FlagStatus   @default(OPEN)
  createdAt  DateTime     @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  flagger    User         @relation(fields: [flaggerId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue?       @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  /// One OPEN flag per user per item (partial unique index in migration 3); after a review you may flag again.
  @@index([targetType, targetId, flaggerId])
  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@map("flag")
}

/// Append-only audit log. UPDATE and DELETE are rejected by a database trigger (see migration).
model ModerationEvent {
  id         String           @id @default(cuid())
  action     ModerationAction
  targetType ContentType      @map("target_type")
  targetId   String           @map("target_id")
  /// null = system action (e.g. auto-hide after 3 distinct flags)
  actorId    String?          @map("actor_id")
  note       String?
  createdAt  DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  actor      User?            @relation(fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([targetType, targetId])
  @@map("moderation_event")
}

/// Failed login attempts, for the email+IP throttle. Rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model LoginAttempt {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  email     String
  ip        String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  @@index([email, ip, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("login_attempt")
}

/// A blocks B. Symmetric in effect: neither sees the other's stamps or profile, and neither can follow the other.
model Block {
  blockerId String   @map("blocker_id")
  blockedId String   @map("blocked_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  blocker   User     @relation("blocker", fields: [blockerId], references: [id])
  blocked   User     @relation("blocked", fields: [blockedId], references: [id])

  @@id([blockerId, blockedId])
  @@index([blockedId])
  @@map("block")
}

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hand-written guarantees (invariant 5): moderation_event is append-only and
-- user content is hidden, never deleted. Row-level DELETE/UPDATE triggers do
-- not fire on TRUNCATE, so `prisma migrate reset` and the test harness still work.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: % on % is not allowed (%)', TG_OP, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_ARGV[0]
    USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER moderation_event_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "moderation_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('moderation log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER moderation_event_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "moderation_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('moderation log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER venue_cover_history_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "venue_cover_history"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('cover history is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER venue_cover_history_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "venue_cover_history"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('cover history is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER report_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "report"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');
CREATE TRIGGER photo_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');
CREATE TRIGGER comment_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "comment"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');

-- Once hidden, content stays hidden (un-hiding would need its own audited action; not in scope).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.hidden_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.hidden_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.hidden_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: hidden_at on % cannot be cleared or changed', TG_TABLE_NAME
      USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER report_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "report" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();
CREATE TRIGGER comment_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "comment" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();

-- Sanity checks on values.
ALTER TABLE "app_user" ADD CONSTRAINT app_user_reputation_range CHECK (reputation >= 0.2 AND reputation <= 1.5);
ALTER TABLE "report" ADD CONSTRAINT report_value_matches_type CHECK (
  (type = 'VIBE'  AND vibe IS NOT NULL AND line IS NULL AND cover_cents IS NULL) OR
  (type = 'LINE'  AND line IS NOT NULL AND vibe IS NULL AND cover_cents IS NULL) OR
  (type = 'COVER' AND cover_cents IS NOT NULL AND cover_cents >= 0 AND vibe IS NULL AND line IS NULL)
);
ALTER TABLE "venue" ADD CONSTRAINT venue_official_cover_nonneg CHECK (official_cover_cents IS NULL OR official_cover_cents >= 0);
ALTER TABLE "promo" ADD CONSTRAINT promo_window CHECK (ends_at > starts_at);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Moderators must be able to restore auto-hidden content after review, so hidden_at is no
-- longer sticky at the DB level. Every hide/restore goes through the moderation service,
-- which writes an append-only moderation_event in the same transaction.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS report_hidden_sticky ON "report";
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS photo_hidden_sticky ON "photo";
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS comment_hidden_sticky ON "comment";
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();

-- User content is immutable apart from moderation state (and a comment author's own edits).
-- Protects authorship, venue, timestamps and values: e.g. nobody can bump a photo's created_at
-- to put a stale photo back into the live feed (invariant 6) or rewrite a report's value.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_report_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.type, NEW.vibe, NEW.line, NEW.cover_cents, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.type, OLD.vibe, OLD.line, OLD.cover_cents, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: reports are immutable except hidden_at' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.caption, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.caption, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photos are immutable except hidden_at' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_comment_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.parent_id, NEW.is_venue_reply, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.parent_id, OLD.is_venue_reply, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only body/edited_at/hidden_at of a comment can change' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER report_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "report" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_report_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER comment_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "comment" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_comment_immutable();

ALTER TABLE "comment" ADD CONSTRAINT comment_reply_shape CHECK (is_venue_reply = (parent_id IS NOT NULL));

-- Backfill the write-time snapshot from each author's current exemption.
UPDATE "report" r SET countable = NOT u.geofence_exempt FROM "app_user" u WHERE u.id = r.user_id AND u.geofence_exempt;
UPDATE "photo" p SET countable = NOT u.geofence_exempt FROM "app_user" u WHERE u.id = p.user_id AND u.geofence_exempt;
UPDATE "comment" c SET countable = NOT u.geofence_exempt FROM "app_user" u WHERE u.id = c.user_id AND u.geofence_exempt;
UPDATE "presence" x SET countable = NOT u.geofence_exempt FROM "app_user" u WHERE u.id = x.user_id AND u.geofence_exempt;
UPDATE "rsvp" x SET countable = NOT u.geofence_exempt FROM "app_user" u WHERE u.id = x.user_id AND u.geofence_exempt;

-- The snapshot is part of the immutable record for reports and photos: once written it never changes.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.countable IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.countable THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: countable on % is a write-time snapshot and cannot change', TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER report_countable_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OF countable ON "report" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_countable_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OF countable ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER comment_countable_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OF countable ON "comment" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hand-written guarantees for the social layer.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Every account starts private, including existing ones: the column default (true) applies to every existing row.
-- Every existing follow is an approved follow: requests live in follow_request, so no follow rows change.

-- A photo's night (America/New_York, 5am rollover) is derived from created_at on insert, so featuring and
-- Regular status can never disagree with the timestamp. created_at is immutable, so night is too.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_night_of(ts timestamptz) RETURNS text AS $$
  SELECT to_char((ts AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York') - interval '5 hours', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_set_night() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.night := lastcall_night_of(NEW.created_at);
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER photo_set_night BEFORE INSERT ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_set_night();

-- Backfill existing photos. The immutability trigger below is replaced after this, and night isn't in the old one.
UPDATE "photo" SET night = lastcall_night_of(created_at);

ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_rating_range CHECK (rating IS NULL OR (rating BETWEEN 1 AND 10));
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_night_set CHECK (night <> '');

-- Photos stay immutable except moderation state and the author's post fields:
--  - caption, shared, edited_at: the author's edits (the service freezes the caption while a flag is open);
--  - rating: may be added or changed only until the end of the photo's night, and never cleared once set;
--  - removed_at: the author removing it from their profile, final once set;
--  - hidden_at: moderation (logged in moderation_event by the service).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at, NEW.night)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at, OLD.night) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photos are immutable except moderation state and the author''s post fields' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.rating IS NOT NULL AND NEW.rating IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a post''s rating can''t be cleared' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.rating IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.rating
     AND clock_timestamp() >= ((OLD.night::date + 1) + time '05:00') AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a post''s rating is locked after its night ends' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.removed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.removed_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.removed_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a removed post stays removed' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- A like is only for someone else's shared post.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT user_id, rating, shared INTO p FROM "photo" WHERE id = NEW.photo_id;
  IF p.user_id = NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: you can''t like your own post' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared posts can be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER photo_like_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "photo_like" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_like_countable_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OF countable ON "photo_like" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable();

ALTER TABLE "follow_request" ADD CONSTRAINT follow_request_not_self CHECK (requester_id <> target_id);
ALTER TABLE "follow" ADD CONSTRAINT follow_not_self CHECK (follower_id <> followee_id);
ALTER TABLE "app_user" ADD CONSTRAINT app_user_bio_length CHECK (char_length(bio) <= 150);

-- Featuring results and runs, and the verification log, are append-only.
ALTER TABLE "featured_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT featured_photo_rank_range CHECK (rank BETWEEN 1 AND 20);
ALTER TABLE "featured_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT featured_photo_min_likes CHECK (eligible_likes >= 3);
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "featured_photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring results are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "featured_photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring results are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "featured_run"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring runs are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "featured_run"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring runs are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER verification_event_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "verification_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('verification log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER verification_event_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "verification_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('verification log is append-only');
ALTER TABLE "verification_event" ADD CONSTRAINT verification_event_note CHECK (char_length(btrim(note)) BETWEEN 3 AND 300);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Review fixes for the social layer (hand-written).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- The end of a night (5am America/New_York the next morning) as an instant.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_night_end(night text) RETURNS timestamptz AS $$
  SELECT ((night::date + 1) + time '05:00') AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York';
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

-- Captions: 280 characters for posts (live-only photos are limited to 140 by the service).
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_caption_length CHECK (char_length(caption) <= 280);

-- Likes: only on someone else's visible, shared post by a live account, and never on App Review posts.
-- FOR SHARE so a concurrent un-share, removal or hide can't interleave with the insert.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT ph.user_id, ph.rating, ph.shared, ph.hidden_at, ph.removed_at, ph.countable, u.deleted_at
    INTO p
    FROM "photo" ph JOIN "app_user" u ON u.id = ph.user_id
    WHERE ph.id = NEW.photo_id
    FOR SHARE OF ph;
  IF p.user_id = NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: you can''t like your own post' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared posts can be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.hidden_at IS NOT NULL OR p.removed_at IS NOT NULL OR NOT p.countable OR p.deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: this post can''t be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- A night can only be featured once it has ended, and a featured row must match its photo: same venue and night,
-- a shared, visible post. Both tables are append-only, so a bad insert would otherwise be permanent.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_run_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF clock_timestamp() < lastcall_night_end(NEW.night) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: night % hasn''t ended yet', NEW.night USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "featured_run" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_run_guard();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_photo_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT venue_id, night, rating, shared, hidden_at, removed_at, countable INTO p FROM "photo" WHERE id = NEW.photo_id;
  IF p.venue_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.venue_id OR p.night IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.night THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a featured photo must match its venue and night' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared OR p.hidden_at IS NOT NULL OR p.removed_at IS NOT NULL OR NOT p.countable THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared, visible posts can be featured' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "featured_photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_photo_guard();

-- A follow and a pending request for the same pair never coexist: creating the follow clears the request.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_follow_clears_request() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  DELETE FROM "follow_request" WHERE requester_id = NEW.follower_id AND target_id = NEW.followee_id;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER follow_clears_request AFTER INSERT ON "follow" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_follow_clears_request();

ALTER TABLE "rate_event" ADD CONSTRAINT rate_event_kind CHECK (kind IN ('follow', 'like', 'post'));

====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20260927230000_verified_logged/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- The verified badge changes only together with its log entry: an UPDATE of app_user.verified_at must be matched by a
-- verification_event for that user, with the right action, written earlier in the same transaction. Whatever path
-- writes the column (the service, a script, a future admin tool), the append-only log can't be skipped.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_verified_needs_event() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.verified_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.verified_at AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM "verification_event" e
    WHERE e.user_id = NEW.id
      AND e.xmin = ((txid_current() % 4294967296)::text)::xid
      AND e.action = (CASE WHEN NEW.verified_at IS NULL THEN 'REVOKE' ELSE 'GRANT' END)::"VerificationAction"
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: the verified badge changes only with a verification log entry' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER app_user_verified_logged BEFORE UPDATE OF verified_at ON "app_user" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_verified_needs_event();

-- A new account can't start verified (there's no log entry yet to match).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_no_verified_insert() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.verified_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: accounts are verified through the verification log, not at creation' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER app_user_no_verified_insert BEFORE INSERT ON "app_user" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_no_verified_insert();

== prisma/migrations/20261002110000_stamps/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- Stamps (a stamp is a row in "photo"): the photo becomes optional, a stamp can carry a song, and at least one of
-- photo, caption or song must exist. "Shared to the venue page" and the night rollover are gone.
-- Hand-written guarantees below replace the triggers that referred to shared and night.

-- 1. Retire the night machinery before its columns go.
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS photo_set_night ON "photo";
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS lastcall_photo_set_night();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS lastcall_night_of(timestamptz);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS lastcall_night_end(text);

-- 2. Schema changes (generated by prisma migrate diff).
-- DropIndex
DROP INDEX "photo_user_id_venue_id_night_idx";

-- DropIndex
DROP INDEX "photo_venue_id_night_idx";

-- AlterTable
ALTER TABLE "photo" DROP COLUMN "night",
DROP COLUMN "shared",
ADD COLUMN     "song_art_url" TEXT,
ADD COLUMN     "song_artist" TEXT,
ADD COLUMN     "song_source_id" TEXT,
ADD COLUMN     "song_title" TEXT,
ALTER COLUMN "storage_key" DROP NOT NULL,
ALTER COLUMN "mime_type" DROP NOT NULL;

-- CreateIndex
CREATE INDEX "photo_created_at_id_idx" ON "photo"("created_at", "id");

-- 3. A stamp has a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix, and nothing else is a stamp.
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_has_content
  CHECK (storage_key IS NOT NULL OR btrim(caption) <> '' OR song_title IS NOT NULL);
-- A photo has both its key and its type, or neither.
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_storage_pair CHECK ((storage_key IS NULL) = (mime_type IS NULL));
-- A song has a title and an artist; the other song fields only exist with a song. Bounded lengths, https art only.
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_song_shape CHECK (
  (song_title IS NULL AND song_artist IS NULL AND song_art_url IS NULL AND song_source_id IS NULL)
  OR (song_title IS NOT NULL AND song_artist IS NOT NULL
      AND char_length(btrim(song_title)) BETWEEN 1 AND 200 AND char_length(btrim(song_artist)) BETWEEN 1 AND 200
      AND (song_art_url IS NULL OR (song_art_url LIKE 'https://%' AND char_length(song_art_url) <= 500))
      AND (song_source_id IS NULL OR char_length(song_source_id) <= 100))
);

-- 4. Stamps stay immutable except caption, song, edited_at, moderation state (hidden_at) and removed_at (final).
-- Everything that says who, where, when and what was photographed is fixed, including the rating.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at, NEW.rating, NEW.countable)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at, OLD.rating, OLD.countable) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: stamps are immutable except caption, song, moderation state and removal' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.removed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.removed_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.removed_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a removed stamp stays removed' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- 5. Likes: only on someone else's visible stamp by a live account, and never on App Review stamps.
-- FOR SHARE so a concurrent removal or hide can't interleave with the insert.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT ph.user_id, ph.hidden_at, ph.removed_at, ph.countable, u.deleted_at
    INTO p
    FROM "photo" ph JOIN "app_user" u ON u.id = ph.user_id
    WHERE ph.id = NEW.photo_id
    FOR SHARE OF ph;
  IF p.user_id = NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: you can''t like your own stamp' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.hidden_at IS NOT NULL OR p.removed_at IS NOT NULL OR NOT p.countable OR p.deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: this stamp can''t be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- 6. Rate-limit kinds: song search joins follow, like and post.
ALTER TABLE "rate_event" DROP CONSTRAINT rate_event_kind;
ALTER TABLE "rate_event" ADD CONSTRAINT rate_event_kind CHECK (kind IN ('follow', 'like', 'post', 'song_search'));

== prisma/migrations/20261003100000_stamp_photos/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- Multi-photo stamps: up to 6 photos per stamp, each with an optional scrapbook layout. Existing stamp photos are backfilled.

-- CreateTable
CREATE TABLE "stamp_photo" (
    "id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "stamp_id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "storage_key" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "mime_type" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "position" SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    "x" DOUBLE PRECISION,
    "y" DOUBLE PRECISION,
    "rotation" DOUBLE PRECISION,
    "created_at" TIMESTAMPTZ(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "removed_at" TIMESTAMPTZ(3),

    CONSTRAINT "stamp_photo_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("id")
);

-- CreateIndex
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "stamp_photo_storage_key_key" ON "stamp_photo"("storage_key");

-- CreateIndex
CREATE INDEX "stamp_photo_stamp_id_position_idx" ON "stamp_photo"("stamp_id", "position");

-- AddForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "stamp_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT "stamp_photo_stamp_id_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("stamp_id") REFERENCES "photo"("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;


-- Shape of a photo row.
ALTER TABLE "stamp_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT stamp_photo_position CHECK (position BETWEEN 0 AND 5);
ALTER TABLE "stamp_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT stamp_photo_layout CHECK (
  (x IS NULL OR x BETWEEN 0 AND 1) AND (y IS NULL OR y BETWEEN 0 AND 1) AND (rotation IS NULL OR rotation BETWEEN -45 AND 45)
);

-- At most 6 live photos per stamp. The parent row is locked so two inserts can't both pass the count.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_stamp_photo_limit() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM 1 FROM "photo" WHERE id = NEW.stamp_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM "stamp_photo" WHERE stamp_id = NEW.stamp_id AND removed_at IS NULL) >= 6 AND NEW.removed_at IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a stamp holds at most 6 photos' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER stamp_photo_limit BEFORE INSERT ON "stamp_photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_stamp_photo_limit();

-- Hidden, never deleted. The file, the stamp and the time are fixed; only the layout and removed_at (final) change.
CREATE TRIGGER stamp_photo_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "stamp_photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_stamp_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.stamp_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.created_at) IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.stamp_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: stamp photos are immutable except their layout and removal' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.removed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.removed_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.removed_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a removed photo stays removed' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER stamp_photo_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "stamp_photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_stamp_photo_immutable();

-- Backfill: every existing stamp photo becomes position 0 of its stamp.
INSERT INTO "stamp_photo" (id, stamp_id, storage_key, mime_type, position, created_at)
SELECT 'bf_' || id, id, storage_key, mime_type, 0, created_at FROM "photo" WHERE storage_key IS NOT NULL;

== prisma/migrations/20261003120000_neighborhoods/migration.sql

-- Neighborhoods and collections: NYC neighborhood boundaries, a place belongs to one, and a durable completion marker per person.

-- AlterTable
ALTER TABLE "venue" ADD COLUMN     "neighborhood_id" TEXT;

-- CreateTable
CREATE TABLE "neighborhood" (
    "id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "name" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "borough" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "boundary" JSONB NOT NULL,
    "min_lat" DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    "max_lat" DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    "min_lng" DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    "max_lng" DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT "neighborhood_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("id")
);

-- CreateTable
CREATE TABLE "neighborhood_completion" (
    "user_id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "neighborhood_id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "completed_at" TIMESTAMPTZ(3) NOT NULL,
    "stamp_id" TEXT NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT "neighborhood_completion_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("user_id","neighborhood_id")
);

-- AddForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "venue" ADD CONSTRAINT "venue_neighborhood_id_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("neighborhood_id") REFERENCES "neighborhood"("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE;

-- AddForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "neighborhood_completion" ADD CONSTRAINT "neighborhood_completion_user_id_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("user_id") REFERENCES "app_user"("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;

-- AddForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "neighborhood_completion" ADD CONSTRAINT "neighborhood_completion_neighborhood_id_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("neighborhood_id") REFERENCES "neighborhood"("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;



====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20261003130000_creation_only_photos/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- AlterTable
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD COLUMN     "creation_transaction" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT txid_current()::text;


-- Server time and xmin are insufficient: an update changes xmin, and a caller can supply created_at.
-- Capture the actual creation transaction in an immutable column, forced by the database.
CREATE FUNCTION nostos_stamp_creation_transaction() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
    NEW.creation_transaction := txid_current()::text;
  ELSIF NEW.creation_transaction IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.creation_transaction THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: the stamp creation transaction is immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER photo_creation_transaction BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON photo
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION nostos_stamp_creation_transaction();

CREATE FUNCTION nostos_stamp_photo_creation_only() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE creation_tx text;
BEGIN
  SELECT creation_transaction INTO creation_tx FROM photo WHERE id = NEW.stamp_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF creation_tx IS DISTINCT FROM txid_current()::text THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photos can only be attached when creating a stamp' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER stamp_photo_creation_only BEFORE INSERT ON stamp_photo
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION nostos_stamp_photo_creation_only();


====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20261003140000_live_stamp_content/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- This is an empty migration.

-- Generated schema diff is empty: cross-table constraints require hand-written PostgreSQL triggers.
-- The historical storage_key is immutable audit data, not evidence of a live photograph.
ALTER TABLE photo DROP CONSTRAINT photo_has_content;

CREATE FUNCTION nostos_check_stamp_content() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE stamp_id_to_check text;
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'photo' THEN stamp_id_to_check := NEW.id;
  ELSE stamp_id_to_check := NEW.stamp_id;
  END IF;
  SELECT caption, song_title, removed_at INTO p FROM photo WHERE id = stamp_id_to_check FOR UPDATE;
  IF FOUND AND p.removed_at IS NULL
     AND btrim(p.caption, E' \t\n\r\f' || chr(11) || U&'\00A0\1680\2000\2001\2002\2003\2004\2005\2006\2007\2008\2009\200A\2028\2029\202F\205F\3000\FEFF') = '' AND p.song_title IS NULL
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM stamp_photo WHERE stamp_id = stamp_id_to_check AND removed_at IS NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photo_has_content: a stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song'
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', CONSTRAINT = 'photo_has_content';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Parent before children is the lock order for every service and database content mutation.
CREATE FUNCTION nostos_lock_stamp_photo_parent() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM 1 FROM photo WHERE id = NEW.stamp_id FOR UPDATE;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER stamp_photo_lock_parent BEFORE UPDATE ON stamp_photo
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION nostos_lock_stamp_photo_parent();

-- Deferred so parent + initial photographs, or photo removal + replacement caption, commit atomically.
CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER photo_has_content AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON photo
  DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION nostos_check_stamp_content();
CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER stamp_photo_has_content AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON stamp_photo
  DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION nostos_check_stamp_content();

-- Do not silently approve historical empty stamps or invent their content. Preserve them for explicit review.
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM photo p WHERE p.removed_at IS NULL
      AND btrim(p.caption, E' \t\n\r\f' || chr(11) || U&'\00A0\1680\2000\2001\2002\2003\2004\2005\2006\2007\2008\2009\200A\2028\2029\202F\205F\3000\FEFF') = '' AND p.song_title IS NULL
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM stamp_photo sp WHERE sp.stamp_id = p.id AND sp.removed_at IS NULL)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: existing empty live stamps require review before migration' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
END;
$$;


====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20261003150000_content_snapshot_guard/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- This is an empty migration.


-- Locking alone does not advance the parent version. A repeatable-read caption edit could otherwise
-- retain an old snapshot of the children after waiting for a photo removal to commit.
-- A no-op update serializes READ COMMITTED and causes older repeatable-read snapshots to abort.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION nostos_lock_stamp_photo_parent() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  UPDATE photo SET edited_at = edited_at WHERE id = NEW.stamp_id;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;


====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20261003160000_song_content_guard/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- This is an empty migration.


-- Match service String.trim semantics at the database boundary; a whitespace-only song is not content.
-- Already removed historical records remain final and are preserved without rewriting metadata.
ALTER TABLE photo ADD CONSTRAINT photo_song_has_text CHECK (
  removed_at IS NOT NULL OR song_title IS NULL
  OR (btrim(song_title, E' \t\n\r\f' || chr(11) || U&'\00A0\1680\2000\2001\2002\2003\2004\2005\2006\2007\2008\2009\200A\2028\2029\202F\205F\3000\FEFF') <> ''
      AND btrim(song_artist, E' \t\n\r\f' || chr(11) || U&'\00A0\1680\2000\2001\2002\2003\2004\2005\2006\2007\2008\2009\200A\2028\2029\202F\205F\3000\FEFF') <> '')
);

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, sessionCookieAttributes } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { clientIp, parseJson, route } from "@/server/http";
import { login, loginSchema } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { isIosClient, publicUser } from "../client";

export const POST = route(async (req) => {
  const ios = isIosClient(req);
  const { user, session } = await login(prisma, await parseJson(req, loginSchema), { ip: clientIp(req), client: ios ? "IOS" : "WEB" });
  if (ios) return NextResponse.json({ token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt, user: publicUser(user) });
  const res = NextResponse.json({ user: publicUser(user) });
  res.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, session.token, sessionCookieAttributes(session.expiresAt));
  return res;
});


====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/auth/logout-all/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, destroyAllSessions } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { requireActor, route } from "@/server/http";

/** Signs out everywhere: revokes every web and iOS session for the current user. */
export const POST = route(async (req) => {
  const actor = await requireActor(req);
  const revoked = await destroyAllSessions(prisma, actor.userId);
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true, revoked });
  res.cookies.delete(SESSION_COOKIE);
  return res;
});


====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/auth/logout/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, destroySession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { credentials, getActor, route } from "@/server/http";

/**
 * Revokes the session this request is authenticated with. A bearer token that doesn't resolve gets 401
 * (via getActor), so the app knows nothing was revoked. Cookie logout is idempotent for the web.
 */
export const POST = route(async (req) => {
  const cred = credentials(req);
  if (cred.kind === "bearer") await getActor(req);
  await destroySession(prisma, cred.token);
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  res.cookies.delete(SESSION_COOKIE);
  return res;
});


====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, sessionCookieAttributes } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { readJson, route } from "@/server/http";
import { signup } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { isIosClient, publicUser } from "../client";

export const POST = route(async (req) => {
  const ios = isIosClient(req);
  const { user, session } = await signup(prisma, await readJson(req), { client: ios ? "IOS" : "WEB" });
  if (ios) return NextResponse.json({ token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt, user: publicUser(user) }, { status: 201 });
  const res = NextResponse.json({ user: publicUser(user) }, { status: 201 });
  res.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, session.token, sessionCookieAttributes(session.expiresAt));
  return res;
});


====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/cron/maintenance/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { isAuthorizedCron } from "@/server/cron";
import { pruneLoginAttempts } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { pruneRateEvents } from "@/server/rate-limit";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const maxDuration = 60;

/**
 * Scheduled housekeeping (daily via vercel.json). Vercel Cron issues GET with `Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET`;
 * POST is accepted too for manual runs. Prunes day-old login attempts and day-old rate-limit rows. Nothing else:
 * there are no jobs that rank, score or feature content.
 */
async function handler(req: Request) {
  if (!isAuthorizedCron(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "unauthorized", message: "Bad cron secret" }, { status: 401 });
  const started = Date.now();
  const [prunedLoginAttempts, prunedRateEvents] = await Promise.all([pruneLoginAttempts(prisma), pruneRateEvents(prisma)]);
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, prunedLoginAttempts, prunedRateEvents, ms: Date.now() - started });
}

export const GET = handler;
export const POST = handler;

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/health/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Liveness + DB check for uptime monitors and post-deploy smoke tests. */
export async function GET() {
  const started = Date.now();
  try {
    await prisma.$queryRaw`SELECT 1`;
    return NextResponse.json(
      { ok: true, db: "up", env: process.env.APP_ENV ?? "development", commit: process.env.VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_SHA?.slice(0, 7) ?? null, ms: Date.now() - started },
      { headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } },
    );
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, db: "down" }, { status: 503, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
  }
}

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/me/avatar/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES } from "@/lib/storage";
import { badRequest, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { getActor, ok, route } from "@/server/http";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { removeAvatar, setAvatar } from "@/server/services/people";

/** Profile photo upload (multipart `photo`). Authenticated and size-checked before the body is read. */
export const POST = route(async (req) => {
  const actor = requireSignedIn(await getActor(req));
  const len = req.headers.get("content-length");
  if (!len) throw new ServiceError(411, "length_required", "Content-Length is required for uploads");
  if (!(Number(len) <= MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 64 * 1024)) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  const form = await req.formData().catch(() => null);
  const file = form?.get("photo");
  if (!(file instanceof Blob)) throw badRequest("Attach a photo");
  return ok(await setAvatar(prisma, actor, Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer())));
});

export const DELETE = route(async (req) => ok(await removeAvatar(prisma, await getActor(req))));

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/places/[id]/mine/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { ok, getActor, route } from "@/server/http";
import { getMyPlacePage } from "@/server/services/stamps";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

/** Your own page for a place: your stamps there (stacked by night) and `inside`, whether `?lat=&lng=` is within its geofence. */
export const GET = route<Ctx>(async (req, { params }) => {
  const q = new URL(req.url).searchParams;
  const lat = q.get("lat"), lng = q.get("lng");
  const pos = lat !== null && lng !== null ? { lat: Number(lat), lng: Number(lng) } : null;
  return ok(await getMyPlacePage(prisma, await getActor(req), (await params).id, pos));
});

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/places/[id]/stamps/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES } from "@/lib/storage";
import { badRequest, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { getActor, ok, route } from "@/server/http";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { createStampWithProgress, MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS } from "@/server/services/stamps";

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

const field = (form: FormData, name: string) => {
  const v = form.get(name);
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : undefined;
};

/** Stamp a place. multipart/form-data: optional `photo`, `caption`, `songTitle`, `songArtist`, `songArtUrl`, `songSourceId`, and `lat`, `lng`. */
export const POST = route<Ctx>(async (req, { params }) => {
  const { id } = await params;
  // Authenticate and size-check BEFORE buffering the body: anonymous or oversized uploads never reach memory.
  const actor = requireSignedIn(await getActor(req));
  const lengthHeader = req.headers.get("content-length");
  if (!lengthHeader) throw new ServiceError(411, "length_required", "Content-Length is required for uploads");
  const declared = Number(lengthHeader);
  if (!Number.isFinite(declared) || declared > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES * MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS + 256 * 1024) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  let form: FormData;
  try {
    form = await req.formData();
  } catch {
    throw badRequest("Expected multipart/form-data");
  }
  // `photos` (repeated, up to 6); a single `photo` is accepted too.
  const files = [...form.getAll("photos"), ...form.getAll("photo")];
  if (files.some((f) => !(f instanceof Blob))) throw badRequest("photos must be files");
  const meta: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const name of ["caption", "lat", "lng", "rating", "songTitle", "songArtist", "songArtUrl", "songSourceId"]) {
    const v = field(form, name);
    if (v !== undefined) meta[name] = v;
  }
  const { stamp, progress } = await createStampWithProgress(prisma, actor, id, { photos: await Promise.all((files as Blob[]).map(async (f) => Buffer.from(await f.arrayBuffer()))), meta });
  return ok({ stamp, progress }, 201);
});

====================================================================================================
== src/app/api/uploads/[...key]/route.ts
====================================================================================================

import { getStorage, isValidKey, mimeForKey, verifyLocalSignature } from "@/lib/storage";
import { notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { route } from "@/server/http";

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ key: string[] }> };

/**
 * Serves images for the local storage driver only. Every request needs a valid, unexpired HMAC signature
 * (`?exp=&sig=`), which the service layer issues only for content the viewer may see. With R2 the API returns
 * presigned R2 URLs and this route is never used. Unsigned or expired requests get 404 so keys can't be probed.
 */
export const GET = route<Ctx>(async (req, { params }) => {
  const key = (await params).key.join("/");
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (!isValidKey(key) || !verifyLocalSignature(key, url.searchParams.get("exp"), url.searchParams.get("sig"))) throw notFound();
  const bytes = await getStorage().get(key).catch(() => null);
  if (!bytes) throw notFound();
  return new Response(new Uint8Array(bytes), {
    headers: {
      "content-type": mimeForKey(key),
      "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
      "content-security-policy": "default-src 'none'",
      "cache-control": "private, max-age=900",
    },
  });
});

====================================================================================================
== src/server/actor.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Role, SessionClient } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { getSessionUser } from "@/lib/auth/session";

/** The authenticated caller, as seen by the service layer. Every permission check takes one of these. */
export type Actor = {
  userId: string;
  role: Role;
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  /** App Review / QA account: may stamp from anywhere; its stamps are visible only to itself. */
  geofenceExempt: boolean;
};

export async function loadActor(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId } });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || user.suspendedAt) return null;
  return {
    userId: user.id,
    role: user.role,
    handle: user.handle,
    displayName: user.displayName,
    geofenceExempt: user.geofenceExempt,
  };
}

export async function actorFromToken(db: Db, token: string | undefined | null, client?: SessionClient): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const user = await getSessionUser(db, token, { client });
  return user ? loadActor(db, user.id) : null;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/cron.ts
====================================================================================================

import { timingSafeEqual, createHash } from "node:crypto";

/** Constant-time check of `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` (the header Vercel Cron sends). */
export function isAuthorizedCron(req: Request, secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET): boolean {
  if (!secret || secret.length < 16) return false;
  const header = req.headers.get("authorization") ?? "";
  const given = header.startsWith("Bearer ") ? header.slice(7) : "";
  const a = createHash("sha256").update(given).digest();
  const b = createHash("sha256").update(secret).digest();
  return timingSafeEqual(a, b) && given.length > 0;
}

import "server-only";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { actorFromToken, type Actor } from "@/server/actor";

/** Current actor for Server Components (one lookup per request). */
export const getCurrentActor = cache(async (): Promise<Actor | null> => {
  const store = await cookies();
  return actorFromToken(prisma, store.get(SESSION_COOKIE)?.value, "WEB");
});


====================================================================================================
== src/server/errors.ts
====================================================================================================

import { containsObjectionable } from "@/lib/content-filter";

/** Errors thrown by the service layer. Route handlers map `status` straight to the HTTP response. */
export class ServiceError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly status: 400 | 401 | 403 | 404 | 409 | 411 | 413 | 415 | 422 | 429 | 503,
    public readonly code: string,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ServiceError";
  }
}

export const badRequest = (message: string, code = "bad_request") => new ServiceError(400, code, message);
export const unauthorized = (message = "Sign in required") => new ServiceError(401, "unauthorized", message);
export const forbidden = (message = "Not allowed") => new ServiceError(403, "forbidden", message);
export const notFound = (message = "Not found") => new ServiceError(404, "not_found", message);
export const conflict = (message: string, code = "conflict") => new ServiceError(409, code, message);
export const tooMany = (message: string) => new ServiceError(429, "rate_limited", message);

/**
 * Rejects user-visible text containing slurs or explicit terms (App Store Guideline 1.2).
 * `fields` maps a human label ("comment", "caption", "handle") to its text.
 */
export function assertCleanText(fields: Record<string, string | null | undefined>) {
  for (const [label, text] of Object.entries(fields)) {
    if (containsObjectionable(text)) {
      throw new ServiceError(400, "objectionable_content", `Your ${label} contains language we don't allow. Please rephrase it.`);
    }
  }
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/http.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { ZodError, type ZodType } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { actorFromToken, type Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { ServiceError, badRequest, forbidden, notFound, unauthorized } from "@/server/errors";
import { socialEnabled } from "@/server/social";

/** Reads one cookie from a raw Request (works in route handlers and in tests without Next's request scope). */
export function readCookie(req: Request, name: string): string | undefined {
  const header = req.headers.get("cookie");
  if (!header) return undefined;
  for (const part of header.split(";")) {
    const idx = part.indexOf("=");
    if (idx === -1) continue;
    if (part.slice(0, idx).trim() === name) return decodeURIComponent(part.slice(idx + 1).trim());
  }
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * Client IP for rate limiting: first hop of X-Forwarded-For (on Vercel that is the real client, set by the
 * edge), then X-Real-IP, else "unknown". Never used for authorization.
 */
export function clientIp(req: Request): string {
  const xff = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for");
  const first = xff?.split(",")[0]?.trim();
  return (first || req.headers.get("x-real-ip")?.trim() || "unknown").slice(0, 64);
}

/**
 * How a request authenticates. If an Authorization header is present it is the only credential considered
 * (a malformed or unknown bearer token is unauthenticated, never a fallback to the cookie).
 */
export function credentials(req: Request): { kind: "bearer" | "cookie" | "none"; token: string | null } {
  const auth = req.headers.get("authorization");
  if (auth !== null) {
    // Auth scheme is case-insensitive (RFC 7235); tokens are base64url.
    const m = /^bearer\s+([A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,200})$/i.exec(auth.trim());
    return { kind: "bearer", token: m ? m[1]! : null };
  }
  const cookie = readCookie(req, SESSION_COOKIE);
  return cookie ? { kind: "cookie", token: cookie } : { kind: "none", token: null };
}

/**
 * The caller, or null when anonymous. A request that *sends* a bearer token which doesn't resolve (malformed,
 * expired, revoked, or not an iOS session) gets 401 `invalid_token` on every route, public ones included,
 * so the app learns immediately that it must sign in again instead of silently seeing anonymous data.
 */
export async function getActor(req: Request): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const cred = credentials(req);
  if (cred.kind === "bearer") {
    const actor = cred.token ? await actorFromToken(prisma, cred.token, "IOS") : null;
    if (!actor) throw new ServiceError(401, "invalid_token", "Your session has expired. Sign in again.");
    return actor;
  }
  return cred.token ? actorFromToken(prisma, cred.token, "WEB") : null;
}

export async function requireActor(req: Request): Promise<Actor> {
  const actor = await getActor(req);
  if (!actor) throw unauthorized();
  return actor;
}

/**
 * CSRF defense for cookie-authenticated mutations: SameSite=Lax cookies plus an Origin check.
 * Browsers always send Origin on cross-site POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE, so a mismatch is rejected.
 * Bearer-authenticated requests (the iOS app) are exempt: a cross-site page can't attach an Authorization
 * header without a CORS preflight, which this API never grants, and the cookie is ignored when a bearer
 * header is present.
 */
export function assertSameOrigin(req: Request) {
  if (req.method === "GET" || req.method === "HEAD") return;
  if (credentials(req).kind === "bearer") return;
  const origin = req.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return; // non-browser clients (tests, curl) don't send Origin; cookies are still SameSite=Lax
  const host = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-host") ?? req.headers.get("host");
  let originHost: string;
  try {
    originHost = new URL(origin).host;
  } catch {
    throw forbidden("Bad origin");
  }
  if (!host || originHost !== host) throw forbidden("Cross-origin request rejected");
}

/** Reads a JSON body without validating it; the service layer owns validation (and may transform values). */
export async function readJson(req: Request): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await req.json();
  } catch {
    throw badRequest("Body must be JSON");
  }
}

export async function parseJson<T>(req: Request, schema: ZodType<T>): Promise<T> {
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = await req.json();
  } catch {
    throw badRequest("Body must be JSON");
  }
  return schema.parse(raw);
}

type Handler<C> = (req: Request, ctx: C) => Promise<Response>;

/** A route that exists only when SOCIAL_ENABLED is on; otherwise it is a plain 404, as if it were never built. */
export function socialRoute<C>(handler: Handler<C>): Handler<C> {
  return route<C>(async (req, ctx) => {
    if (!socialEnabled()) throw notFound();
    return handler(req, ctx);
  });
}

/** For `/api/users/:handle/...`: with social off, only your own handle exists. Anyone else's is a 404. */
export function selfOrSocialRoute<C extends { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }>(handler: Handler<C>): Handler<C> {
  return route<C>(async (req, ctx) => {
    if (!socialEnabled()) {
      const actor = await getActor(req);
      const handle = (await ctx.params).handle.toLowerCase();
      if (!actor || actor.handle.toLowerCase() !== handle) throw notFound();
    }
    return handler(req, ctx);
  });
}

/** Wraps a route handler: same-origin check for mutations and uniform error mapping. */
export function route<C>(handler: Handler<C>): Handler<C> {
  return async (req, ctx) => {
    try {
      assertSameOrigin(req);
      return await handler(req, ctx);
    } catch (err) {
      return errorResponse(err);
    }
  };
}

export function errorResponse(err: unknown): Response {
  if (err instanceof ServiceError) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (err.code === "invalid_token") headers["www-authenticate"] = 'Bearer error="invalid_token"';
    else if (err.status === 401) headers["www-authenticate"] = 'Bearer realm="nostos"';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.code, message: err.message }, { status: err.status, headers });
  }
  if (err instanceof ZodError) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "invalid_input", message: err.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".") || "body"}: ${i.message}`).join("; ") },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }
  // A database backstop fired (the service checks first, so this is a race at a boundary, e.g. a rating edit that
  // commits just after 5am). The triggers' messages start with "lastcall:"; answer 409 with that sentence.
  const guard = err instanceof Error ? /lastcall: ([^\n"]+)/.exec(err.message) : null;
  if (guard) {
    const text = guard[1]!.trim();
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "conflict", message: text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1) }, { status: 409 });
  }
  console.error("[api] unhandled error", err);
  return NextResponse.json({ error: "internal", message: "Something went wrong" }, { status: 500 });
}

export const ok = (data: unknown, status = 200) => NextResponse.json(data, { status });


====================================================================================================
== src/server/media.ts
====================================================================================================

import { getStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

/**
 * Short-lived signed GET URL for a stored image (15-20 min, see lib/storage). Only ever called for content the
 * viewer may see: visible stamps, avatars shown under the privacy rules, or (for moderators) flagged stamps in the queue.
 */
export const uploadUrl = (key: string, now = new Date()) => getStorage().signedUrl(key, now);

/** Every summary uses the first live photograph in layout order, never the immutable legacy storage key. */
export const livePhotoCoverSelect = {
  photos: {
    where: { removedAt: null },
    orderBy: [{ position: "asc" as const }, { createdAt: "asc" as const }],
    take: 1,
    select: { storageKey: true as const },
  },
};


====================================================================================================
== src/server/permissions.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Central permission checks for the service layer. Every mutating service calls one of these before touching
 * the database; the UI hiding a button is never the enforcement point.
 *
 * Users own their stamps: only the author may edit one, nobody may delete one, and only MODERATOR/ADMIN may hide or
 * restore user content.
 */
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { forbidden, notFound, unauthorized } from "@/server/errors";

export function requireSignedIn(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  if (!actor) throw unauthorized();
  return actor;
}

export function isModerator(actor: Actor | null): boolean {
  return actor?.role === "MODERATOR" || actor?.role === "ADMIN";
}

export function assertModerator(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (!isModerator(a)) throw forbidden("Moderators only");
  return a;
}

export function assertAdmin(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (a.role !== "ADMIN") throw forbidden("Admins only");
  return a;
}

/** Editing user-generated content: author only. Moderators don't edit (they hide), so they're refused too. */
export function assertCanEditOwnContent(actor: Actor | null, content: { userId: string }): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (a.userId !== content.userId) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  return a;
}

/** Hiding / restoring user content is a moderator decision. */
export function assertCanModerateContent(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  return assertModerator(actor);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/privacy.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Who can see what. Every payload that names a person or shows a stamp goes through this file.
 *
 * Accounts are private by default. A viewer sees a person's **full** profile (display name, photo, bio, counts, stamps,
 * places) when any of these hold:
 *  - the viewer is that person, or a moderator/admin (staff always see real people);
 *  - the account is public;
 *  - the viewer is an approved follower (a pending request is not enough).
 * Otherwise the viewer gets the **limited** view: the handle, the verified badge and a generic avatar only, and no
 * stamps or places at all.
 *
 * A stamp is shown only to people who may see its author's full profile, so a stamp always comes with its author.
 * Nothing in Nostos is ever labeled "Anonymous". Deleted accounts are "Deleted user" and their stamps are hidden.
 *
 * Blocks (either direction) override everything except staff: a blocked pair gets the limited view and no stamps.
 * Callers also filter blocked people out (and 404 their profiles); these rules are the backstop so a forgotten caller
 * check can't leak.
 *
 * Suspended accounts are limited to everyone but staff (their profile 404s, so a handle would lead nowhere), and
 * callers' lists and search skip them.
 *
 * The functions here are pure; `followedAmong` and `blockedAmong` load the relations they need.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { socialEnabled } from "@/server/social";

/** The viewer as the rules need it. null = signed out. */
