  if (user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  if (viewer && !isSelf && (await isBlockedEitherWay(db, viewer.userId, user.id))) throw notFound("User not found");

  const [states, followsViewer] = await Promise.all([
    followStates(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]),
    viewer && !isSelf
      ? db.follow.findUnique({ where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: user.id, followeeId: viewer.userId } } }).then(Boolean)
      : Promise.resolve(false),
  ]);
  const follow: FollowState = isSelf ? "none" : (states.get(user.id) ?? "none");
  const relationship = { isSelf, follow, followsYou: followsViewer, mutual: follow === "following" && followsViewer };
  const access = profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follow === "following");
  const verified = user.verifiedAt !== null;

  if (access === "limited") {
    return { access: "limited" as const, user: { handle: user.handle, verified, isPrivate: true as const }, relationship };
  }

  const [followers, following, stamps, places] = await Promise.all([
    // Same people the lists show (active accounts).
    db.follow.count({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.follow.count({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.photo.count({ where: profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf) }),
    db.photo.findMany({ where: profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf), distinct: ["venueId"], select: { venueId: true } }).then((r) => r.length),
  ]);
  return {
    access: "full" as const,
    user: {
      handle: user.handle,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(user.avatarKey, now),
      bio: user.bio,
      verified,
      isPrivate: user.isPrivate,
      memberSince: user.createdAt,
      // Reputation is private: shown to the account itself and to moderators only (rule 7).
      reputation: isSelf || isModerator(viewer) ? user.reputation : null,
      counts: { stamps, places, followers, following },
    },
    relationship,
  };
}

export type ProfileView = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getProfile>>;

/** A list of people as the viewer may see them, with the viewer's follow state toward each. */
async function peopleView(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, rows: SubjectRow[], now: Date) {
  const states = await followStates(db, viewer?.userId, rows.map((r) => r.id));
  const v = viewerOf(viewer);
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => {
      const state = states.get(r.id) ?? "none";
      const access = profileAccess(v, toSubject(r), state === "following");
      return {
        ...personView(access, { ...r, verified: r.verifiedAt !== null }, access === "full" ? await avatarUrl(r.avatarKey, now) : null),
        follow: state,
        isSelf: viewer?.userId === r.id,
      };
    }),
  );
}

/** Followers or following of a profile. Needs full access to the profile (403 `private_account` otherwise). */
export async function listConnections(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, handle: string, which: "followers" | "following", now = new Date()) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer?.userId);
  if (blocked.includes(user.id)) throw notFound("User not found");
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]);
  if (profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follows.has(user.id)) === "limited") {
    throw new ServiceError(403, "private_account", "This account is private. Follow it to see who they follow and who follows them.");
  }
  const active = { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null, id: { notIn: blocked } };
  const rows =
    which === "followers"
      ? (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { follower: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.follower)
      : (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { followee: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.followee);
  return peopleView(db, viewer, rows, now);
}

/**
 * People search by handle, and by display name where the viewer may see it (public accounts, people they follow).
 * Matching a private account's display name would reveal it, so those match on the handle only.
 */
export async function searchUsers(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, q: string, now = new Date()) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  const query = q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^@/, "");
  if (query.length < 2) return [];
  const nameVisible = [{ isPrivate: false }, ...(viewer ? [{ followers: { some: { followerId: viewer.userId } } }] : [])];
  const users = await db.user.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [{ handle: { contains: query } }, { displayName: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" }, OR: nameVisible }],
      deletedAt: null,
      suspendedAt: null,
      ...(viewer ? { id: { notIn: [viewer.userId, ...(await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer.userId))] } } : {}),
    },
    take: 20,
    orderBy: { handle: "asc" },
    select: subjectSelect,
  });
  return peopleView(db, viewer, users, now);
}

/** People you follow (approved follows only, so you have full access to each). */
export async function listFollowing(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  const me = requireSignedIn(viewer);
  const rows = await db.follow.findMany({
    where: { followerId: me.userId, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { followee: { handle: "asc" } },
    select: { followee: { select: { ...subjectSelect, following: { where: { followeeId: me.userId }, select: { followerId: true } } } } },
  });
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async ({ followee: f }) => ({
      handle: f.handle, displayName: f.displayName, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(f.avatarKey, now), verified: f.verifiedAt !== null,
      mutual: f.following.length > 0,
    })),
  );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Profile settings
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const BIO_MAX = 150;

export const profileSettingsSchema = z
  .object({
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1, "Enter a name").max(60, "Name: 60 characters max"),
    bio: z.string().trim().max(BIO_MAX, `Bio: ${BIO_MAX} characters max`),
    isPrivate: z.boolean(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict();

async function settingsView(db: Db, userId: string, now: Date) {
  const [u, pendingRequests] = await Promise.all([
    db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: userId }, select: { handle: true, displayName: true, bio: true, isPrivate: true, avatarKey: true, verifiedAt: true } }),
    db.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
  ]);
  return {
    profile: { handle: u.handle, displayName: u.displayName, bio: u.bio, isPrivate: u.isPrivate, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(u.avatarKey, now), verified: u.verifiedAt !== null },
    /** Still pending after switching to public: the app offers "Approve N pending requests?". */
    pendingRequests,
  };
}

/**
 * Edit display name, bio and the private toggle. Switching private to public doesn't approve pending requests;
 * that's a separate, explicit action (approveAllFollowRequests).
 */
export async function updateProfile(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const input = profileSettingsSchema.parse(raw);
  assertCleanText({ name: input.displayName, bio: input.bio });
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: input });
  return settingsView(db, me.userId, now);
}

export async function getProfileSettings(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  return settingsView(db, requireSignedIn(actor).userId, now);
}

export const AVATAR_SIZE = 512;

/** Profile photos: same checks as venue photos (size, magic bytes, full decode), then a square JPEG with no metadata. */
export async function processAvatar(input: Buffer) {
  if (input.length === 0) throw badRequest("Empty file");
  if (input.length > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  if (!sniffImage(input)) throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "Only JPEG, PNG or WebP images are allowed");
  try {
    return await sharp(input, { failOn: "error", limitInputPixels: 50_000_000 })
      .rotate()
      .resize({ width: AVATAR_SIZE, height: AVATAR_SIZE, fit: "cover" })
      .jpeg({ quality: 85 })
      .toBuffer();
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "That image couldn't be read. Try a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.");
  }
}

export async function setAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, bytes: Buffer, now = new Date(), storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const clean = await processAvatar(bytes);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  const key = newStorageKey("avatars", "jpg");
  await storage.put(key, clean, "image/jpeg");
  try {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: key } });
  } catch (err) {
    await storage.delete(key).catch(() => {});
    throw err;
  }
  if (before.avatarKey) await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  return { avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(key, now) };
}

export async function removeAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  if (before.avatarKey) {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: null } });
    await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  }
  return { avatarUrl: null };
}

export type PersonWithState = PersonView & { follow: FollowState; isSelf: boolean };

== src/server/services/pins.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Pinned stamps: up to 4 of your own, shown as ribbons on the first spread of your passport. Owner only: the only reader is
 * `/api/me/pins`, so nothing here is ever part of anyone else's view of you (rule 2).
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { getStamp } from "@/server/services/stamps";

export const MAX_PINS = 4;
export const pinsSchema = z.object({ stampIds: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(64)).max(MAX_PINS, `Pin up to ${MAX_PINS} stamps`) }).strict();

/** Your pinned stamps, in the order you pinned them. Stamps that were since hidden or removed drop out. */
export async function listPins(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const rows = await db.pinnedStamp.findMany({
    where: { userId: me.userId, stamp: { hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { position: "asc" },
    select: { stampId: true },
  });
  const stamps = await Promise.all(rows.map((r) => getStamp(db, me, r.stampId, now).then((x) => x.stamp)));
  return { stamps };
}

/** Replace your pins with `stampIds` (in order). Only your own live stamps, no repeats, at most 4. */
export async function setPins(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const { stampIds } = pinsSchema.parse(raw);
  if (new Set(stampIds).size !== stampIds.length) throw badRequest("Each stamp can be pinned once");
  const own = await db.photo.findMany({ where: { id: { in: stampIds }, userId: me.userId, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null }, select: { id: true } });
  if (own.length !== stampIds.length) throw badRequest("You can only pin your own stamps");
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.pinnedStamp.deleteMany({ where: { userId: me.userId } });
    for (const [position, stampId] of stampIds.entries()) await tx.pinnedStamp.create({ data: { userId: me.userId, stampId, position, pinnedAt: now } });
  });
  return listPins(db, me, now);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/places.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Places: name, location and geofence. No live data of any kind (no crowd, no reports, no counts of who is there).
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { distanceMeters } from "@/lib/geo";
import { notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { OSM_ATTRIBUTION } from "@/lib/osm";

const placeSelect = { id: true, slug: true, name: true, address: true, neighborhood: true, lat: true, lng: true, geofenceMeters: true, neighborhoodId: true, osmId: true } as const;
type PlaceRow = { id: string; slug: string; name: string; address: string; neighborhood: string; lat: number; lng: number; geofenceMeters: number; neighborhoodId: string | null; osmId: string | null };

export type PlaceView = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  geofenceMeters: number;
  /** The NYC neighborhood this place is in, when known. */
  neighborhoodId: string | null;
  /** Imported from OpenStreetMap (show "© OpenStreetMap contributors"). */
  fromOsm: boolean;
};

const toView = ({ osmId, ...p }: PlaceRow): PlaceView => ({ ...p, fromOsm: osmId !== null });

/** ODbL attribution to show with a place list, when any place in it came from OpenStreetMap. */
export const attributionFor = (places: readonly { fromOsm: boolean }[]) => (places.some((p) => p.fromOsm) ? OSM_ATTRIBUTION : null);

export async function listPlaces(db: Db): Promise<PlaceView[]> {
  return (await db.venue.findMany({ orderBy: { name: "asc" }, select: placeSelect })).map(toView);
}

/**
 * Places near a position, nearest first, within `radiusM` (default 300 m: inside or close to the geofence). The server
 * recomputes the geofence again when a stamp is submitted; this list is only a convenience. The caller's own
 * position is used for the distance and never stored.
 */
export async function nearbyPlaces(db: Db, pos: { lat: number; lng: number }, radiusM = 300, limit = 20) {
  const all = await db.venue.findMany({ select: placeSelect });
  return all
    .map((p) => ({ ...toView(p), distanceM: Math.round(distanceMeters(pos, p)) }))
    .filter((p) => p.distanceM <= Math.max(radiusM, p.geofenceMeters))
    .sort((a, b) => a.distanceM - b.distanceM || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map((p) => ({ ...p, inside: p.distanceM <= p.geofenceMeters }));
}

/** A place by id or slug. */
export async function getPlace(db: Db, key: string): Promise<PlaceView> {
  const p = await db.venue.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ id: key }, { slug: key }] }, select: placeSelect });
  if (!p) throw notFound("Place not found");
  return toView(p);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/songs.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Song search for the stamp composer. v1 stores metadata only (title, artist, art URL); there is no audio.
 * Proxied to Apple's public iTunes Search API through our backend so the phone never talks to Apple, with a short
 * timeout, a per-user rate limit, and no user identifiers sent upstream: only the search term, a fixed country and a
 * fixed limit. The terms-of-use question for this API is in OPEN-QUESTIONS.md (L1); nothing here decides it.
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { containsObjectionable } from "@/lib/content-filter";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";

export const ITUNES_SEARCH_URL = "https://itunes.apple.com/search";
export const SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
export const SONG_RESULTS = 15;

export type SongResult = { title: string; artist: string; album: string | null; artUrl: string | null; sourceId: string };

const itunesResponse = z.object({
  results: z.array(
    z.object({
      trackId: z.number(),
      trackName: z.string(),
      artistName: z.string(),
      collectionName: z.string().optional(),
      artworkUrl100: z.string().optional(),
    }).passthrough(),
  ),
});

/** Apple's art URLs end in `100x100bb.jpg`; ask for a larger rendition. Only https links on Apple's CDN are kept. */
function artFrom(url: string | undefined): string | null {
  if (!url) return null;
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    if (u.protocol !== "https:" || !/(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(u.hostname)) return null;
    return u.toString().replace(/\/\d+x\d+bb\./, "/300x300bb.");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export const songQuerySchema = z.string().trim().min(2, "Type at least 2 characters").max(80, "Search is limited to 80 characters");

export async function searchSongs(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, rawQuery: unknown, now = new Date(), fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<{ songs: SongResult[] }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const term = songQuerySchema.parse(rawQuery ?? "");
  await db.$transaction((tx) => consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "song_search", now));

  const url = new URL(ITUNES_SEARCH_URL);
  url.searchParams.set("term", term);
  url.searchParams.set("media", "music");
  url.searchParams.set("entity", "song");
  url.searchParams.set("country", "US");
  url.searchParams.set("limit", String(SONG_RESULTS));
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    // No cookies, no auth header, no user id: the request carries only the search term.
    const res = await fetchImpl(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS), headers: { accept: "application/json" }, credentials: "omit", redirect: "error" });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);
    body = await res.json();
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(503, "song_search_unavailable", "Song search isn't available right now. Try again in a moment.");
  }
  const parsed = itunesResponse.safeParse(body);
  if (!parsed.success) throw badRequest("Song search returned something unexpected", "song_search_bad_response");
  const songs = parsed.data.results
    .filter((r) => r.trackName.length <= 200 && r.artistName.length <= 200 && !containsObjectionable(r.trackName) && !containsObjectionable(r.artistName))
    .map((r) => ({ title: r.trackName, artist: r.artistName, album: r.collectionName ?? null, artUrl: artFrom(r.artworkUrl100), sourceId: String(r.trackId) }));
  return { songs };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/stamps.ts
====================================================================================================

import { assertSocialEnabled, socialEnabled } from "@/server/social";
/**
 * Stamps: one visit to a place, with a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix (at least one). A stamp is a row in the
 * `photo` table (the name is kept to avoid migration churn); the API and the app say "stamp".
 *
 *  - Created only inside the place's geofence, checked here from the submitted coordinates (rule 1). Geofence-exempt
 *    (App Review) accounts may stamp from anywhere, and their stamps are visible only to themselves.
 *  - One stamp per person per place per 15 minutes, and a per-hour limit across places. EXIF is stripped (rule 5).
 *  - Visibility = the author's account privacy (src/server/privacy.ts#canSeeStamp). There is no per-stamp setting.
 *  - The author can edit the caption and the song (frozen while a moderator is looking at it) and "delete from
 *    profile", which sets removed_at for good. Nothing is ever deleted (rule 4).
 *  - Song data is metadata only; no audio. The art URL must come from Apple's artwork CDN.
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { distanceMeters, isValidLatLng } from "@/lib/geo";
import { localParts } from "@/lib/time";
import { pageAndSlot } from "@/lib/layout";
import { getStorage, newStorageKey, type BlobStorage } from "@/lib/storage";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { livePhotoCoverSelect, uploadUrl } from "@/server/media";
import { recordProgress } from "@/server/services/neighborhoods";
import { assertCleanText, badRequest, conflict, notFound, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertCanEditOwnContent, isModerator, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { authorResolver, canSeeStamp, subjectSelect, toSubject, type SubjectRow } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { assertInsideGeofence, lockAndCheckCooldown } from "@/server/services/geofence";
import { processImage } from "@/server/services/image";
import { isBlockedEitherWay } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { likeSummaries, type LikeSummary } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { avatarUrl, profileStampsWhere } from "@/server/services/people";

export const CAPTION_MAX = 280;
export const STAMPS_PAGE = 30;
const SONG_FIELD_MAX = 200;

/** Album art only from Apple's artwork CDN, over https, so a stamp can't embed a tracking pixel from anywhere else. */
const artUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(500)
  .refine((u) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(u);
      return url.protocol === "https:" && /(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(url.hostname);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "Song art must be an https link from Apple's artwork servers");

/** Empty strings (multipart fields) mean "absent". */
const optional = <T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(schema: T) => z.preprocess((v) => (v === "" || v === null ? undefined : v), schema.optional());

const songShape = {
  songTitle: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX)),
  songArtist: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX)),
  songArtUrl: optional(artUrl),
  songSourceId: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(100)),
};

const checkSong = (m: { songTitle?: string; songArtist?: string; songArtUrl?: string; songSourceId?: string }, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) => {
  const any = m.songTitle !== undefined || m.songArtist !== undefined || m.songArtUrl !== undefined || m.songSourceId !== undefined;
  if (any && (m.songTitle === undefined || m.songArtist === undefined)) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["songTitle"], message: "A song needs a title and an artist" });
  }
};

export const stampMetaSchema = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(CAPTION_MAX, `Caption: ${CAPTION_MAX} characters max`).default(""),
    lat: z.coerce.number().finite(),
    lng: z.coerce.number().finite(),
    /** Optional, stored but not shown in v1. */
    rating: optional(z.coerce.number().int("Rating: a whole number from 1 to 10").min(1, "Rating: 1 to 10").max(10, "Rating: 1 to 10")),
    ...songShape,
  })
  .strict()
  .superRefine(checkSong);

export const stampEditSchema = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(CAPTION_MAX, `Caption: ${CAPTION_MAX} characters max`),
    /** null removes the song. */
    song: z
      .object({
        title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX),
        artist: z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX),
        artUrl: artUrl.nullish(),
        sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).nullish(),
      })
      .strict()
      .nullable(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");

type StampRow = {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  storageKey: string | null;
  caption: string;
  songTitle: string | null;
  songArtist: string | null;
  songArtUrl: string | null;
  songSourceId: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  editedAt: Date | null;
  hiddenAt: Date | null;
  removedAt: Date | null;
  countable: boolean;
  user: SubjectRow;
  venue: { id: string; slug: string; name: string; neighborhood: string; neighborhoodId: string | null };
  photos: { id: string; storageKey: string; position: number; x: number | null; y: number | null; rotation: number | null }[];
};

const stampSelect = {
  id: true, userId: true, storageKey: true, caption: true, songTitle: true, songArtist: true, songArtUrl: true, songSourceId: true,
  createdAt: true, editedAt: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true,
  user: { select: subjectSelect },
  venue: { select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true, neighborhood: true, neighborhoodId: true } },
  /** Live photos only, in page order. */
  photos: { where: { removedAt: null }, orderBy: [{ position: "asc" as const }, { createdAt: "asc" as const }], select: { id: true, storageKey: true, position: true, x: true, y: true, rotation: true } },
};

export const MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS = 6;

export type StampView = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof toStampViews>>[number];

/** Each stamp's 0-based position among its owner's live stamps, oldest first (the input to the shared layout function). */
async function ordinals(db: Db, rows: readonly StampRow[]) {
  const out = new Map<string, number>();
  const userIds = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.userId))];
  if (!userIds.length) return out;
  const all = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: { userId: { in: userIds }, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null },
    orderBy: [{ userId: "asc" }, { createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    select: { id: true, userId: true },
  });
  const counter = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const s of all) {
    const n = counter.get(s.userId) ?? 0;
    out.set(s.id, n);
    counter.set(s.userId, n + 1);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Builds the wire shape. Callers have already decided each stamp is visible to `actor`. Never includes coordinates, keys or user ids. */
async function toStampViews(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, rows: readonly StampRow[], now: Date) {
  const places = await ordinals(db, rows);
  const likes = await likeSummaries(db, rows.map((r) => r.id), actor?.userId);
  const none: LikeSummary = { count: 0, viewerLiked: false };
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      photoUrl: r.photos[0] ? await uploadUrl(r.photos[0].storageKey, now) : null,
      /** Up to 6, in page order. x and y are 0..1 of the page and rotation is degrees, null until the owner lays them out. */
      photos: await Promise.all(r.photos.map(async (p) => ({ id: p.id, url: await uploadUrl(p.storageKey, now), position: p.position, x: p.x, y: p.y, rotation: p.rotation }))),
      caption: r.caption,
      song: r.songTitle ? { title: r.songTitle, artist: r.songArtist!, artUrl: r.songArtUrl, sourceId: r.songSourceId } : null,
      /** Page and slot in the owner's passport (oldest first, three to a page); null for a stamp that is hidden or removed. */
      ...(places.has(r.id) ? pageAndSlot(places.get(r.id)!) : { page: null, slot: null }),
      createdAt: r.createdAt,
      editedAt: r.editedAt,
      place: { id: r.venue.id, slug: r.venue.slug, name: r.venue.name, neighborhood: r.venue.neighborhood, neighborhoodId: r.venue.neighborhoodId },
      author: { handle: r.user.handle, displayName: r.user.displayName, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(r.user.avatarKey, now), verified: r.user.verifiedAt !== null },
      mine: actor?.userId === r.userId,
      likes: likes.get(r.id) ?? none,
      /** Staff see hidden and removed stamps (moderation); everyone else never receives them. */
      hidden: r.hiddenAt !== null,
      removed: r.removedAt !== null,
    })),
  );
}

/** Creates a stamp. Photos are optional (up to 6): a stamp may be a caption, a song, or both with no photo. */
export async function createStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, venueId: string, input: { bytes?: Buffer | null; photos?: Buffer[]; meta: unknown }, now = new Date(), storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  return (await createStampWithProgress(db, actor, venueId, input, now, storage)).stamp;
}

/** The same, plus the neighborhood progress this stamp made (for the landing animation). */
export async function createStampWithProgress(
  db: Db,
  actor: Actor | null,
  venueId: string,
  input: { bytes?: Buffer | null; photos?: Buffer[]; meta: unknown },
  now = new Date(),
  storage: BlobStorage = getStorage(),
) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const meta = stampMetaSchema.parse(input.meta);
  const buffers = [...(input.bytes ? [input.bytes] : []), ...(input.photos ?? [])].filter((b) => b.length > 0);
  if (buffers.length > MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS) throw badRequest(`A stamp holds up to ${MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS} photos`);
  if (buffers.length === 0 && !meta.caption && meta.songTitle === undefined) throw badRequest("A stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song");
  assertCleanText({ caption: meta.caption, "song title": meta.songTitle, "song artist": meta.songArtist });
  const place = await db.venue.findUnique({ where: { id: venueId }, select: { id: true, lat: true, lng: true, geofenceMeters: true } });
  if (!place) throw notFound("Place not found");
  if (me.geofenceExempt) {
    if (!isValidLatLng(meta)) throw badRequest("Invalid coordinates");
  } else assertInsideGeofence(meta, place);
  // Every photo is fully re-encoded: EXIF and GPS never reach storage (rule 5).
  const images = await Promise.all(buffers.map((b) => processImage(b)));
  const keys = images.map((img) => newStorageKey("photos", img.kind.ext));
  const written: string[] = [];
  let progress: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof recordProgress>> = null;
  try {
    const row = await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await lockAndCheckCooldown(tx, { userId: me.userId, venueId }, now);
      await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "post", now);
      const created = await tx.photo.create({
        data: {
          userId: me.userId, venueId, storageKey: keys[0] ?? null, mimeType: images[0]?.kind.mime ?? null, caption: meta.caption, lat: meta.lat, lng: meta.lng,
          createdAt: now, countable: !me.geofenceExempt, rating: meta.rating ?? null,
          songTitle: meta.songTitle ?? null, songArtist: meta.songArtist ?? null, songArtUrl: meta.songArtUrl ?? null, songSourceId: meta.songSourceId ?? null,
        },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      for (const [i, img] of images.entries()) {
        await tx.stampPhoto.create({ data: { stampId: created.id, storageKey: keys[i]!, mimeType: img.kind.mime, position: i, createdAt: now } });
      }
      // App Review stamps (outside the geofence) never count toward a neighborhood.
      if (!me.geofenceExempt) progress = await recordProgress(tx, me.userId, venueId, created.id, now);
      // Written last inside the transaction: if a write fails the rows roll back, and if the commit fails we clean up below.
      for (const [i, img] of images.entries()) {
        await storage.put(keys[i]!, img.bytes, img.kind.mime);
        written.push(keys[i]!);
      }
      return tx.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: created.id }, select: stampSelect });
    });
    return { stamp: (await toStampViews(db, me, [row], now))[0]!, progress };
  } catch (err) {
    await Promise.all(written.map((k) => storage.delete(k).catch(() => {})));
    throw err;
  }
}

async function ownStamp(db: Db | Prisma.TransactionClient, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const stamp = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: stampId }, select: { id: true, userId: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, caption: true, songTitle: true } });
  if (!stamp || stamp.removedAt) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  assertCanEditOwnContent(me, stamp);
  if (stamp.hiddenAt) throw conflict("This stamp was hidden by a moderator and can't be edited");
  return { me, stamp };
}

/** Compatibility endpoint: photographs are fixed at creation, never appended later. */
export async function addStampPhotos(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, buffers: Buffer[], now = new Date(), storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  await ownStamp(db, actor, stampId);
  // Retain the signature for callers, but reject before decoding images or writing files.
  void buffers;
  void now;
  void storage;
  throw badRequest("Photos can only be attached when creating a stamp");
}

/** Lock the parent before reading any editable content or live photo count. */
async function lockStamp(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, stampId: string) {
  await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM photo WHERE id = ${stampId} FOR UPDATE`;
}

/** Remove one photo permanently, atomically with the actual live-content check. */
export async function removeStampPhoto(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, photoId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockStamp(tx, stampId);
    const { stamp } = await ownStamp(tx, me, stampId);
    const live = await tx.stampPhoto.findMany({ where: { stampId, removedAt: null }, select: { id: true } });
    if (!live.some((p) => p.id === photoId)) throw notFound("Photo not found");
    if (live.length === 1 && !stamp.caption.trim() && !stamp.songTitle) throw badRequest("A stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song");
    await tx.stampPhoto.update({ where: { id: photoId }, data: { removedAt: now } });
    await tx.photo.update({ where: { id: stampId }, data: { editedAt: now } });
  });
  return getStamp(db, me, stampId, now);
}

export const photoLayoutSchema = z
  .object({
    photos: z
      .array(
        z
          .object({
            id: z.string().min(1).max(64),
            position: z.number().int().min(0).max(MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS - 1).optional(),
            x: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(),
            y: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(),
            rotation: z.number().min(-45).max(45).nullable().optional(),
          })
          .strict(),
      )
      .min(1)
      .max(MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS),
  })
  .strict();

/**
 * The scrapbook layout: where each photo sits on the page (x, y as 0..1, rotation in degrees) and their order. Positions
 * given for the live photos must stay unique. Only the owner may do this, and the files never change.
 */
export async function setPhotoLayout(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const { me } = await ownStamp(db, actor, stampId);
  const input = photoLayoutSchema.parse(raw);
  const live = await db.stampPhoto.findMany({ where: { stampId, removedAt: null }, select: { id: true, position: true } });
  const ids = new Set(live.map((p) => p.id));
  if (new Set(input.photos.map((p) => p.id)).size !== input.photos.length || input.photos.some((p) => !ids.has(p.id))) throw badRequest("Those photos aren't on this stamp");
  const after = new Map(live.map((p) => [p.id, p.position]));
  for (const p of input.photos) if (p.position !== undefined) after.set(p.id, p.position);
  if (new Set(after.values()).size !== after.size) throw badRequest("Each photo needs its own position");
  await db.$transaction(
    input.photos.map((p) =>
      db.stampPhoto.update({ where: { id: p.id }, data: { ...(p.position !== undefined ? { position: p.position } : {}), ...(p.x !== undefined ? { x: p.x } : {}), ...(p.y !== undefined ? { y: p.y } : {}), ...(p.rotation !== undefined ? { rotation: p.rotation } : {}) } }),
    ),
  );
  return getStamp(db, me, stampId, now);
}

/** Stamp detail. 404 when the viewer may not see it (privacy.ts#canSeeStamp), so existence isn't revealed. */
export async function getStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const row = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: stampId }, select: stampSelect });
  if (!row) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  await assertVisible(db, actor, row);
  return { stamp: (await toStampViews(db, actor, [row], now))[0]! };
}

/** Throws 404 unless the viewer may see `row`; shared by detail, likes and flags. */
export async function assertVisible(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, row: Pick<StampRow, "userId" | "hiddenAt" | "removedAt" | "countable" | "user">) {
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, [row.userId]);
  if ((row.user.deletedAt || row.user.suspendedAt) && !resolve.viewer?.staff) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  const subject = { authorId: row.userId, hidden: row.hiddenAt !== null, removed: row.removedAt !== null, countable: row.countable };
  if (!canSeeStamp(resolve.viewer, subject, resolve.access(toSubject(row.user)), resolve.blocks.has(row.userId))) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
}

/** Author edits: caption and song. Send only changed fields; `song: null` removes the song. */
export async function editStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const input = stampEditSchema.parse(raw);
  assertCleanText({ caption: input.caption, "song title": input.song?.title, "song artist": input.song?.artist });
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockStamp(tx, stampId);
    const stamp = await tx.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: stampId } });
    if (!stamp || stamp.removedAt) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
    assertCanEditOwnContent(me, stamp);
    if (stamp.hiddenAt) throw conflict("This stamp was hidden by a moderator and can't be edited");
    // Freeze while a moderator is looking at it, so it can't be rewritten mid-review.
    if ((await tx.flag.count({ where: { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: stamp.id, status: "OPEN" } })) > 0) {
      throw conflict("This stamp is under moderator review, so it can't be edited right now");
    }
    const caption = input.caption ?? stamp.caption;
    const song =
      input.song === undefined
        ? stamp.songTitle ? { title: stamp.songTitle, artist: stamp.songArtist!, artUrl: stamp.songArtUrl, sourceId: stamp.songSourceId } : null
        : input.song && { title: input.song.title, artist: input.song.artist, artUrl: input.song.artUrl ?? null, sourceId: input.song.sourceId ?? null };
    const livePhotos = await tx.stampPhoto.count({ where: { stampId, removedAt: null } });
    if (livePhotos === 0 && !caption.trim() && !song) throw badRequest("A stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song");
    await tx.photo.update({
      where: { id: stamp.id },
      data: {
        caption,
        songTitle: song?.title ?? null, songArtist: song?.artist ?? null, songArtUrl: song?.artUrl ?? null, songSourceId: song?.sourceId ?? null,
        editedAt: now,
      },
    });
  });
  return getStamp(db, me, stampId, now);
}

/** "Delete from profile": hides the stamp everywhere for good (removed_at, final). Nothing is deleted. */
export async function removeStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const stamp = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: stampId } });
  if (!stamp || stamp.removedAt) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  assertCanEditOwnContent(me, stamp);
  await db.photo.update({ where: { id: stamp.id }, data: { removedAt: now } });
  return { removed: true as const };
}

/** Access to a person's stamps and places: the full profile (403 `private_account` otherwise), and no block. */
async function assertProfileVisible(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(actor))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const isSelf = actor?.userId === user.id;
  if (!socialEnabled() && !isSelf) throw notFound("User not found");
  if (actor && !isSelf && (await isBlockedEitherWay(db, actor.userId, user.id))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, [user.id]);
  if (resolve.access(toSubject(user)) === "limited") {
    throw new ServiceError(403, "private_account", "This account is private. Follow it to see their stamps.");
  }
  return { user, isSelf };
}

/** Page cursor: the last stamp's time and id, so stamps sharing a millisecond are never skipped. */
const cursorOf = (p: { createdAt: Date; id: string }) => `${p.createdAt.toISOString()}_${p.id}`;
export function parseCursor(before: string) {
  const m = /^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T[\d:.]+Z)_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64})$/.exec(before);
  const at = m ? new Date(m[1]!) : null;
  if (!m || !at || Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) throw badRequest("before: use the previous page's nextBefore");
  return { at, id: m[2]! };
}
export const beforeCursor = (c: { at: Date; id: string }) => ({ OR: [{ createdAt: { lt: c.at } }, { createdAt: c.at, id: { lt: c.id } }] });

/** The passport's stamps, OLDEST first (so page 1 stays page 1), 30 at a time. `after` = the previous page's `nextAfter`. */
export async function listUserStamps(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, after?: string | null, now = new Date()) {
  const { user, isSelf } = await assertProfileVisible(db, actor, handle);
  const c = after ? parseCursor(after) : null;
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: { ...profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf), ...(c ? { OR: [{ createdAt: { gt: c.at } }, { createdAt: c.at, id: { gt: c.id } }] } : {}) },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    take: STAMPS_PAGE + 1,
    select: stampSelect,
  });
  const page = rows.slice(0, STAMPS_PAGE);
  return { stamps: await toStampViews(db, actor, page, now), nextAfter: rows.length > STAMPS_PAGE ? cursorOf(page[page.length - 1]!) : null };
}

/** Every place a person has stamped (the Places view), with their stamp count there and the latest stamp. Newest first. */
export async function listUserPlaces(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, now = new Date()) {
  const { user, isSelf } = await assertProfileVisible(db, actor, handle);
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf),
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    select: { id: true, createdAt: true, ...livePhotoCoverSelect, caption: true, venue: { select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true, neighborhood: true, lat: true, lng: true } } },
  });
  const places = new Map<string, { place: (typeof rows)[number]["venue"]; stamps: number; latest: (typeof rows)[number] }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const p = places.get(r.venue.id);
    if (p) p.stamps++;
    else places.set(r.venue.id, { place: r.venue, stamps: 1, latest: r });
  }
  return {
    places: await Promise.all(
      [...places.values()].map(async ({ place, stamps, latest }) => ({
        place,
        stamps,
        latestStamp: { id: latest.id, createdAt: latest.createdAt, photoUrl: latest.photos[0] ? await uploadUrl(latest.photos[0].storageKey, now) : null, caption: latest.caption },
      })),
    ),
  };
}

/**
 * Home feed: stamps from the people you follow, newest first, 30 at a time. Strictly chronological: no ranking, no
 * personalization, no suggestions (rule 9). Only approved follows, never blocked, suspended or deleted people.
 */
export async function listFeed(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, before?: string | null, now = new Date()) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const c = before ? parseCursor(before) : null;
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: {
      hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null, countable: true,
      user: {
        deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null,
        followers: { some: { followerId: me.userId } },
        blocking: { none: { blockedId: me.userId } },
        blockedBy: { none: { blockerId: me.userId } },
      },
      ...(c ? beforeCursor(c) : {}),
    },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: STAMPS_PAGE + 1,
    select: stampSelect,
  });
  const page = rows.slice(0, STAMPS_PAGE);
  return { stamps: await toStampViews(db, me, page, now), nextBefore: rows.length > STAMPS_PAGE ? cursorOf(page[page.length - 1]!) : null };
}

/** The local calendar day (America/New_York) a stamp was made on: YYYY-MM-DD. Stamps at one place on one day stack together. */
export const stampDay = (d: Date) => {
  const p = localParts(d);
  return `${p.year}-${String(p.month).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(p.day).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};

/**
 * Your own page for one place: every stamp you made there, oldest first, grouped by the day (so the same place on different
 * nights stacks as separate visits), plus whether you are inside its geofence right now. The position is only used for this
 * answer (never stored). Owner only: it is built from your own stamps and nobody else's.
 */
export async function getMyPlacePage(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, idOrSlug: string, pos?: { lat: number; lng: number } | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const place = await db.venue.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ id: idOrSlug }, { slug: idOrSlug }] }, select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true, address: true, neighborhood: true, neighborhoodId: true, lat: true, lng: true, geofenceMeters: true, osmId: true } });
  if (!place) throw notFound("Place not found");
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({ where: { userId: me.userId, venueId: place.id, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null }, orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }], select: stampSelect });
  const stamps = await toStampViews(db, me, rows, now);
  const visits: { day: string; stamps: typeof stamps }[] = [];
  for (const st of stamps) {
    const day = stampDay(st.createdAt);
    const last = visits[visits.length - 1];
    if (last && last.day === day) last.stamps.push(st);
    else visits.push({ day, stamps: [st] });
  }
  const here = pos && isValidLatLng(pos) ? Math.round(distanceMeters(pos, place)) : null;
  const { osmId, ...rest } = place;
  return {
    place: { ...rest, fromOsm: osmId !== null },
    stamps,
    visits,
    /** true or false when a position was sent, null when it wasn't. */
    inside: here === null ? null : here <= place.geofenceMeters,
    distanceM: here,
  };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/verification.ts
====================================================================================================

import { assertSocialEnabled } from "@/server/social";
/**
 * The verified badge: handpicked by an admin, no application flow. Every grant and revoke needs a note and is
 * written to the append-only `verification_event` log (a trigger rejects UPDATE and DELETE) in the same transaction
 * as the change.
 *
 * Badge only: verification never changes visibility, order or anything else. Nothing reads
 * `verified_at` except the code that displays the badge.
 */
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { conflict, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertAdmin } from "@/server/permissions";

/** Serializes badge changes for one account (the admin tools and account deletion). */
export async function lockVerification(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, userId: string) {
  await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`verify:${userId}`}))`;
}

export const verificationSchema = z
  .object({
    verified: z.boolean(),
    note: z.string().trim().min(3, "Add a note (why)").max(300, "Note: 300 characters max"),
  })
  .strict();

export async function setVerified(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  const admin = assertAdmin(actor);
  const { verified, note } = verificationSchema.parse(raw);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true } });
  if (!user) throw notFound("User not found");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Same lock as account deletion, and re-read under it: a grant can't land on an account being deleted.
    await lockVerification(tx, user.id);
    const current = await tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: user.id }, select: { verifiedAt: true, deletedAt: true } });
    if (current.deletedAt) throw notFound("User not found");
    if ((current.verifiedAt !== null) === verified) throw conflict(verified ? "Already verified" : "Not verified");
    // The log entry first: the database refuses a badge change without one in the same transaction.
    await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: user.id, action: verified ? "GRANT" : "REVOKE", note, actorId: admin.userId, createdAt: now } });
    const updated = await tx.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { verifiedAt: verified ? now : null }, select: { handle: true, verifiedAt: true } });
    return { handle: updated.handle, verified: updated.verifiedAt !== null, verifiedAt: updated.verifiedAt };
  });
}

/** Admin user search for the verification tools. Staff see real accounts (email included), private or not. */
export async function adminSearchUsers(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, q: string) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  assertAdmin(actor);
  const query = q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^@/, "");
  if (query.length < 2) return [];
  const users = await db.user.findMany({
    where: {
      deletedAt: null,
      OR: [{ handle: { contains: query } }, { displayName: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } }, { email: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } }],
    },
    orderBy: { handle: "asc" },
    take: 25,
    select: { handle: true, displayName: true, email: true, role: true, isPrivate: true, verifiedAt: true, suspendedAt: true, createdAt: true },
  });
  return users.map((u) => ({
    handle: u.handle, displayName: u.displayName, email: u.email, role: u.role, isPrivate: u.isPrivate,
    verified: u.verifiedAt !== null, verifiedAt: u.verifiedAt, suspended: u.suspendedAt !== null, memberSince: u.createdAt,
  }));
}

/** The verification log, newest first (optionally for one account). */
export async function verificationLog(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle?: string | null, take = 100) {
  assertSocialEnabled();
  assertAdmin(actor);
  const user = handle ? await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true } }) : null;
  if (handle && !user) throw notFound("User not found");
  const rows = await db.verificationEvent.findMany({
    where: user ? { userId: user.id } : {},
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    take,
    include: { user: { select: { handle: true } }, actor: { select: { handle: true } } },
  });
  return rows.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, handle: e.user.handle, action: e.action, note: e.note, by: e.actor?.handle ?? "system", at: e.createdAt }));
}

== src/server/social.ts

import { notFound } from "@/server/errors";

/**
 * SOCIAL_ENABLED (default off). Nostos v1 is strictly personal: no feed, follows, likes, sharing, activity or blocks, and
 * every passport is visible only to its owner. With the flag on, the social layer works as built in session 1.
 * The flag is read on every call so tests can switch it.
 */
export function socialEnabled(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): boolean {
  return ["1", "true", "yes"].includes((env.SOCIAL_ENABLED ?? "").trim().toLowerCase());
}

/** Moderation is part of the social layer: legacy reports never expose personal passports. */
export function assertSocialEnabled() {
  if (!socialEnabled()) throw notFound();
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/age.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The 21+ age gate. A birthdate is read once to answer "is this person 21 or older today?" and is never stored: there is
 * no birthdate column anywhere, and a refused attempt writes nothing at all (no account, no email, no log row).
 * "Today" is the calendar date in New York, where the app operates.
 */
import { localParts } from "@/lib/time";

export const MIN_AGE = 21;

export type YMD = { y: number; m: number; d: number };

/** A real calendar date written YYYY-MM-DD, or null. "2001-02-29" and "2001-13-01" are not dates. */
export function parseBirthdate(input: string): YMD | null {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(input);
  if (!m) return null;
  const [y, mo, d] = [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])];
  const probe = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  if (probe.getUTCFullYear() !== y || probe.getUTCMonth() !== mo - 1 || probe.getUTCDate() !== d) return null;
  return { y, m: mo, d };
}

/** Whole years between a birthdate and a day. A birthday counts from its own date. */
export function ageOn(birth: YMD, today: YMD): number {
  const had = today.m > birth.m || (today.m === birth.m && today.d >= birth.d);
  return today.y - birth.y - (had ? 0 : 1);
}

export function todayInNewYork(now: Date): YMD {
  const p = localParts(now);
  return { y: p.year, m: p.month, d: p.day };
}

/** True when the birthdate is a real date in the past and the person is at least 21 today. */
export function isOldEnough(birthdate: string, now = new Date()): boolean {
  const birth = parseBirthdate(birthdate);
  if (!birth) return false;
  const today = todayInNewYork(now);
  const age = ageOn(birth, today);
  return age >= MIN_AGE && age < 130;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/api-client.ts
====================================================================================================

/** Small fetch wrapper for client components. Throws an Error carrying the server's message. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(public status: number, public code: string, message: string) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export async function api<T = unknown>(url: string, init?: RequestInit & { json?: unknown }): Promise<T> {
  const { json, ...rest } = init ?? {};
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(url, {
      ...rest,
      headers: json !== undefined ? { "content-type": "application/json", ...(rest.headers ?? {}) } : rest.headers,
      body: json !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(json) : rest.body,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if ((err as Error).name === "AbortError") throw err;
    throw new ApiError(0, "network", "Can't reach Nostos right now. Check your connection and try again.");
  }
  const text = await res.text();
  let data: unknown = null;
  try {
    data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
  } catch {
    /* non-JSON error page */
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    const d = (data ?? {}) as { error?: string; message?: string };
    throw new ApiError(res.status, d.error ?? "error", d.message ?? `Request failed (${res.status})`);
  }
  return data as T;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/auth/password.ts
====================================================================================================

import { randomBytes, scrypt as scryptCb, timingSafeEqual, type ScryptOptions } from "node:crypto";

// scrypt parameters. N=2^15, r=8, p=1 is the OWASP-recommended floor for interactive logins (~32 MiB, ~50ms).
export const SCRYPT_PARAMS = { N: 32768, r: 8, p: 1, keyLen: 64, saltLen: 16 } as const;
const MAXMEM = 64 * 1024 * 1024;

function scrypt(password: string, salt: Buffer, keyLen: number, opts: ScryptOptions): Promise<Buffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) =>
    scryptCb(password.normalize("NFKC"), salt, keyLen, opts, (err, key) => (err ? reject(err) : resolve(key))),
  );
}

/** Returns "scrypt$N$r$p$saltB64$hashB64" so parameters can be raised later without breaking old hashes. */
export async function hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
  const { N, r, p, keyLen, saltLen } = SCRYPT_PARAMS;
  const salt = randomBytes(saltLen);
  const key = await scrypt(password, salt, keyLen, { N, r, p, maxmem: MAXMEM });
  return ["scrypt", N, r, p, salt.toString("base64"), key.toString("base64")].join("$");
}

export async function verifyPassword(password: string, stored: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const parts = stored.split("$");
  if (parts.length !== 6 || parts[0] !== "scrypt") return false;
  const [, nS, rS, pS, saltB64, hashB64] = parts as [string, string, string, string, string, string];
  const N = Number(nS), r = Number(rS), p = Number(pS);
  if (![N, r, p].every(Number.isSafeInteger)) return false;
  const expected = Buffer.from(hashB64, "base64");
  const actual = await scrypt(password, Buffer.from(saltB64, "base64"), expected.length, { N, r, p, maxmem: MAXMEM });
  return actual.length === expected.length && timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
}

/** A real hash of a random password, used to keep login timing flat when the email doesn't exist. */
let dummyHash: Promise<string> | null = null;
export function getDummyHash(): Promise<string> {
  dummyHash ??= hashPassword(randomBytes(16).toString("hex"));
  return dummyHash;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/auth/session.ts
====================================================================================================

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import type { SessionClient } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";

export const SESSION_COOKIE = "lc_session";
/** Fixed (non-sliding) lifetimes: the cookie (or keychain token) and the DB row expire together. */
export const SESSION_TTL_MS = 30 * DAY_MS;
/** iOS tokens live in the keychain; a longer fixed lifetime avoids monthly forced sign-outs. */
export const IOS_SESSION_TTL_MS = 90 * DAY_MS;

export function hashToken(token: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
}

export async function createSession(db: Db, userId: string, opts: { client?: SessionClient; now?: Date } = {}) {
  const client = opts.client ?? "WEB";
  const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
  const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + (client === "IOS" ? IOS_SESSION_TTL_MS : SESSION_TTL_MS));
  await db.session.create({ data: { id: hashToken(token), userId, client, expiresAt, createdAt: now } });
  return { token, expiresAt, client };
}

/**
 * Resolves a session token to its user. When `client` is given, the session must have been issued for that
 * channel: IOS tokens only work as bearer tokens and WEB tokens only as the cookie, so a leaked 90-day iOS
 * token can't be replayed as a browser cookie (and vice versa).
 */
export async function getSessionUser(
  db: Db,
  token: string | undefined | null,
  opts: { client?: SessionClient; now?: Date } = {},
) {
  const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
  if (!token || token.length > 200) return null;
  const session = await db.session.findUnique({ where: { id: hashToken(token) }, include: { user: true } });
  if (!session) return null;
  if (opts.client && session.client !== opts.client) return null;
  if (session.user.deletedAt || session.user.suspendedAt) return null;
  if (session.expiresAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) {
    await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { id: session.id } });
    return null;
  }
  return session.user;
}

export async function destroySession(db: Db, token: string | undefined | null) {
  if (!token) return;
  await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { id: hashToken(token) } });
}

/** Revokes every session (web and iOS) for a user. Returns how many were revoked. */
export async function destroyAllSessions(db: Db, userId: string) {
  const { count } = await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { userId } });
  return count;
}

export function sessionCookieAttributes(expiresAt: Date) {
  return {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "lax" as const,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    path: "/",
    expires: expiresAt,
  };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/content-filter/index.ts
====================================================================================================

import { SUBSTRING, WHOLE_WORD } from "./wordlist";

const LEET: Record<string, string> = { "0": "o", "1": "i", "!": "i", "|": "i", "3": "e", "4": "a", "@": "a", "5": "s", "$": "s", "7": "t", "+": "t", "8": "b", "9": "g" };

/** Lowercase, strip accents and map leetspeak. */
function normalize(text: string): string {
  return text
    .normalize("NFKD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[0-9!|@$+]/g, (c) => LEET[c] ?? c);
}

/**
 * A term as a pattern of letter runs: each run of k identical letters becomes `x{k,}`. So stretched spellings
 * ("faaaggggot") still match, but a term never matches a *different* word ("coon" won't match "con"/"cons",
 * "nigger" won't match "Nigerian").
 */
function pattern(term: string): string {
  return term.replace(/([a-z])\1*/g, (run, ch: string) => `${ch}{${run.length},}`);
}
const WHOLE_RE = new RegExp(`^(?:${WHOLE_WORD.map(pattern).join("|")})$`);
const SUB_RE = new RegExp(SUBSTRING.map(pattern).join("|"));

/**
 * Letter tokens. Runs of single letters ("f a g", "w h o r e") are spelled-out words, so every contiguous piece
 * of such a run (3+ letters) is also checked; that catches "what a w h o r e" even though "a" joins the run.
 */
function tokens(norm: string): string[] {
  const raw = norm.split(/[^a-z]+/).filter(Boolean);
  const out = [...raw];
  const runs: string[] = [];
  let run = "";
  for (const t of raw) {
    if (t.length === 1) run += t;
    else {
      if (run.length > 1) runs.push(run);
      run = "";
    }
  }
  if (run.length > 1) runs.push(run);
  for (const r of runs) for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i++) for (let j = i + 3; j <= r.length; j++) out.push(r.slice(i, j));
  return out;
}

export function containsObjectionable(text: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!text) return false;
  const toks = tokens(normalize(text));
  return toks.some((t) => WHOLE_RE.test(t) || SUB_RE.test(t));
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/content-filter/wordlist.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Server-side list of slurs and sexually explicit terms that are rejected in user-visible text
 * (App Store Guideline 1.2). Deliberately small and high-precision: ordinary profanity is allowed (this is
 * a nightlife app), hate speech and explicit sexual terms are not. Moderators handle everything else.
 *
 * WHOLE_WORD terms match a complete token after normalization, so "cocktail", "spicy", "raccoon",
 * "Scunthorpe" and "fire retardant" are fine. SUBSTRING terms are long and unambiguous enough to also be
 * caught inside a single run-together token such as a handle ("xx<term>xx"), never across word breaks.
 *
 * Deliberately NOT listed: words with common innocent uses ("cock" as in Cock & Bull, "cum" as in cum laude),
 * and "rape": people must be able to warn others about assault at a venue. Moderators handle abuse of these.
 */
export const WHOLE_WORD: readonly string[] = [
  // racial and ethnic slurs
  "nigger", "niggers", "nigga", "niggas", "chink", "chinks", "gook", "gooks", "spic", "spics", "kike", "kikes",
  "wetback", "wetbacks", "raghead", "ragheads", "towelhead", "towelheads", "coon", "coons", "beaner", "beaners",
  "paki", "pakis", "zipperhead",
  // homophobic / transphobic slurs
  "fag", "fags", "faggot", "faggots", "dyke", "dykes", "tranny", "trannies", "shemale",
  // ableist slurs
  "retard", "retards", "retarded",
  // sexually explicit terms
  "cunt", "cunts", "pussy", "pussies", "blowjob", "blowjobs", "handjob", "handjobs",
  "cumshot", "cumshots", "porn", "porno", "whore", "whores", "slut", "sluts", "dildo", "jizz",
];

export const SUBSTRING: readonly string[] = ["nigger", "nigga", "faggot", "wetback", "towelhead", "raghead", "blowjob", "cumshot"];
