====================================================================================================
== src/lib/geo.ts
====================================================================================================

export const GEOFENCE_METERS = 100;
const EARTH_RADIUS_M = 6_371_008.8;

export type LatLng = { lat: number; lng: number };

/** Great-circle distance in meters (haversine). */
export function distanceMeters(a: LatLng, b: LatLng): number {
  const toRad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLat = toRad(b.lat - a.lat);
  const dLng = toRad(b.lng - a.lng);
  const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(toRad(a.lat)) * Math.cos(toRad(b.lat)) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_M * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)));
}

export function isValidLatLng(p: { lat: unknown; lng: unknown }): p is LatLng {
  return (
    typeof p.lat === "number" &&
    typeof p.lng === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(p.lat) &&
    Number.isFinite(p.lng) &&
    Math.abs(p.lat) <= 90 &&
    Math.abs(p.lng) <= 180
  );
}

/** Inclusive: exactly 100m away counts as inside. */
export function withinGeofence(user: LatLng, venue: LatLng, radius = GEOFENCE_METERS): boolean {
  return distanceMeters(user, venue) <= radius;
}

/** Ray casting: is the point inside the ring? Ring is [lng, lat] pairs (closed or not). */
export function pointInRing(pt: LatLng, ring: readonly (readonly [number, number])[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = ring.length - 1; i < ring.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = ring[i]!;
    const [xj, yj] = ring[j]!;
    if (yi > pt.lat !== yj > pt.lat && pt.lng < ((xj - xi) * (pt.lat - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/layout.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Where a stamp sits in the passport book. One function, used by the server for every stamp response and mirrored
 * (with the same test vectors) in the app, so the page and slot a person sees are the ones the server says.
 *
 * Stamps are numbered oldest first among the owner's live stamps (not hidden, not removed), starting at 0. Three slots to
 * a page, pages from 1, so the oldest stamps stay on page 1 however many come later. A spread is two facing pages.
 */
export const SLOTS_PER_PAGE = 3;
export const PAGES_PER_SPREAD = 2;

export type PageSlot = { page: number; slot: number };

/** `index` is the 0-based position among the owner's live stamps, oldest first. Page and slot both start at 1. */
export function pageAndSlot(index: number): PageSlot {
  if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0) throw new RangeError("index must be a whole number, 0 or more");
  return { page: Math.floor(index / SLOTS_PER_PAGE) + 1, slot: (index % SLOTS_PER_PAGE) + 1 };
}

/** The spread (1-based) a page is on: pages 1 and 2 are spread 1, 3 and 4 are spread 2. */
export function spreadOfPage(page: number): number {
  return Math.ceil(page / PAGES_PER_SPREAD);
}

/** The next empty slot after `count` live stamps: where the next stamp will land. */
export function nextSlot(count: number): PageSlot {
  return pageAndSlot(count);
}

/** Shared test vectors: [index, page, slot]. The app's tests use the same table. */
export const LAYOUT_VECTORS: readonly [number, number, number][] = [
  [0, 1, 1], [1, 1, 2], [2, 1, 3], [3, 2, 1], [5, 2, 3], [6, 3, 1], [11, 4, 3], [12, 5, 1], [29, 10, 3],
];


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/osm.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * OpenStreetMap venue import: pure parsing. Data © OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0.
 * Pulls places worth a stamp via Overpass: bars, restaurants, cafes, theatres, museums, parks and more (see PLACE_KINDS).
 */
export const OSM_ATTRIBUTION = {
  text: "© OpenStreetMap contributors",
  url: "https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright",
  license: "ODbL-1.0",
} as const;

/** South, west, north, east: a superset of both neighborhoods; parseOverpass keeps only points inside them. */
export const EV_LES_BBOX = [40.7115, -73.9945, 40.7345, -73.972] as const;

type Pt = readonly [lat: number, lng: number];
/**
 * Neighborhood outlines (approximate, drawn from street intersections, checked against a live Overpass pull):
 * - East Village: 14th St (both sides) to Houston St; the Bowery frontage, Cooper Square and 4th Ave (north of Astor Pl)
 *   to Avenue D. Lafayette St, Broadway and University Pl (NoHo / the Village) are outside.
 * - Lower East Side: Houston St to Canal St / East Broadway; the Bowery frontage (incl. Chrystie and Forsyth) to the FDR.
 *   Chinatown / Two Bridges (south-west of Canal & East Broadway) is outside.
 * Houston St is a polyline through its real intersections because it bends.
 */
const HOUSTON: readonly Pt[] = [
  [40.7248, -73.9932], // Bowery (west side)
  [40.7236, -73.9904], // 2nd Ave
  [40.7224, -73.9868], // Avenue A
  [40.7213, -73.9837], // Avenue B
  [40.7203, -73.9808], // Avenue C
  [40.7193, -73.9781], // Avenue D
  [40.7185, -73.975], // FDR Drive
];
export const EAST_VILLAGE: readonly Pt[] = [
  [40.7351, -73.9901], // 14th St (north frontage) & 4th Ave
  [40.7317, -73.9818], // 14th St (north frontage) & 1st Ave
  [40.7297, -73.976], // 14th St (north frontage) & Avenue C/D
  ...[...HOUSTON].reverse(), // Houston St, east to west
  [40.7274, -73.9928], // Bowery (west side) & E 4th St
  [40.7299, -73.9908], // Astor Place / Cooper Square
  [40.7351, -73.9903], // 4th Ave up to 14th St
];
export const LOWER_EAST_SIDE: readonly Pt[] = [
  ...HOUSTON, // Houston St, west to east
  [40.7131, -73.9776], // Grand St & FDR
  [40.7133, -73.9895], // East Broadway & Rutgers St
  [40.7166, -73.9962], // Canal St & Bowery (west side)
];

/** Ray-casting point-in-polygon on lat/lng (fine at neighborhood scale). */
export function inPolygon([lat, lng]: Pt, poly: readonly Pt[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [yi, xi] = poly[i]!;
    const [yj, xj] = poly[j]!;
    if (yi > lat !== yj > lat && lng < ((xj - xi) * (lat - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

export function neighborhoodOf(lat: number, lng: number): "East Village" | "Lower East Side" | null {
  if (inPolygon([lat, lng], EAST_VILLAGE)) return "East Village";
  if (inPolygon([lat, lng], LOWER_EAST_SIDE)) return "Lower East Side";
  return null;
}

/** What counts as a place: OSM key and the values kept. Bars and clubs are no longer the only kind (Nostos is about anywhere). */
export const PLACE_KINDS = {
  amenity: ["bar", "pub", "nightclub", "restaurant", "cafe", "theatre", "cinema", "arts_centre", "library"],
  leisure: ["park", "garden", "playground"],
  tourism: ["museum", "gallery", "attraction", "viewpoint"],
  shop: ["books"],
} as const;

/** The default import area: Manhattan from the Battery up to 59th St. Pass another bbox to the importer for more. */
export const DEFAULT_BBOX = [40.7, -74.02, 40.77, -73.93] as const;

export function overpassQuery(bbox: readonly [number, number, number, number] = EV_LES_BBOX) {
  const b = bbox.join(",");
  const parts = Object.entries(PLACE_KINDS).map(([key, values]) => `nwr["${key}"~"^(${values.join("|")})$"]["name"](${b});`);
  return `[out:json][timeout:180];(${parts.join("")});out center tags;`;
}

/** The kind of place an element is (its OSM value), or null if it isn't one we keep. */
export function placeKind(tags: Record<string, string>): string | null {
  for (const [key, values] of Object.entries(PLACE_KINDS)) {
    const v = tags[key];
    if (v && (values as readonly string[]).includes(v)) return v;
  }
  return null;
}

export type OverpassElement = {
  type: "node" | "way" | "relation";
  id: number;
  lat?: number;
  lon?: number;
  center?: { lat: number; lon: number };
  tags?: Record<string, string>;
};

export type OsmVenue = { osmId: string; name: string; address: string; neighborhood: string; neighborhoodId?: string; lat: number; lng: number; kind: string };
/** Finds the neighborhood containing a point (from the neighborhoods table). Without one, the old East Village and Lower East Side outlines are used. */
export type Locate = (lat: number, lng: number) => { id: string; name: string } | null;

export function slugify(name: string): string {
  return (
    name
      .normalize("NFKD")
      .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/&/g, " and ")
      .replace(/['’]/g, "")
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
      .slice(0, 60) || "venue"
  );
}

const M_PER_DEG = 111_195;
function closeTogether(a: { lat: number; lng: number }, b: { lat: number; lng: number }, meters: number) {
  const dLat = (a.lat - b.lat) * M_PER_DEG;
  const dLng = (a.lng - b.lng) * M_PER_DEG * Math.cos((a.lat * Math.PI) / 180);
  return Math.hypot(dLat, dLng) <= meters;
}
const normName = (n: string) => slugify(n).replace(/-/g, "");

/**
 * Parses Overpass elements into venues: named bars/pubs/nightclubs with coordinates inside the East Village or
 * Lower East Side outlines. OSM sometimes maps one venue twice (a node and a building way): elements with the
 * same normalized name within 60 m collapse to one, chosen deterministically (has a street address, then node
 * over way over relation, then lowest id). Addresses come from addr:* tags; when absent the address is "".
 * Sorted by osmId.
 */
export function parseOverpass(elements: readonly OverpassElement[], locate?: Locate): OsmVenue[] {
  const candidates: (OsmVenue & { rank: [number, number, number] })[] = [];
  for (const el of elements) {
    const tags = el.tags ?? {};
    const name = (tags.name ?? "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const lat = el.lat ?? el.center?.lat;
    const lng = el.lon ?? el.center?.lon;
    if (!name || lat == null || lng == null) continue;
    const kind = placeKind(tags);
    if (!kind) continue;
    const hood = locate ? locate(lat, lng) : null;
    const neighborhood = locate ? hood?.name : neighborhoodOf(lat, lng);
    if (!neighborhood) continue;
    const street = [tags["addr:housenumber"], tags["addr:street"]].filter(Boolean).join(" ").trim().slice(0, 120);
    candidates.push({
      osmId: `${el.type}/${el.id}`, name: name.slice(0, 80), address: street, neighborhood, ...(hood ? { neighborhoodId: hood.id } : {}), lat, lng, kind,
      rank: [street ? 0 : 1, el.type === "node" ? 0 : el.type === "way" ? 1 : 2, el.id],
    });
  }
  candidates.sort((a, b) => a.rank[0] - b.rank[0] || a.rank[1] - b.rank[1] || a.rank[2] - b.rank[2]);
  const kept: typeof candidates = [];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (!kept.some((k) => normName(k.name) === normName(c.name) && closeTogether(k, c, 60))) kept.push(c);
  }
  return kept
    .map((c): OsmVenue => ({ osmId: c.osmId, name: c.name, address: c.address, neighborhood: c.neighborhood, ...(c.neighborhoodId ? { neighborhoodId: c.neighborhoodId } : {}), lat: c.lat, lng: c.lng, kind: c.kind }))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.osmId < b.osmId ? -1 : a.osmId > b.osmId ? 1 : 0));
}

/**
 * Deterministic slugs from the OSM data itself: the name's slug if no other venue in the import shares it, else
 * `<slug>-<n|w|r><id>` for every venue with that name. `reserved` holds slugs owned by non-OSM venues (demo or
 * manually created), which also force the suffix. Already-imported venues keep their slug forever (see import).
 */
export function assignSlugs(venues: readonly OsmVenue[], reserved: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set()): Map<string, string> {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const v of venues) counts.set(slugify(v.name), (counts.get(slugify(v.name)) ?? 0) + 1);
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const v of venues) {
    const base = slugify(v.name);
    const [type, id] = v.osmId.split("/") as [string, string];
    out.set(v.osmId, counts.get(base)! > 1 || reserved.has(base) ? `${base}-${type[0]}${id}` : base);
  }
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/rng.ts
====================================================================================================

/** Deterministic PRNG (mulberry32). Same seed -> same sequence, used by the seed script. */
export function mulberry32(seed: number) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  const next = () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
  return {
    next,
    int: (min: number, max: number) => min + Math.floor(next() * (max - min + 1)),
    pick: <T>(arr: readonly T[]): T => arr[Math.floor(next() * arr.length)]!,
    chance: (p: number) => next() < p,
    shuffle: <T>(arr: readonly T[]): T[] => {
      const out = [...arr];
      for (let i = out.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(next() * (i + 1));
        [out[i], out[j]] = [out[j]!, out[i]!];
      }
      return out;
    },
    weighted: <K extends string>(weights: Partial<Record<K, number>>): K => {
      const entries = Object.entries(weights) as [K, number][];
      const total = entries.reduce((s, [, w]) => s + w, 0);
      let r = next() * total;
      for (const [k, w] of entries) {
        r -= w;
        if (r <= 0) return k;
      }
      return entries[entries.length - 1]![0];
    },
  };
}
export type Rng = ReturnType<typeof mulberry32>;


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/storage/index.ts
====================================================================================================

import { createHmac, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { GetObjectCommand, PutObjectCommand, DeleteObjectCommand, S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";

/**
 * Storage for uploaded images. Photos are never public: clients only ever get short-lived signed GET URLs,
 * issued by the service layer for content the viewer is allowed to see.
 *
 * - LocalDiskStorage (development/tests): files under UPLOAD_DIR, served by /api/uploads/<key>?exp=&sig=
 *   with an HMAC signature (UPLOAD_URL_SECRET).
 * - R2Storage (preview/production): Cloudflare R2 via its S3-compatible API, presigned GET URLs.
 * Selected by STORAGE_DRIVER=local|r2.
 */
export interface BlobStorage {
  put(key: string, data: Buffer, contentType: string): Promise<void>;
  get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null>;
  delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
  /** A GET URL valid for at least `minValidSeconds`. */
  signedUrl(key: string, now?: Date): Promise<string>;
}

/** Signed URLs are valid for at least 15 minutes. */
export const SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S = 15 * 60;
/**
 * Signing time is floored to 5-minute buckets and the TTL is 20 minutes, so every URL is valid for 15 to 20
 * minutes. A photo keeps the same URL within a bucket, which lets browsers and the iOS image cache reuse it
 * across the 60-second refreshes.
 */
const BUCKET_MS = 5 * 60_000;
export const SIGNED_URL_TTL_S = SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S + BUCKET_MS / 1000;
export function signingInstant(now: Date): Date {
  return new Date(Math.floor(now.getTime() / BUCKET_MS) * BUCKET_MS);
}

const KEY_RE = /^[a-z0-9/_-]+\.(jpg|png|webp)$/;
export function isValidKey(key: string) {
  return KEY_RE.test(key) && !key.includes("..");
}

function uploadSecret(): string {
  const s = process.env.UPLOAD_URL_SECRET;
  if (s && s.length >= 32) return s;
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") throw new Error("UPLOAD_URL_SECRET (>= 32 chars) is required in production");
  return "dev-only-upload-url-secret-not-for-production-use";
}

function localSignature(key: string, exp: number) {
  return createHmac("sha256", uploadSecret()).update(`${key}\n${exp}`).digest("base64url");
}

/** Verifies a local signed URL's exp/sig. */
export function verifyLocalSignature(key: string, exp: string | null, sig: string | null, now = new Date()): boolean {
  if (!exp || !sig || !/^\d{10}$/.test(exp)) return false;
  if (Number(exp) * 1000 <= now.getTime()) return false;
  const expected = Buffer.from(localSignature(key, Number(exp)));
  const given = Buffer.from(sig);
  return expected.length === given.length && timingSafeEqual(expected, given);
}

export class LocalDiskStorage implements BlobStorage {
  constructor(private readonly root: string) {}

  private resolve(key: string): string {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    const full = path.resolve(this.root, key);
    if (!full.startsWith(path.resolve(this.root) + path.sep)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    return full;
  }

  async put(key: string, data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    const full = this.resolve(key);
    await mkdir(path.dirname(full), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(full, data);
  }

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await rm(this.resolve(key), { force: true });
  }

  async get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
    try {
      return await readFile(this.resolve(key));
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async signedUrl(key: string, now = new Date()): Promise<string> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    const exp = Math.floor(signingInstant(now).getTime() / 1000) + SIGNED_URL_TTL_S;
    return `/api/uploads/${key}?exp=${exp}&sig=${localSignature(key, exp)}`;
  }
}

export type R2Config = { accountId: string; accessKeyId: string; secretAccessKey: string; bucket: string };

export class R2Storage implements BlobStorage {
  readonly client: S3Client;
  constructor(private readonly cfg: R2Config, client?: S3Client) {
    this.client =
      client ??
      new S3Client({
        region: "auto",
        endpoint: `https://${cfg.accountId}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
        credentials: { accessKeyId: cfg.accessKeyId, secretAccessKey: cfg.secretAccessKey },
      });
  }

  async put(key: string, data: Buffer, contentType: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    await this.client.send(
      new PutObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key, Body: data, ContentType: contentType, CacheControl: "private, max-age=900" }),
    );
  }

  async get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
    try {
      const res = await this.client.send(new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }));
      return res.Body ? Buffer.from(await res.Body.transformToByteArray()) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.client.send(new DeleteObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }));
  }

  async signedUrl(key: string, now = new Date()): Promise<string> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    return getSignedUrl(this.client, new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }), {
      expiresIn: SIGNED_URL_TTL_S,
      signingDate: signingInstant(now),
    });
  }
}

export function storageFromEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): BlobStorage {
  const driver = (env.STORAGE_DRIVER ?? "local").toLowerCase();
  if (driver === "r2") {
    const cfg = {
      accountId: env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID ?? "",
      accessKeyId: env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID ?? "",
      secretAccessKey: env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY ?? "",
      bucket: env.R2_BUCKET ?? "",
    };
    const names: Record<keyof R2Config, string> = { accountId: "R2_ACCOUNT_ID", accessKeyId: "R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID", secretAccessKey: "R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY", bucket: "R2_BUCKET" };
    const missing = (Object.keys(cfg) as (keyof R2Config)[]).filter((k) => !cfg[k]).map((k) => names[k]);
    if (missing.length) throw new Error(`STORAGE_DRIVER=r2 but missing: ${missing.join(", ")}`);
    return new R2Storage(cfg);
  }
  if (driver !== "local") throw new Error(`Unknown STORAGE_DRIVER: ${driver}`);
  if (env.VERCEL === "1") throw new Error("STORAGE_DRIVER=local can't work on Vercel (read-only, ephemeral disk). Use r2.");
  return new LocalDiskStorage(path.resolve(process.cwd(), env.UPLOAD_DIR ?? "uploads"));
}

let storage: BlobStorage | null = null;
export function getStorage(): BlobStorage {
  storage ??= storageFromEnv();
  return storage;
}
export function setStorageForTests(s: BlobStorage) {
  storage = s;
}

export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
export type ImageKind = { mime: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp"; ext: "jpg" | "png" | "webp" };

/** Detect image type from magic bytes. The client-declared Content-Type is never trusted. */
export function sniffImage(buf: Buffer): ImageKind | null {
  if (buf.length >= 3 && buf[0] === 0xff && buf[1] === 0xd8 && buf[2] === 0xff) return { mime: "image/jpeg", ext: "jpg" };
  if (buf.length >= 8 && buf.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a])))
    return { mime: "image/png", ext: "png" };
  if (buf.length >= 12 && buf.toString("ascii", 0, 4) === "RIFF" && buf.toString("ascii", 8, 12) === "WEBP")
    return { mime: "image/webp", ext: "webp" };
  return null;
}

export function newStorageKey(prefix: "photos" | "official" | "avatars", ext: ImageKind["ext"]): string {
  return `${prefix}/${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}.${ext}`;
}

export function mimeForKey(key: string): string {
  if (key.endsWith(".png")) return "image/png";
  if (key.endsWith(".webp")) return "image/webp";
  return "image/jpeg";
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/time.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Time conventions: everything is stored in UTC. "Tonight" is computed in America/New_York,
 * where a night runs from 5:00am local to 4:59:59.999am local the next day. A night is named by the
 * local calendar date it starts on, e.g. Friday night 11pm and Saturday 2am are both night "YYYY-MM-DD" of Friday.
 */
export const NIGHT_TZ = "America/New_York";
export const NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR = 5;

export const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
export const HOUR_MS = 60 * MINUTE_MS;
export const DAY_MS = 24 * HOUR_MS;

type LocalParts = { year: number; month: number; day: number; hour: number; minute: number; second: number };

const partsFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
  timeZone: NIGHT_TZ,
  hourCycle: "h23",
  year: "numeric",
  month: "2-digit",
  day: "2-digit",
  hour: "2-digit",
  minute: "2-digit",
  second: "2-digit",
});

/** Wall-clock components of an instant in America/New_York. */
export function localParts(date: Date): LocalParts {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const p of partsFormatter.formatToParts(date)) {
    if (p.type !== "literal") out[p.type] = Number(p.value);
  }
  return {
    year: out.year!,
    month: out.month!,
    day: out.day!,
    hour: out.hour!,
    minute: out.minute!,
    second: out.second!,
  };
}

function pad(n: number) {
  return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
}

/** The night an instant belongs to, as YYYY-MM-DD (local date the night started on). */
export function nightKey(date: Date): string {
  const p = localParts(date);
  // Use a UTC-date object purely for calendar arithmetic on the local wall date.
  const cal = new Date(Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day));
  if (p.hour < NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR) cal.setUTCDate(cal.getUTCDate() - 1);
  return `${cal.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad(cal.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(cal.getUTCDate())}`;
}

/** Calendar arithmetic on a night key (YYYY-MM-DD), independent of daylight saving time. */
export function addNights(key: string, days: number): string {
  const [y, m, d] = key.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + days));
  return `${t.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(t.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(t.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/** Offset of America/New_York from UTC at an instant, in ms (e.g. -4h during EDT). */
function tzOffsetMs(date: Date): number {
  const p = localParts(date);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute, p.second);
  return asUtc - Math.floor(date.getTime() / 1000) * 1000;
}

/** UTC instant of a local America/New_York wall-clock time (resolves DST gaps forward). */
export function zonedTimeToUtc(year: number, month: number, day: number, hour: number, minute = 0): Date {
  const guess = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, hour, minute);
  // Two passes handle the DST boundary correctly.
  let ts = guess - tzOffsetMs(new Date(guess));
  ts = guess - tzOffsetMs(new Date(ts));
  return new Date(ts);
}

/** [start, end) of a night in UTC. */
export function nightBounds(key: string): { start: Date; end: Date } {
  const [y, m, d] = key.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const start = zonedTimeToUtc(y, m, d, NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR);
  const next = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + 1));
  const end = zonedTimeToUtc(next.getUTCFullYear(), next.getUTCMonth() + 1, next.getUTCDate(), NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR);
  return { start, end };
}

/** Human-friendly "12 min ago" style age. */
export function formatAge(from: Date, now: Date): string {
  const mins = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now.getTime() - from.getTime()) / MINUTE_MS));
  if (mins < 1) return "just now";
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min ago`;
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  if (hours < 24) return `${hours} hr ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)} d ago`;
}

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

/** Dev-only pages (seeded-account login) return a hard 404 outside development. */
export function middleware(req: NextRequest) {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "development" && req.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith("/dev")) {
    return new NextResponse("Not found", { status: 404 });
  }
  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = { matcher: ["/dev/:path*"] };

  assertSeedAllowed(process.env);

====================================================================================================
== prisma/placeholder-image.ts
====================================================================================================

// Generates simple deterministic "nightlife" placeholder PNGs for seed photos.
import { PNG } from "pngjs";
import type { Rng } from "../src/lib/rng";

function hsl(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
  const k = (n: number) => (n + h / 30) % 12;
  const a = s * Math.min(l, 1 - l);
  const f = (n: number) => l - a * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k(n) - 3, Math.min(9 - k(n), 1)));
  return [Math.round(f(0) * 255), Math.round(f(8) * 255), Math.round(f(4) * 255)];
}

export function placeholderPng(rng: Rng, width = 240, height = 300): Buffer {
  const png = new PNG({ width, height });
  const hue = rng.int(0, 359);
  const lights = Array.from({ length: rng.int(4, 9) }, () => ({
    x: rng.int(0, width), y: rng.int(0, Math.floor(height * 0.6)), r: rng.int(12, 45), hue: (hue + rng.int(-60, 60) + 360) % 360,
  }));
  const crowdLine = Math.floor(height * (0.62 + rng.next() * 0.1));
  for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
      const t = y / height;
      let [r, g, b] = hsl(hue, 0.55, 0.08 + 0.12 * (1 - t));
      for (const l of lights) {
        const d = Math.hypot(x - l.x, y - l.y);
        if (d < l.r * 2.2) {
          const glow = Math.max(0, 1 - d / (l.r * 2.2)) ** 2;
          const [lr, lg, lb] = hsl(l.hue, 0.9, 0.6);
          r += lr * glow; g += lg * glow; b += lb * glow;
        }
      }
      // silhouettes of a crowd along the bottom
      const bump = Math.sin(x / 9) * 6 + Math.sin(x / 23 + hue) * 10;
      if (y > crowdLine + bump) { r *= 0.15; g *= 0.15; b *= 0.18; }
      const i = (width * y + x) << 2;
      png.data[i] = Math.min(255, r);
      png.data[i + 1] = Math.min(255, g);
      png.data[i + 2] = Math.min(255, b);
      png.data[i + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  return PNG.sync.write(png);
}


====================================================================================================
== prisma/seed-data.ts
====================================================================================================

// Static seed fixtures for places. Names are fictional; coordinates sit in the East Village and Lower East Side.

export type PlaceFixture = { slug: string; name: string; address: string; neighborhood: string; lat: number; lng: number };

====================================================================================================
== prisma/seed-guard.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The demo seed (and `db:reset`, which drops the database first) wipes every table and inserts fictional
 * venues and people. It must never run against production. It is allowed only when:
 *   - APP_ENV is "development" or "preview" (explicitly), and VERCEL_ENV is not "production"; or
 *   - APP_ENV is unset AND the database host is local (localhost / 127.0.0.1 / docker "db").
 * A missing APP_ENV against a remote database is refused, so a forgotten variable fails safe.
 */
export function assertSeedAllowed(env: Record<string, string | undefined>) {
  const appEnv = env.APP_ENV?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const refuse = (why: string) => {
    throw new Error(`Refusing to run the demo seed: ${why}. It wipes all tables and inserts fake data. Use \`pnpm venues:import\` for real venues.`);
  };
  if (appEnv === "production") refuse("APP_ENV=production");
  if (env.VERCEL_ENV === "production") refuse("VERCEL_ENV=production");
  if (appEnv === "preview") {
    // Preview is a public URL: seeded accounts (incl. admin/moderator) must not use the well-known dev password.
    const pw = env.DEV_SEED_PASSWORD ?? "";
    if (pw.length < 16 || pw === "lastcall-dev") refuse("APP_ENV=preview needs a strong DEV_SEED_PASSWORD (16+ chars, not the dev default)");
    if (!env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD || env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD.length < 12) refuse("APP_ENV=preview needs APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD (12+ chars) for the App Review account");
    return;
  }
  if (appEnv === "development") return;
  if (appEnv) refuse(`unknown APP_ENV "${appEnv}"`);
  let host = "";
  try {
    host = new URL(env.DIRECT_URL ?? env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").hostname;
  } catch {
    refuse("DATABASE_URL is missing or invalid");
  }
  if (!["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1", "db"].includes(host)) refuse(`APP_ENV is not set and the database (${host}) is not local`);
}


====================================================================================================
== prisma/seed.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Deterministic seed: `pnpm db:seed` (or `pnpm db:reset`, which runs it after migrating).
 *
 * Determinism: all content comes from a fixed-seed PRNG, ids are assigned explicitly and every timestamp is derived
 * from an anchor time (SEED_NOW, or the current minute), so the data is "recent" whenever you run it. With a pinned
 * SEED_NOW every row is identical across runs; the only difference is the random salt inside the shared dev password hash.
 *
 * What it makes: public and private accounts, pending follow requests, follows, stamps (photo + caption + song, photo
 * only, caption only, song only) at several places, likes, one verified account, one blocked pair, one hidden stamp, one
 * open report for the moderation queue, and an App Review stamp that only that account can see.
 */
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { PrismaClient, type Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { hashPassword } from "../src/lib/auth/password";
import { mulberry32 } from "../src/lib/rng";
import { DAY_MS, HOUR_MS, MINUTE_MS } from "../src/lib/time";
import { storageFromEnv, type BlobStorage } from "../src/lib/storage";
import { CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION } from "../src/server/services/auth";
import { assignNeighborhoods, importNeighborhoods } from "../src/server/services/neighborhoods";
import { placeholderPng } from "./placeholder-image";
import { assertSeedAllowed } from "./seed-guard";
import { CAPTIONS, PEOPLE, PLACES, SONGS } from "./seed-data";

const TABLES = ["rate_event", "verification_event", "photo_like", "follow_request", "block", "login_attempt", "moderation_event", "flag", "follow", "pinned_stamp", "stamp_photo", "photo", "venue", "session", "app_user"];

export type SeedOptions = { now?: Date; storage?: BlobStorage; env?: Record<string, string | undefined> };
export type SeedSummary = { places: number; people: number; stamps: number; likes: number; follows: number; requests: number };

const uid = (handle: string) => `seed_u_${handle}`;

export async function seed(db: PrismaClient, opts: SeedOptions = {}): Promise<SeedSummary> {
  const env = opts.env ?? process.env;
  assertSeedAllowed(env);
  const NOW = opts.now ?? (env.SEED_NOW ? new Date(env.SEED_NOW) : new Date(Math.floor(Date.now() / MINUTE_MS) * MINUTE_MS));
  const ago = (ms: number) => new Date(NOW.getTime() - ms);
  const rng = mulberry32(20261002);
  const storage = opts.storage ?? storageFromEnv(env as Record<string, string | undefined>);

  await db.$executeRawUnsafe(`TRUNCATE TABLE ${TABLES.map((t) => `"${t}"`).join(", ")} RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`);
  await db.venue.createMany({ data: PLACES.map((p, i) => ({ id: `seed_v_${String(i + 1).padStart(3, "0")}`, ...p })) });
  // Neighborhoods are reference data: load them, then file every place under the one that contains it.
  await importNeighborhoods(db);
  await assignNeighborhoods(db);
  const places = await db.venue.findMany({ orderBy: { id: "asc" } });

  // ---------------- People ----------------
  const password = env.DEV_SEED_PASSWORD ?? "lastcall-dev";
  const hash = await hashPassword(password);
  const reviewHash = env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD ? await hashPassword(env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD) : hash;
  await db.user.createMany({
    data: PEOPLE.map((p) => ({
      id: uid(p.handle), email: `${p.handle}@lastcall.dev`, handle: p.handle, displayName: p.name, passwordHash: p.handle === "appreview" ? reviewHash : hash,
      role: p.role ?? "USER", isPrivate: p.isPrivate, bio: p.bio ?? "", geofenceExempt: p.exempt ?? false,
      createdAt: ago(p.ageDays * DAY_MS), termsAcceptedAt: ago(p.ageDays * DAY_MS), termsVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
    })),
  });
  // The verified badge is only ever set with a log entry in the same transaction (a database trigger insists).
  for (const p of PEOPLE.filter((x) => x.verified)) {
    await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: uid(p.handle), action: "GRANT", note: "Seeded demo account", createdAt: ago(30 * DAY_MS) } });
      await tx.user.update({ where: { id: uid(p.handle) }, data: { verifiedAt: ago(30 * DAY_MS) } });
    });
  }

  // ---------------- Follows, requests, one block ----------------
  const follows: [string, string][] = [
    ["maya_r", "avaa"], ["theo_k", "avaa"], ["kofi_b", "avaa"], ["avaa", "maya_r"], ["avaa", "theo_k"], ["avaa", "nia_s"],
    ["theo_k", "maya_r"], ["theo_k", "nia_s"], ["nia_s", "maya_r"], ["nia_s", "sol_m"], ["sol_m", "maya_r"], ["kofi_b", "maya_r"],
    ["kofi_b", "theo_k"], ["pia_l", "maya_r"], ["pia_l", "nia_s"], ["jules_v", "maya_r"], ["omar_q", "nia_s"], ["maya_r", "theo_k"],
    ["maya_r", "nia_s"], ["appreview", "maya_r"], ["blocked_bea", "maya_r"], ["maya_r", "kofi_b"],
  ];
  await db.follow.createMany({ data: follows.map(([a, b], i) => ({ followerId: uid(a), followeeId: uid(b), createdAt: ago((60 - i) * HOUR_MS) })) });
  // Pending requests to private accounts: avaa has four waiting. A pending request grants nothing.
  const requests: [string, string][] = [["rowan_k", "avaa"], ["jules_v", "avaa"], ["ines_p", "avaa"], ["omar_q", "avaa"], ["moth_hopeful", "kofi_b"], ["theo_k", "pia_l"]];
  await db.followRequest.createMany({ data: requests.map(([a, b], i) => ({ requesterId: uid(a), targetId: uid(b), createdAt: ago((8 - i) * HOUR_MS) })) });
  // One blocked pair: Blake blocked Bea (and any follows between them were removed).
  await db.block.create({ data: { blockerId: uid("blocker_blake"), blockedId: uid("blocked_bea"), createdAt: ago(5 * DAY_MS) } });

  // ---------------- Stamps ----------------
  const visitors = PEOPLE.filter((p) => !["admin", "mod_morgan", "moth_hopeful"].includes(p.handle));
  const stampIds: { id: string; userId: string; hidden: boolean; countable: boolean }[] = [];
  const near = (p: { lat: number; lng: number; geofenceMeters: number }) => ({ lat: p.lat + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.0005, lng: p.lng + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.0006 });
  let n = 0;
  for (const person of visitors) {
    const count = person.handle === "maya_r" ? 14 : person.handle === "appreview" ? 2 : rng.int(3, 8);
    const favorites = rng.shuffle(places).slice(0, rng.int(3, 6));
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const place = rng.chance(0.75) ? rng.pick(favorites) : rng.pick(places);
      // Spread over ~60 days with the most recent ones within the last day, so the feed has fresh stamps.
      const age = i < 2 ? rng.int(10, 20 * 60) * MINUTE_MS : rng.int(1, 60) * DAY_MS + rng.int(0, 23) * HOUR_MS;
      const kind = rng.weighted({ all: 3, photo: 3, caption: 2, song: 1, photoCaption: 3 });
      const hasPhoto = kind === "all" || kind === "photo" || kind === "photoCaption";
      const hasCaption = kind === "all" || kind === "caption" || kind === "photoCaption";
      const hasSong = kind === "all" || kind === "song";
      const id = `seed_s_${String(++n).padStart(4, "0")}`;
      const song = hasSong ? rng.pick(SONGS) : null;
      // 1 to 4 photos on a photo stamp; the first is the stamp's cover. Some get a scrapbook layout.
      const keys: string[] = [];
      if (hasPhoto) {
        const count = rng.weighted({ "1": 5, "2": 3, "3": 2, "4": 1 });
        for (let k = 0; k < Number(count); k++) {
          const key = `photos/seed${String(n).padStart(4, "0")}${k === 0 ? "" : `_${k}`}.png`;
          await storage.put(key, placeholderPng(rng), "image/png");
          keys.push(key);
        }
      }
      const exempt = person.exempt === true;
      const where = exempt ? { lat: place.lat + 0.02, lng: place.lng } : near(place);
      await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.photo.create({
          data: {
            id, userId: uid(person.handle), venueId: place.id, storageKey: keys[0] ?? null, mimeType: keys[0] ? "image/png" : null,
            caption: hasCaption ? rng.pick(CAPTIONS) : "", songTitle: song?.title ?? null, songArtist: song?.artist ?? null,
            lat: where.lat, lng: where.lng, createdAt: ago(age), countable: !exempt,
          },
        });
        for (const [k, key] of keys.entries()) {
          const laid = rng.chance(0.5);
          await tx.stampPhoto.create({
            data: {
              stampId: id, storageKey: key, mimeType: "image/png", position: k, createdAt: ago(age),
              ...(laid ? { x: Math.round(rng.next() * 80) / 100 + 0.1, y: Math.round(rng.next() * 80) / 100 + 0.1, rotation: Math.round((rng.next() - 0.5) * 20) } : {}),
            },
          });
        }
      });
      stampIds.push({ id, userId: uid(person.handle), hidden: false, countable: !exempt });
    }
  }

  // One stamp hidden by a moderator, one the author removed ("delete from profile"), and one open report in the queue.
  const target = stampIds.find((s) => s.userId === uid("theo_k") && s.countable)!;
  const hiddenAt = ago(2 * DAY_MS);
  await db.photo.update({ where: { id: target.id }, data: { hiddenAt } });
  await db.moderationEvent.createMany({
    data: [
      { action: "FLAGGED", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: target.id, actorId: uid("nia_s"), note: "Not at this place", createdAt: ago(3 * DAY_MS) },
      { action: "HIDE", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: target.id, actorId: uid("mod_morgan"), note: "Seeded example of a hidden stamp", createdAt: hiddenAt },
    ],
  });
  await db.flag.create({ data: { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: target.id, flaggerId: uid("nia_s"), reason: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE", status: "RESOLVED", createdAt: ago(3 * DAY_MS), venueId: null } });
  stampIds.find((s) => s.id === target.id)!.hidden = true;
  const removable = stampIds.find((s) => s.userId === uid("sol_m") && s.countable)!;
  await db.photo.update({ where: { id: removable.id }, data: { removedAt: ago(DAY_MS) } });
  stampIds.find((s) => s.id === removable.id)!.hidden = true;
  const reported = stampIds.find((s) => s.userId === uid("maya_r") && s.countable && !s.hidden)!;
  await db.flag.create({ data: { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: reported.id, flaggerId: uid("jules_v"), reason: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE", createdAt: ago(3 * HOUR_MS) } });
  await db.moderationEvent.create({ data: { action: "FLAGGED", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: reported.id, actorId: uid("jules_v"), note: "Not at this place", createdAt: ago(3 * HOUR_MS) } });

  // ---------------- Likes ----------------
  // Never your own, never on hidden, removed or App Review stamps (the database refuses those). App Review doesn't like.
  const likers = visitors.filter((p) => !p.exempt && p.handle !== "blocked_bea");
  const likeRows = new Map<string, Prisma.PhotoLikeUncheckedCreateInput>();
  for (const s of stampIds.filter((x) => !x.hidden && x.countable)) {
    for (const liker of likers) {
      if (uid(liker.handle) === s.userId || !rng.chance(0.25)) continue;
      likeRows.set(`${liker.handle}:${s.id}`, { userId: uid(liker.handle), photoId: s.id, createdAt: ago(rng.int(1, 40) * HOUR_MS), countable: true });
    }
  }
  // Bea and Blake never like each other's stamps (they are a blocked pair).
  for (const [k, row] of [...likeRows]) {
    const a = row.userId, b = stampIds.find((s) => s.id === row.photoId)!.userId;
    if ((a === uid("blocked_bea") && b === uid("blocker_blake")) || (a === uid("blocker_blake") && b === uid("blocked_bea"))) likeRows.delete(k);
  }
  await db.photoLike.createMany({ data: [...likeRows.values()] });

  return { places: places.length, people: PEOPLE.length, stamps: stampIds.length, likes: likeRows.size, follows: follows.length, requests: requests.length };
}

async function main() {
  const db = new PrismaClient();
  try {
    const s = await seed(db);
    console.log(`[seed] ${s.places} places, ${s.people} people, ${s.stamps} stamps, ${s.likes} likes, ${s.follows} follows, ${s.requests} pending requests`);
    console.log(`[seed] sign in at /dev/login (password: ${process.env.DEV_SEED_PASSWORD ?? "lastcall-dev"}); try @avaa (private, 4 requests waiting), @maya_r (public, verified), @moth_hopeful (follows no one), @mod_morgan, @admin`);
  } finally {
    await db.$disconnect();
  }
}

// Run only as a script (`tsx prisma/seed.ts`), not when the tests import `seed`.
if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) void main();

}


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/age-gate.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { login, signup, UNDERAGE_MESSAGE } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { POST as signupRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/signup/route";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z");
const base = { email: "kid@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "Kid", handle: "kid_user", acceptTerms: true };

/** Row counts of every table in the public schema. */
async function rowCounts() {
  const tables = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ table_name: string }[]>`SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE' AND table_name <> '_prisma_migrations'`;
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const { table_name } of tables) out[table_name] = Number((await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<{ n: bigint }[]>(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM "${table_name}"`))[0]!.n);
  return out;
}

describe("the 21+ age gate at signup", () => {
  it("turns away someone one day short of 21 with 403 underage, and stores nothing at all", async () => {
    const before = await rowCounts();
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: "2005-10-03" }, { now: NOW })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "underage", message: UNDERAGE_MESSAGE });
    expect(await rowCounts()).toEqual(before);
    // Not even a login attempt, a session or a user row for that email or handle.
    expect(await prisma.user.count({ where: { OR: [{ email: base.email }, { handle: base.handle }] } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("the refusal comes before the duplicate check, so it reveals nothing about existing accounts", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: "1990-05-05" }, { now: NOW });
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: "2010-01-01" }, { now: NOW })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "underage" });
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: "1990-05-05" }, { now: NOW })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
  });

  it("lets someone in on their 21st birthday, and keeps no birthdate or age anywhere", async () => {
    const { user } = await signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: "2005-10-02" }, { now: NOW });
    expect(JSON.stringify(user)).not.toMatch(/2005|birth|dob|age/i);
    const cols = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ table_name: string; column_name: string }[]>`SELECT table_name, column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'public'`;
    expect(cols.filter((c) => /birth|dob|age$|^age/i.test(c.column_name)).map((c) => `${c.table_name}.${c.column_name}`)).toEqual([]);
    expect(await login(prisma, { email: base.email, password: base.password })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("requires a real birthdate in YYYY-MM-DD form", async () => {
    for (const bad of [undefined, "", "05/05/1990", "1990-13-01", "1990-02-30", "not a date"]) {
      await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, birthdate: bad }, { now: NOW })).rejects.toThrow();
    }
    expect(await prisma.user.count()).toBe(0);
    // The old checkbox is gone: it is no longer accepted in place of a birthdate.
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, confirm21: true }, { now: NOW })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("over HTTP: an under-21 gets 403 underage and no session cookie or token", async () => {
    const res = await signupRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/signup", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-client": "ios" }, body: JSON.stringify({ ...base, birthdate: "2015-06-01" }) }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: "underage", message: UNDERAGE_MESSAGE });
    expect(res.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.session.count()).toBe(0);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/auth.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { createSession, getSessionUser, hashToken } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { login, signup } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { POST as loginRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/login/route";

describe("auth", () => {
  it("signs up as USER only and stores a scrypt hash, never the password", async () => {
    const { user } = await signup(prisma, { email: "A@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "aaa", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    expect(user.role).toBe("USER");
    expect(user.email).toBe("a@x.dev");
    expect(user.passwordHash).toMatch(/^scrypt\$/);
    expect(user.passwordHash).not.toContain("long enough pw");
    expect(user.reputation).toBe(0.5);
  });

  it("rejects role escalation in the signup payload", async () => {
    await expect(
      signup(prisma, { email: "b@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "B", handle: "bbb", role: "MODERATOR" } as never),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("logs in with the right password and rejects the wrong one", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "C", handle: "ccc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "c@x.dev", password: "nope nope nope" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    const { session } = await login(prisma, { email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" });
    expect((await getSessionUser(prisma, session.token))?.email).toBe("c@x.dev");
  });

  it("throttles repeated failures", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "d@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "D", handle: "ddd", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) await login(prisma, { email: "d@x.dev", password: "wrong" }).catch(() => {});
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "d@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
  });

  it("stores only the token hash and expires sessions", async () => {
    const { user } = await signup(prisma, { email: "e@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "E", handle: "eee", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, user.id);
    const row = await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: hashToken(token) } });
    expect(row).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: token } })).toBeNull();
    const later = new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 86_400_000 + 1000);
    expect(await getSessionUser(prisma, token, { now: later })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("login route sets an httpOnly SameSite=Lax cookie", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "f@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "F", handle: "fff", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const res = await loginRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: "f@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }),
      }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const cookie = res.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "";
    expect(cookie).toMatch(/lc_session=/);
    expect(cookie.toLowerCase()).toContain("httponly");
    expect(cookie.toLowerCase()).toContain("samesite=lax");
  });

  it("rejects cross-origin mutations", async () => {
    const res = await loginRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: "x@x.dev", password: "whatever" }),
      }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });
});

describe("same-origin check", () => {
  const call = (headers: Record<string, string>) =>
    loginRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: "nobody@x.dev", password: "whatever" }),
      }),
      undefined as never,
    );

  it("allows requests without an Origin header (non-browser clients)", async () => {
    expect((await call({ host: "localhost" })).status).toBe(401);
  });
  it("allows same-origin requests, including behind a proxy host header", async () => {
    expect((await call({ origin: "http://localhost:3000", host: "localhost:3000" })).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await call({ origin: "https://app.example", host: "internal:3000", "x-forwarded-host": "app.example" })).status).toBe(401);
  });
  it("rejects a malformed Origin", async () => {
    expect((await call({ origin: "not a url", host: "localhost" })).status).toBe(403);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/backend-audit.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { listActivity } from "@/server/services/activity";
import { blockUser, listBlocks, unblockUser } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { approveAllFollowRequests, decideFollowRequest, follow, getProfile, listConnections, listFollowRequests, listFollowing, removeFollower, searchUsers, unfollow } from "@/server/services/people";
import { createStamp, listFeed, listUserPlaces, removeStampPhoto } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { likeStamp, unlikeStamp } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, INSIDE, makeStamp, makeVenue, pngBytes, verify } from "./factories";

import { adminSearchUsers, setVerified, verificationLog } from "@/server/services/verification";
import { setUserFlags } from "@/server/services/admin";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import * as adminUsers from "@/app/api/admin/users/route";
import * as adminLog from "@/app/api/admin/verification-log/route";
import * as adminFlags from "@/app/api/admin/users/[handle]/route";
import * as adminVerify from "@/app/api/admin/users/[handle]/verification/route";
import * as pins from "@/app/api/me/pins/route";
import { listPins } from "@/server/services/pins";
import { getStamp } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { flagContent, moderate, moderationQueue, recentlyHidden } from "@/server/services/moderation";

installMemoryStorage();
afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllEnvs());

describe("backend visibility audit", () => {
  it("personal-mode service callers cannot bypass disabled social HTTP routes", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const other = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const s = await makeStamp(other, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await prisma.follow.create({ data: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: other.userId } });
    await likeStamp(prisma, me, s.id);
    vi.stubEnv("SOCIAL_ENABLED", "0");
    const calls = [
      () => listFeed(prisma, me), () => listActivity(prisma, me), () => listBlocks(prisma, me),
      () => listFollowRequests(prisma, me), () => listFollowing(prisma, me), () => searchUsers(prisma, me, other.handle),
      () => getProfile(prisma, other.handle, me), () => listUserPlaces(prisma, me, other.handle),
      () => follow(prisma, me, other.handle), () => unfollow(prisma, me, other.handle),
      () => blockUser(prisma, me, other.handle), () => unblockUser(prisma, me, other.handle),
      () => listConnections(prisma, me, other.handle, "followers"), () => removeFollower(prisma, me, other.handle),
      () => decideFollowRequest(prisma, me, other.handle, { approve: true }), () => approveAllFollowRequests(prisma, me),
      () => likeStamp(prisma, me, s.id), () => unlikeStamp(prisma, me, s.id),
    ];
    for (const call of calls) await expect(call()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect((await getProfile(prisma, me.handle, me)).access).toBe("full");
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, other, s.id)).stamp.likes).toEqual({ count: 0, viewerLiked: false });
    expect(await listPins(prisma, me)).toEqual({ stamps: [] });
    expect(await prisma.follow.count()).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.photoLike.count()).toBe(1);
  });

  it("personal-mode admin endpoints cannot disclose handles, emails or legacy verification records", async () => {
    const admin = await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" });
    const other = await makeActor();
    await verify(other.userId);
    const token = (await createSession(prisma, admin.userId, { client: "IOS" })).token;
    vi.stubEnv("SOCIAL_ENABLED", "0");
    const handlers = [
      [adminUsers.GET, "GET", undefined], [adminLog.GET, "GET", undefined],
      [adminFlags.PATCH, "PATCH", { geofenceExempt: true }], [adminVerify.POST, "POST", { verified: false, note: "probing" }],
    ] as const;
    for (const [handler, method, body] of handlers) {
      const res = await handler(new Request(`http://localhost/api/admin?q=${other.handle}`, { method, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "content-type": "application/json" }, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined }), { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: other.handle }) } as never);
      expect(res.status).toBe(404);
      expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: "not_found", message: "Not found" });
    }
    for (const call of [() => adminSearchUsers(prisma, admin, other.handle), () => verificationLog(prisma, admin), () => setVerified(prisma, admin, other.handle, { verified: false, note: "probing" }), () => setUserFlags(prisma, admin, other.handle, { geofenceExempt: true })]) await expect(call()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect(await prisma.verificationEvent.count()).toBe(1);
    expect((await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: other.userId } })).geofenceExempt).toBe(false);
  });

  it("pin replacement already gives identical errors for foreign, hidden, removed and missing stamps", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const other = await makeActor();
    const foreign = await makeStamp(other, (await makeVenue()).id);
    const hidden = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    const removed = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: hidden.id }, data: { hiddenAt: new Date() } });
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: removed.id }, data: { removedAt: new Date() } });
    const token = (await createSession(prisma, me.userId, { client: "IOS" })).token;
    const responses = [];
    for (const id of ["missing", foreign.id, hidden.id, removed.id]) {
      const res = await pins.PUT(new Request("http://localhost/api/me/pins", { method: "PUT", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ stampIds: [id] }) }), undefined as never);
      responses.push({ status: res.status, body: await res.text(), headers: Object.fromEntries(res.headers) });
    }
    expect(responses[0]!.status).toBe(400);
    for (const res of responses.slice(1)) expect(res).toEqual(responses[0]);
  });

  it("place summaries never return a URL for a removed original photo", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const s = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await removeStampPhoto(prisma, me, s.id, s.photos[0]!.id);
    expect((await listUserPlaces(prisma, me, me.handle)).places[0]!.latestStamp.photoUrl).toBeNull();
  });
  it("activity and moderation previews never resurrect a removed photo", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const liker = await makeActor();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const s = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await likeStamp(prisma, liker, s.id);
    await flagContent(prisma, liker, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: s.id, reason: "legacy report" });
    await removeStampPhoto(prisma, me, s.id, s.photos[0]!.id);
    const activity = await listActivity(prisma, me);
    expect(activity.find((x) => x.kind === "like")).toMatchObject({ stamp: { url: null } });
    expect((await moderationQueue(prisma, mod))[0]!.content?.url).toBeUndefined();
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: s.id, action: "HIDE" });
    expect((await recentlyHidden(prisma, mod))[0]!.url).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("place summaries choose the first live photo in the current layout order", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const s = await createStamp(prisma, me, (await makeVenue()).id, { photos: [pngBytes(), pngBytes()], meta: { ...INSIDE, caption: "words" } });
    await removeStampPhoto(prisma, me, s.id, s.photos[0]!.id);
    const summary = (await listUserPlaces(prisma, me, me.handle)).places[0]!.latestStamp;
    expect(summary.photoUrl?.split("?")[0]).toBe(s.photos[1]!.url.split("?")[0]);
  });

});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/compliance.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { createSession, SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { hashPassword } from "@/lib/auth/password";
import { login, signup, CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { deleteAccount } from "@/server/services/account";
import { blockUser, listBlocks, unblockUser } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { follow, getProfile, searchUsers } from "@/server/services/people";
import { flagContent, moderate, moderationQueue } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { getStamp, listUserStamps } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { setUserFlags } from "@/server/services/admin";
import { acceptCurrentTerms, getMe } from "@/server/services/me";
import { loadActor } from "@/server/actor";
import { GET as meRoute } from "@/app/api/me/route";
import { PATCH as adminUserRoute } from "@/app/api/admin/users/[handle]/route";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { actorFor, makeActor, makeStamp, makeUser, makeVenue, OUTSIDE } from "./factories";

const mem = installMemoryStorage();

async function userWithPassword(handle: string, password = "correct horse battery") {
  const u = await makeUser({ handle });
  await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { passwordHash: await hashPassword(password), email: `${handle}@real.dev` } });
  return (await actorFor(u.id))!;
}

describe("account deletion (5.1.1(v)): anonymize, never delete content", () => {
  it("requires the current password", async () => {
    const me = await userWithPassword("delme");
    await expect(deleteAccount(prisma, me, { password: "wrong" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "wrong_password" });
    await expect(deleteAccount(prisma, null, { password: "x" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("scrubs identity, revokes sessions, removes social ties, hides stamps and deletes their image files", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await userWithPassword("leaver");
    const friend = await makeActor();
    const other = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, me, friend.handle);
    await follow(prisma, friend, me.handle);
    await blockUser(prisma, me, other.handle);
    const stamp = await makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "dance floor" });
    const key = (await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).storageKey!;
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, me.userId, { client: "IOS" });

    await deleteAccount(prisma, me, { password: "correct horse battery" });

    const row = await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId } });
    expect(row.deletedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(row.email).toMatch(/^deleted-[a-f0-9]{16}@deleted\.invalid$/);
    expect(row.handle).toMatch(/^deleted-[a-f0-9]{16}$/);
    expect(row.displayName).toBe("Deleted user");
    expect(row.passwordHash).not.toMatch(/^scrypt/);
    expect(await prisma.session.count({ where: { userId: me.userId } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.follow.count({ where: { OR: [{ followerId: me.userId }, { followeeId: me.userId }] } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.block.count({ where: { OR: [{ blockerId: me.userId }, { blockedId: me.userId }] } })).toBe(0);

    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(mem.has(key)).toBe(false);
    expect(await prisma.moderationEvent.findFirst({ where: { targetId: stamp.id, action: "ACCOUNT_DELETED" } })).toMatchObject({ actorId: null });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, friend, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });

    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "leaver@real.dev", password: "correct horse battery" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, "leaver", null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, row.handle, null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(follow(prisma, friend, row.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect(await searchUsers(prisma, friend, "deleted")).toEqual([]);
    expect(await loadActor(prisma, me.userId)).toBeNull();
    const res = await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }), undefined as never);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("a deleted account's stamps stay hidden for good: RESTORE is refused and DISMISS never un-hides", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { passwordHash: await hashPassword("pw pw pw pw") } });
    const visible = await makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "one" });
    const flagged = await makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "two", now: new Date(Date.now() + 16 * 60_000) });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: flagged.id, reason: "fake" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: flagged.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();

    await deleteAccount(prisma, me, { password: "pw pw pw pw" });
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await expect(moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: visible.id, action: "RESTORE" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: flagged.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: flagged.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.moderationEvent.count({ where: { action: "ACCOUNT_DELETED" } })).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("blocking (1.2)", () => {
  it("removes follows both ways and prevents new follows either way", async () => {
    const [a, b] = [await makeActor(), await makeActor()];
    await follow(prisma, a, b.handle);
    await follow(prisma, b, a.handle);
    await blockUser(prisma, a, b.handle);
    expect(await prisma.follow.count({ where: { OR: [{ followerId: a.userId }, { followerId: b.userId }] } })).toBe(0);
    await expect(follow(prisma, a, b.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(follow(prisma, b, a.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
  });

  it("lists and undoes blocks; can't block yourself", async () => {
    const [a, b] = [await makeActor(), await makeActor()];
    await blockUser(prisma, a, b.handle);
    expect((await listBlocks(prisma, a)).map((x) => x.handle)).toEqual([b.handle]);
    await unblockUser(prisma, a, b.handle);
    expect(await listBlocks(prisma, a)).toEqual([]);
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, b.handle, a)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(blockUser(prisma, a, a.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
  });
});

describe("objectionable content filter at every entry point", () => {
  it("rejects slurs and explicit terms in captions, song fields, handles and display names with a clear 400", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const bad = { status: 400, code: "objectionable_content" };
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "porn night" })).rejects.toMatchObject(bad);
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "ok", meta: { songTitle: "total f4gg0t", songArtist: "Someone" } })).rejects.toMatchObject(bad);
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "ok", meta: { songTitle: "Fine", songArtist: "porn star" } })).rejects.toMatchObject(bad);
    const base = { email: "n@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" };
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, handle: "xxniggerxx", displayName: "ok" })).rejects.toMatchObject(bad);
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, handle: "fine_handle", displayName: "Slut Queen" })).rejects.toMatchObject(bad);
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, handle: "fine_handle", displayName: "Cocktail Queen" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("terms acceptance", () => {
  const base = { email: "t@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "T", handle: "terms_t" };
  it("signup requires acceptTerms and a birthdate, and stores the version and time (never the birthdate)", async () => {
    await expect(signup(prisma, base)).rejects.toThrow(/Terms/);
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, acceptTerms: true })).rejects.toThrow(/birthdate/i);
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...base, acceptTerms: "yes", birthdate: "1990-05-05" })).rejects.toThrow();
    const { user } = await signup(prisma, { ...base, acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    expect(user.termsVersion).toBe(CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION);
    expect(user.termsAcceptedAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(Object.keys(user)).not.toContain("dateOfBirth");
  });

  it("/api/me reports acceptance; accounts on an older version can accept the current one", async () => {
    const old = await makeActor();
    expect((await getMe(prisma, old)).terms).toMatchObject({ needsAcceptance: true, currentVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION });
    await expect(acceptCurrentTerms(prisma, old, { version: "1999-01-01" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    await acceptCurrentTerms(prisma, old, { version: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION });
    expect((await getMe(prisma, old)).terms).toMatchObject({ needsAcceptance: false, acceptedVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION });
  });
});

describe("geofence-exempt review accounts", () => {
  async function exempt() {
    const u = await makeUser({ isPrivate: false });
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { geofenceExempt: true } });
    return (await actorFor(u.id))!;
  }

  it("can stamp from anywhere, but only they ever see it: not strangers, not followers, not the feed, not the profile", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const reviewer = await exempt();
    const follower = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, follower, reviewer.handle);
    const s = await makeStamp(reviewer, venue.id, { at: OUTSIDE });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: s.id } })).countable).toBe(false);
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, follower, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, reviewer, s.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, follower, reviewer.handle)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, reviewer, reviewer.handle)).stamps).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("countable is a write-time snapshot: toggling the flag later changes neither past exempt nor past real stamps", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const admin = await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" });
    const reviewer = await exempt();
    const real = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const a = await makeStamp(reviewer, venue.id, { at: OUTSIDE });
    const b = await makeStamp(real, venue.id);
    await setUserFlags(prisma, admin, reviewer.handle, { geofenceExempt: false });
    await setUserFlags(prisma, admin, real.handle, { geofenceExempt: true });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: a.id } })).countable).toBe(false);
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: b.id } })).countable).toBe(true);
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: a.id }, data: { countable: true } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("only an admin can set the flag (service and HTTP)", async () => {
    const target = await makeActor();
    await expect(setUserFlags(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), target.handle, { geofenceExempt: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(setUserFlags(prisma, target, target.handle, { geofenceExempt: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    const admin = await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" });
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, admin.userId);
    const res = await adminUserRoute(
      new Request(`http://localhost/api/admin/users/${target.handle}`, { method: "PATCH", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ geofenceExempt: true }) }),
      { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: target.handle }) },
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect((await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: target.userId } })).geofenceExempt).toBe(true);
    // Not settable through signup or any user-facing payload.
    await expect(signup(prisma, { email: "g@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "G", handle: "ggg", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05", geofenceExempt: true })).rejects.toThrow(/Unrecognized key/);
  });
});

describe("reporting and suspending accounts", () => {
  it("users report a profile by handle; a moderator suspends: signed out, can't sign in, profile gone; then unsuspend", async () => {
    const abuser = await makeActor();
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: abuser.userId }, data: { passwordHash: await hashPassword("abuser password"), email: "abuser@x.dev" } });
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, abuser.userId, { client: "IOS" });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "USER", targetId: abuser.handle, reason: "harassing people" });
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const queue = await moderationQueue(prisma, mod);
    expect(queue[0]).toMatchObject({ targetType: "USER", targetId: abuser.userId, venue: null });

    await expect(moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "USER", targetId: abuser.userId, action: "HIDE" })).rejects.toThrow(/suspended/);
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "USER", targetId: abuser.userId, action: "SUSPEND", note: "harassment" });
    expect(await prisma.session.count({ where: { userId: abuser.userId } })).toBe(0);
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }), undefined as never)).status).toBe(401);
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "abuser@x.dev", password: "abuser password" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "account_suspended" });
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, abuser.handle, await makeActor())).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, abuser.handle, mod)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    expect((await searchUsers(prisma, await makeActor(), abuser.handle)).length).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.moderationEvent.findFirst({ where: { action: "SUSPEND", targetId: abuser.userId } })).toMatchObject({ actorId: mod.userId, note: "harassment" });

    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "USER", targetId: abuser.userId, action: "UNSUSPEND" });
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "abuser@x.dev", password: "abuser password" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("only moderators suspend; you can't report yourself", async () => {
    const target = await makeActor();
    await expect(moderate(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "USER", targetId: target.userId, action: "SUSPEND" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, target, { targetType: "USER", targetId: target.handle, reason: "me me me" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
  });
});

/**
 * Contract drift test: calls every API route the way the iOS app does (bearer tokens) against a realistic fixture
 * and parses each response with the strict schemas in src/contracts/api.ts. A second test scans src/app/api and
 * fails if any route handler is missing from the registry below, so new endpoints can't skip the contract.
 */

installMemoryStorage();

/** Every route handler and the schema its success response must satisfy. Binary routes are listed as "binary". */
const REGISTRY: Record<string, ZodTypeAny | "binary"> = {
  "POST /api/auth/login": C.TokenResponse,
  "POST /api/auth/signup": C.TokenResponse,
  "POST /api/auth/logout": C.OkResponse,
  "POST /api/auth/logout-all": C.LogoutAllResponse,
  "GET /api/me": C.MeResponse,
  "POST /api/me/terms": C.AcceptTermsResponse,
  "POST /api/me/delete": C.DeleteAccountResponse,
  "GET /api/me/blocks": C.BlocksResponse,
  "GET /api/me/following": C.FollowingResponse,
  "GET /api/places": C.PlacesResponse,
  "GET /api/places/[id]": C.PlaceResponse,
  "GET /api/places/nearby": C.NearbyPlacesResponse,
  "GET /api/places/[id]/mine": C.MyPlacePageResponse,
  "POST /api/places/[id]/stamps": C.CreateStampResponse,
  "GET /api/stamps/[id]": C.StampResponse,
  "PATCH /api/stamps/[id]": C.StampResponse,
  "POST /api/stamps/[id]/photos": C.ErrorResponse,
  "PATCH /api/stamps/[id]/photos": C.StampResponse,
  "POST /api/stamps/[id]/photos/[photoId]/remove": C.StampResponse,
  "GET /api/me/neighborhoods": C.NeighborhoodIndexResponse,
  "GET /api/me/pins": C.PinsResponse,
  "PUT /api/me/pins": C.PinsResponse,
  "PUT /api/stamps/[id]/like": C.LikeResponse,
  "DELETE /api/stamps/[id]/like": C.LikeResponse,
  "POST /api/stamps/[id]/remove": C.RemoveStampResponse,
  "GET /api/feed": C.FeedResponse,
  "GET /api/songs/search": C.SongSearchResponse,
  "GET /api/users": C.UserSearchResponse,
  "GET /api/users/[handle]": C.ProfileResponse,
  "PUT /api/users/[handle]/follow": C.FollowResponse,
  "DELETE /api/users/[handle]/follow": C.FollowResponse,
  "POST /api/users/[handle]/block": C.BlockResponse,
  "DELETE /api/users/[handle]/block": C.BlockResponse,
  "GET /api/users/[handle]/followers": C.PeopleListResponse,
  "GET /api/users/[handle]/following": C.PeopleListResponse,
  "GET /api/users/[handle]/stamps": C.UserStampsResponse,
  "GET /api/users/[handle]/places": C.UserPlacesResponse,
  "GET /api/me/follow-requests": C.FollowRequestsResponse,
  "POST /api/me/follow-requests/[handle]": C.FollowRequestDecisionResponse,
  "POST /api/me/follow-requests/approve-all": C.ApproveAllResponse,
  "DELETE /api/me/followers/[handle]": C.RemoveFollowerResponse,
  "GET /api/me/profile": C.ProfileSettingsResponse,
  "PATCH /api/me/profile": C.ProfileSettingsResponse,
  "POST /api/me/avatar": C.AvatarResponse,
  "DELETE /api/me/avatar": C.AvatarResponse,
  "GET /api/me/activity": C.ActivityResponse,
  "POST /api/flags": C.FlagResponse,
  "GET /api/mod/queue": C.ModQueueResponse,
  "GET /api/mod/log": C.ModLogResponse,
  "POST /api/mod/actions": C.ModActionResponse,
  "GET /api/mod/hidden": C.ModHiddenResponse,
  "PATCH /api/admin/users/[handle]": C.AdminUserFlagsResponse,
  "GET /api/admin/users": C.AdminUserSearchResponse,
  "POST /api/admin/users/[handle]/verification": C.VerificationResponse,
  "GET /api/admin/verification-log": C.VerificationLogResponse,
  "GET /api/health": C.HealthResponse,
  "GET /api/cron/maintenance": C.CronResponse,
  "POST /api/cron/maintenance": C.CronResponse,
  "GET /api/uploads/[...key]": "binary",
};

const checked = new Set<string>();
type Handler = (req: Request, ctx: never) => Promise<Response>;

async function call(
  key: keyof typeof REGISTRY & string,
  handler: Handler,
