  opts: { token?: string; body?: unknown; form?: FormData; params?: Record<string, string>; query?: string; headers?: Record<string, string>; status?: number } = {},
) {
  const [method, p] = key.split(" ") as [string, string];
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { ...(opts.headers ?? {}) };
  if (opts.token) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${opts.token}`;
  let body: BodyInit | undefined;
  if (opts.form) {
    // Serialize once so the multipart boundary in Content-Type matches the bytes, and send Content-Length.
    const tmp = new Request("http://localhost", { method: "POST", body: opts.form });
    const bytes = await tmp.arrayBuffer();
    body = bytes;
    headers["content-type"] = tmp.headers.get("content-type")!;
    headers["content-length"] = String(bytes.byteLength);
  } else if (opts.body !== undefined) {
    headers["content-type"] = "application/json";
    body = JSON.stringify(opts.body);
  }
  const res = await handler(new Request(`http://localhost${p}${opts.query ?? ""}`, { method, headers, body }), { params: Promise.resolve(opts.params ?? {}) } as never);
  const json = await res.json();
  expect(res.status, `${key} -> ${JSON.stringify(json).slice(0, 300)}`).toBe(opts.status ?? 200);
  const schema = REGISTRY[key];
  if (schema && schema !== "binary") {
    const parsed = schema.safeParse(json);
    expect(parsed.success, `${key} drifted from src/contracts/api.ts: ${parsed.success ? "" : JSON.stringify(parsed.error.issues).slice(0, 600)}`).toBe(true);
  }
  checked.add(key);
  return json;
}


const png = () => pngBytes(150);
const stampForm = (fields: Record<string, string>, photo = true) => {
  const form = new FormData();
  if (photo) form.set("photo", new Blob([new Uint8Array(png())], { type: "image/png" }), "p.png");
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(fields)) form.set(k, v);
  return form;
};

/** An iTunes Search API response, served to the songs route instead of calling Apple. */
const fakeItunes = async () =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify({ resultCount: 1, results: [{ trackId: 1440857781, trackName: "Contract Song", artistName: "The Schemas", collectionName: "Strict", artworkUrl100: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/x/100x100bb.jpg" }] }), { headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });

describe("API contract", () => {
  it("every route's response matches src/contracts/api.ts", async () => {
    // Fixture: an OSM place, people with follows, stamps with photo, caption and song, likes, flags.
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, [{ type: "node", id: 42, lat: VENUE_POS.lat, lon: VENUE_POS.lng, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "Contract Bar", "addr:housenumber": "1", "addr:street": "Avenue A" } }]);
    const place = await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/42" } });
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const admin = await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" });
    const friend = await makeActor();
    const tok = async (userId: string) => (await createSession(prisma, userId, { client: "IOS" })).token;
    const [modT, adminT, friendT] = await Promise.all([tok(mod.userId), tok(admin.userId), tok(friend.userId)]);

    // Auth (iOS)
    const su = await call("POST /api/auth/signup", signupR.POST, {
      headers: { "x-client": "ios" }, status: 201,
      body: { email: "app@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "App User", handle: "app_user", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
    });
    const li = await call("POST /api/auth/login", loginR.POST, { headers: { "x-client": "ios" }, body: { email: "app@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" } });
    const me = li.token as string;
    void su;
    const meBody = await call("GET /api/me", meR.GET, { token: me });
    await call("POST /api/me/terms", meTermsR.POST, { token: me, body: { version: meBody.terms.currentVersion } });

    // Places and stamps
    const pid = place.id;
    await call("GET /api/places", placesR.GET, { token: me });
    await call("GET /api/places/[id]", placeR.GET, { token: me, params: { id: place.slug } });
    const near = await call("GET /api/places/nearby", nearbyR.GET, { token: me, query: `?lat=${INSIDE.lat}&lng=${INSIDE.lng}` });
    expect(near.places[0]).toMatchObject({ slug: place.slug, inside: true });
    const full = await call("POST /api/places/[id]/stamps", placeStampsR.POST, {
      token: me, params: { id: pid }, status: 201,
      form: stampForm({ caption: "dance floor", lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng), songTitle: "Contract Song", songArtist: "The Schemas", songArtUrl: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/x/300x300bb.jpg", songSourceId: "1440857781" }),
    });
    expect(full.stamp).toMatchObject({ caption: "dance floor", song: { title: "Contract Song" }, mine: true });
    // Caption only, song only and photo only are stamps too (each at its own place: one stamp per place per 15 minutes).
    const p2 = await makeVenue({ slug: "p2" });
    const p3 = await makeVenue({ slug: "p3" });
    const p4 = await makeVenue({ slug: "p4" });
    const captionOnly = await call("POST /api/places/[id]/stamps", placeStampsR.POST, { token: friendT, params: { id: p2.id }, status: 201, form: stampForm({ caption: "just words", lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng) }, false) });
    expect(captionOnly.stamp).toMatchObject({ photoUrl: null, song: null });
    await call("POST /api/places/[id]/stamps", placeStampsR.POST, { token: friendT, params: { id: p3.id }, status: 201, form: stampForm({ lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng), songTitle: "Only Song", songArtist: "Solo" }, false) });
    await call("POST /api/places/[id]/stamps", placeStampsR.POST, { token: friendT, params: { id: p4.id }, status: 201, form: stampForm({ lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng) }) });
    const stampId = full.stamp.id as string;
    const mine = await call("GET /api/places/[id]/mine", placeMineR.GET, { token: me, params: { id: place.slug }, query: `?lat=${INSIDE.lat}&lng=${INSIDE.lng}` });
    expect(mine).toMatchObject({ inside: true, visits: [{ stamps: [{ id: stampId }] }] });

    // Songs (proxied): the route calls Apple, so the contract test serves it a canned reply.
    const realFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    globalThis.fetch = fakeItunes as typeof fetch;
    try {
      const found = await call("GET /api/songs/search", songsR.GET, { token: me, query: "?q=contract" });
      expect(found.songs[0]).toMatchObject({ title: "Contract Song", sourceId: "1440857781" });
    } finally {
      globalThis.fetch = realFetch;
    }

    // People: both accounts are private, so follows start as requests.
    await call("GET /api/users", usersR.GET, { token: me, query: `?q=${friend.handle.slice(0, 5)}` });
    const req1 = await call("PUT /api/users/[handle]/follow", followR.PUT, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    expect(req1.state).toBe("requested");
    const limited = await call("GET /api/users/[handle]", userR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    expect(limited.access).toBe("limited");
    await call("PUT /api/users/[handle]/follow", followR.PUT, { token: friendT, params: { handle: "app_user" } });
    const reqs = await call("GET /api/me/follow-requests", meRequestsR.GET, { token: friendT });
    expect(reqs.requests).toHaveLength(1);
    await call("POST /api/me/follow-requests/[handle]", meRequestR.POST, { token: friendT, params: { handle: "app_user" }, body: { approve: true } });
    await call("POST /api/me/follow-requests/approve-all", meApproveAllR.POST, { token: me });
    const fullProfile = await call("GET /api/users/[handle]", userR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    expect(fullProfile.access).toBe("full");
    expect(fullProfile.user.counts).toMatchObject({ stamps: 3, places: 3 });
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]", userR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: "app_user" } });
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]/followers", followersR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]/following", followingOfR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    await call("GET /api/me/following", meFollowingR.GET, { token: me });
    // Friend follows back so my stamp reaches their feed; I follow friend (approved above), so my feed has theirs.
    const myFeed = await call("GET /api/feed", feedR.GET, { token: me });
    expect(myFeed.stamps).toHaveLength(3);
    await call("GET /api/feed", feedR.GET, { token: me, query: "?before=2999-01-01T00:00:00.000Z_zzzz" });
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]/stamps", userStampsR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]/places", userPlacesR.GET, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });

    await call("GET /api/me/neighborhoods", neighborhoodsR.GET, { token: me });
    await call("PUT /api/me/pins", pinsR.PUT, { token: me, body: { stampIds: [stampId] } });
    const pins = await call("GET /api/me/pins", pinsR.GET, { token: me });
    expect(pins.stamps.map((x: { id: string }) => x.id)).toEqual([stampId]);

    // Photos: reject additions, lay out and remove the creation-time photo.
    const more = new FormData();
    more.append("photos", new Blob([new Uint8Array(png())], { type: "image/png" }), "a.png");
    more.append("photos", new Blob([new Uint8Array(png())], { type: "image/png" }), "b.png");
    const rejected = await call("POST /api/stamps/[id]/photos", stampPhotosR.POST, { token: me, params: { id: stampId }, form: more, status: 400 });
    expect(rejected).toEqual({ error: "bad_request", message: "Photos can only be attached when creating a stamp" });
    const photoId = full.stamp.photos[0].id;
    const laid = await call("PATCH /api/stamps/[id]/photos", stampPhotosR.PATCH, { token: me, params: { id: stampId }, body: { photos: [{ id: photoId, x: 0.4, y: 0.6, rotation: -7 }] } });
    expect(laid.stamp.photos[0]).toMatchObject({ x: 0.4, y: 0.6, rotation: -7 });
    await call("POST /api/stamps/[id]/photos/[photoId]/remove", stampPhotoRemoveR.POST, { token: me, params: { id: stampId, photoId } });

    // Stamp detail, likes, activity, edit, remove
    const friendStamp = myFeed.stamps[0].id as string;
    await call("GET /api/stamps/[id]", stampR.GET, { token: me, params: { id: friendStamp } });
    await call("PUT /api/stamps/[id]/like", stampLikeR.PUT, { token: me, params: { id: friendStamp } });
    await call("GET /api/me/activity", activityR.GET, { token: friendT });
    await call("DELETE /api/stamps/[id]/like", stampLikeR.DELETE, { token: me, params: { id: friendStamp } });
    await call("PATCH /api/stamps/[id]", stampR.PATCH, { token: me, params: { id: stampId }, body: { caption: "still great", song: { title: "Edited Song", artist: "Edit Band" } } });
    await call("PATCH /api/stamps/[id]", stampR.PATCH, { token: me, params: { id: stampId }, body: { song: null } });

    // Profile settings and avatar
    await call("GET /api/me/profile", meProfileR.GET, { token: me });
    await call("PATCH /api/me/profile", meProfileR.PATCH, { token: me, body: { bio: "Out most Fridays", isPrivate: false } });
    const avatarForm = new FormData();
    avatarForm.set("photo", new Blob([new Uint8Array(png())], { type: "image/png" }), "a.png");
    const av = await call("POST /api/me/avatar", meAvatarR.POST, { token: me, form: avatarForm });
    expect(av.avatarUrl).toMatch(/^\/api\/uploads\/avatars\//);
    await call("GET /api/users/[handle]", userR.GET, { token: friendT, params: { handle: "app_user" } });
    await call("DELETE /api/me/avatar", meAvatarR.DELETE, { token: me });
    await call("DELETE /api/me/followers/[handle]", meFollowerR.DELETE, { token: friendT, params: { handle: "app_user" } });
    await call("DELETE /api/users/[handle]/follow", followR.DELETE, { token: me, params: { handle: friend.handle } });
    const other = await makeUser();
    await call("POST /api/users/[handle]/block", blockR.POST, { token: me, params: { handle: other.handle } });
    const blocks = await call("GET /api/me/blocks", meBlocksR.GET, { token: me });
    expect(blocks.blocks).toHaveLength(1);
    await call("DELETE /api/users/[handle]/block", blockR.DELETE, { token: me, params: { handle: other.handle } });

    // Flags + moderation (stamp and account reports)
    await call("POST /api/flags", flagsR.POST, { token: friendT, body: { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stampId, reason: "not at this place" }, status: 201 });
    await call("POST /api/flags", flagsR.POST, { token: friendT, body: { targetType: "USER", targetId: other.handle, reason: "harassing people" }, status: 201 });
    await call("GET /api/mod/queue", modQueueR.GET, { token: modT });
    await call("POST /api/mod/actions", modActionsR.POST, { token: modT, body: { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stampId, action: "HIDE", note: "check" } });
    await call("POST /api/mod/actions", modActionsR.POST, { token: modT, body: { targetType: "USER", targetId: other.id, action: "SUSPEND" } });
    const hidden = await call("GET /api/mod/hidden", modHiddenR.GET, { token: modT });
    expect(hidden.items.map((i: { targetId: string }) => i.targetId)).toContain(stampId);
    await call("GET /api/mod/queue", modQueueR.GET, { token: modT });
    await call("GET /api/mod/log", modLogR.GET, { token: modT });
    await call("POST /api/stamps/[id]/remove", stampRemoveR.POST, { token: friendT, params: { id: friendStamp } });

    // Admin (web-only, still contracted)
    await call("PATCH /api/admin/users/[handle]", adminUserR.PATCH, { token: adminT, params: { handle: friend.handle }, body: { geofenceExempt: true } });
    await call("GET /api/admin/users", adminUsersR.GET, { token: adminT, query: `?q=${friend.handle.slice(0, 6)}` });
    await call("POST /api/admin/users/[handle]/verification", adminVerifyR.POST, { token: adminT, params: { handle: friend.handle }, body: { verified: true, note: "Known promoter" } });
    await call("GET /api/admin/verification-log", adminVerifyLogR.GET, { token: adminT });

    // Ops
    const health = await healthR.GET();
    expect(C.HealthResponse.safeParse(await health.json()).success).toBe(true);
    checked.add("GET /api/health");
    process.env.CRON_SECRET = "contract-cron-secret-000";
    await call("GET /api/cron/maintenance", cronR.GET as Handler, { headers: { authorization: "Bearer contract-cron-secret-000" } });
    await call("POST /api/cron/maintenance", cronR.POST as Handler, { headers: { authorization: "Bearer contract-cron-secret-000" } });
    checked.add("GET /api/uploads/[...key]"); // binary, covered by serverless.test.ts

    // Sign-out flows and deletion last.
    await call("POST /api/auth/logout", logoutR.POST, { token: friendT });
    await call("POST /api/auth/logout-all", logoutAllR.POST, { token: me });
    const fresh = await signup(prisma, { email: "bye@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "Bye", handle: "bye_bye", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const byeT = await tok(fresh.user.id);
    await call("POST /api/me/delete", meDeleteR.POST, { token: byeT, body: { password: "long enough pw" } });
  }, 60_000);

  it("error responses from real failure paths match ErrorResponse", async () => {
    const place = await makeVenue();
    const u = await makeActor();
    const t = (await createSession(prisma, u.userId, { client: "IOS" })).token;
    const stampAt = (fields: Record<string, string>) => {
      const form = stampForm(fields, false);
      return new Request("http://x", { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${t}`, "content-length": "500" }, body: form });
    };
    const ctx = { params: Promise.resolve({ id: place.id }) };
    const cases: [string, Response][] = [
      ["401", await meR.GET(new Request("http://localhost/api/me"), undefined as never)],
      ["401 invalid_token", await meR.GET(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer dead-dead-dead-dead-dead-0" } }), undefined as never)],
      ["403 geofence", await placeStampsR.POST(stampAt({ caption: "far", lat: String(VENUE_POS.lat + 0.01), lng: String(VENUE_POS.lng) }), ctx)],
      ["400 invalid_input", await placeStampsR.POST(stampAt({ lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng) }), ctx)],
      ["404", await userR.GET(new Request("http://x"), { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: "nobody_here" }) })],
    ];
    for (const [label, res] of cases) {
      expect(res.status, label).toBe(Number(label.slice(0, 3)));
      expect(C.ErrorResponse.safeParse(await res.json()).success, label).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("exercised every registered route", () => {
    const missing = Object.keys(REGISTRY).filter((k) => !checked.has(k));
    expect(missing, "routes in the registry that the contract test didn't exercise").toEqual([]);
  });

  it("error responses match ErrorResponse", async () => {
    const res = await meR.GET(new Request("http://localhost/api/me"), undefined as never);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(C.ErrorResponse.safeParse(await res.json()).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("every route handler in src/app/api is registered in the contract", () => {
    const root = path.resolve("src/app/api");
    const found: string[] = [];
    const walk = (dir: string) => {
      for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
        const full = path.join(dir, name);
        if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) walk(full);
        else if (name === "route.ts") {
          const src = readFileSync(full, "utf8");
          const p = "/api" + path.dirname(full).slice(root.length).split(path.sep).join("/");
          // Any way of exporting a method handler: `export const X`, `export (async) function X`, `export { h as X }`.
          const methods = new Set<string>();
          for (const m of src.matchAll(/export\s+(?:const|let|var|async\s+function|function)\s+(GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS)\b/g)) methods.add(m[1]!);
          for (const block of src.matchAll(/export\s*\{([^}]*)\}/g)) {
            for (const m of block[1]!.matchAll(/(?:\bas\s+|^|,)\s*(GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS)\b/g)) methods.add(m[1]!);
          }
          for (const m of methods) found.push(`${m} ${p}`);
        }
      }
    };
    walk(root);
    expect(found.filter((k) => !(k in REGISTRY)), "add these routes to REGISTRY + src/contracts/api.ts").toEqual([]);
    expect(Object.keys(REGISTRY).filter((k) => !found.includes(k)), "stale REGISTRY entries").toEqual([]);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/db-guards.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { makeUser, makeVenue, INSIDE } from "./factories";

// Database-level backstops: rule 4 (hide, never delete; append-only log), the stamp shape, likes, follows and the badge log.
describe("database guards", () => {
  async function stampRow(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    const user = await makeUser();
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const photo = await prisma.photo.create({
      data: { userId: user.id, venueId: venue.id, storageKey: `photos/${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}.png`, mimeType: "image/png", caption: "x", ...INSIDE, ...extra },
    });
    return { user, venue, photo };
  }

  it("rejects UPDATE and DELETE on moderation_event", async () => {
    const { user, photo } = await stampRow();
    const event = await prisma.moderationEvent.create({ data: { action: "HIDE", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: photo.id, actorId: user.id } });
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.update({ where: { id: event.id }, data: { note: "rewrite history" } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.delete({ where: { id: event.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DELETE FROM moderation_event`)).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
  });

  it("rejects DELETE of a stamp; allows hiding; refuses rewriting who, where, when or what", async () => {
    const { photo } = await stampRow();
    await expect(prisma.photo.delete({ where: { id: photo.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/hide user content/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { createdAt: new Date() } })).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { lat: 1 } })).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { storageKey: "photos/other.png" } })).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { rating: 9 } })).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { hiddenAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { hiddenAt: null } })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("caption and song are the only editable content, and the caption is 280 characters at most", async () => {
    const { photo } = await stampRow();
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { caption: "edited", editedAt: new Date(), songTitle: "Song", songArtist: "Artist" } });
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { caption: "x".repeat(281) } })).rejects.toThrow(/caption_length/);
  });

  it("a removed stamp stays removed", async () => {
    const { photo } = await stampRow();
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { removedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { removedAt: null } })).rejects.toThrow(/stays removed/);
  });

  it("a stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song", async () => {
    const user = await makeUser();
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const base = { userId: user.id, venueId: venue.id, ...INSIDE };
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "" } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_has_content/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "   " } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_has_content/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "hello" } })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "", songTitle: "T", songArtist: "A" } })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "", storageKey: "photos/stale.png", mimeType: "image/png" } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_has_content/);
    await expect(prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const p = await tx.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "", storageKey: "photos/p.png", mimeType: "image/png" } });
      await tx.stampPhoto.create({ data: { stampId: p.id, storageKey: "photos/p.png", mimeType: "image/png", position: 0 } });
    })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    // A photo has both key and type; a song has title and artist; art must be https.
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "x", storageKey: "photos/q.png" } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_storage_pair/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "x", songTitle: "T" } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_song_shape/);
    await expect(prisma.photo.create({ data: { ...base, caption: "x", songTitle: "T", songArtist: "A", songArtUrl: "http://x.test/a.jpg" } })).rejects.toThrow(/photo_song_shape/);
  });

  it("likes: never your own stamp, and refused on hidden, removed and App Review stamps", async () => {
    const { user, photo } = await stampRow();
    const liker = await makeUser();
    await expect(prisma.photoLike.create({ data: { userId: user.id, photoId: photo.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/own stamp/);
    await expect(prisma.photoLike.create({ data: { userId: liker.id, photoId: photo.id } })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    const hidden = (await stampRow({ hiddenAt: new Date() })).photo;
    const removed = (await stampRow({ removedAt: new Date() })).photo;
    const exempt = (await stampRow({ countable: false })).photo;
    for (const p of [hidden, removed, exempt]) await expect(prisma.photoLike.create({ data: { userId: liker.id, photoId: p.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/can't be liked/);
  });

  it("enforces value constraints", async () => {
    const { user } = await stampRow();
    await expect(prisma.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { reputation: 2 } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.rateEvent.create({ data: { userId: user.id, kind: "typo" } })).rejects.toThrow(/rate_event_kind/);
    await expect(prisma.rateEvent.create({ data: { userId: user.id, kind: "song_search" } })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a follow clears the matching request; no following or requesting yourself; bios are 150 characters max", async () => {
    const [a, b] = [await makeUser(), await makeUser()];
    await prisma.followRequest.create({ data: { requesterId: a.id, targetId: b.id } });
    await prisma.follow.create({ data: { followerId: a.id, followeeId: b.id } });
    expect(await prisma.followRequest.count({ where: { requesterId: a.id } })).toBe(0);
    await expect(prisma.follow.create({ data: { followerId: a.id, followeeId: a.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/follow_not_self/);
    await expect(prisma.followRequest.create({ data: { requesterId: a.id, targetId: a.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/follow_request_not_self/);
    await expect(prisma.user.update({ where: { id: a.id }, data: { bio: "x".repeat(151) } })).rejects.toThrow(/bio_length/);
  });

  it("the verification log is append-only; the badge changes only with a matching log entry in the same transaction", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser();
    await expect(prisma.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { verifiedAt: new Date() } })).rejects.toThrow(/verification log entry/);
    await prisma.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: u.id, action: "GRANT", note: "Earlier" } });
    await expect(prisma.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { verifiedAt: new Date() } })).rejects.toThrow(/verification log entry/);
    await expect(
      prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: u.id, action: "REVOKE", note: "Wrong way" } });
        await tx.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { verifiedAt: new Date() } });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/verification log entry/);
    const ev = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const e = await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: u.id, action: "GRANT", note: "Known artist" } });
      await tx.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { verifiedAt: new Date() } });
      return e;
    });
    await expect(prisma.verificationEvent.update({ where: { id: ev.id }, data: { note: "rewrite" } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.verificationEvent.delete({ where: { id: ev.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.user.create({ data: { email: "v@x.dev", handle: "born_verified", displayName: "V", passwordHash: "x", verifiedAt: new Date() } })).rejects.toThrow(/at creation/);
  });

  it("new accounts are private by default", async () => {
    expect((await makeUser()).isPrivate).toBe(true);
  });

  it("places have a default 100 m geofence", async () => {
    expect((await makeVenue()).geofenceMeters).toBe(100);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/env.ts
====================================================================================================

// Point every Prisma client in the test process at the separate test database.
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";

if (existsSync(".env")) process.loadEnvFile(".env");
const url = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;
if (!url) throw new Error("TEST_DATABASE_URL is not set (see .env.example)");
const isTestDatabase = (value: string) => ["nostos_test", "nostos_codex_test"].includes(new URL(value).pathname.slice(1));
if (!isTestDatabase(url) || !isTestDatabase(process.env.TEST_DIRECT_URL ?? url)) {
  throw new Error(`Refusing to run integration tests against a non-test database: ${url}`);
}
process.env.DATABASE_URL = url;
// Migrations run through DIRECT_URL. Tests may override it (e.g. pooled runs point DATABASE_URL at PgBouncer).
process.env.DIRECT_URL = process.env.TEST_DIRECT_URL ?? url;
export const TEST_DATABASE_URL = url;


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/factories.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Role } from "@prisma/client";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { loadActor, type Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";

let n = 0;
// Precomputed scrypt hash is not needed here: factories never log in with a password.
const FAKE_HASH = "scrypt$32768$8$1$AAAA$AAAA";

/** Accounts are private by default, as in the product; pass `isPrivate: false` for a public one. */
export async function makeUser(opts: { role?: Role; ageDays?: number; reputation?: number; handle?: string; isPrivate?: boolean } = {}) {
  n++;
  const handle = opts.handle ?? `user${n}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`;
  return prisma.user.create({
    data: {
      email: `${handle}@test.dev`, handle, displayName: handle, passwordHash: FAKE_HASH, role: opts.role ?? "USER",
      reputation: opts.reputation ?? 0.5, isPrivate: opts.isPrivate ?? true, createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - (opts.ageDays ?? 30) * DAY_MS),
    },
  });
}

export const VENUE_POS = { lat: 40.7265, lng: -73.9845 };

export async function makeVenue(opts: { slug?: string; lat?: number; lng?: number; geofenceMeters?: number } = {}) {
  n++;
  const slug = opts.slug ?? `venue-${n}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`;
  return prisma.venue.create({
    data: {
      slug, name: `Venue ${n}`, address: `${n} Test St`, neighborhood: "East Village",
      lat: opts.lat ?? VENUE_POS.lat, lng: opts.lng ?? VENUE_POS.lng, ...(opts.geofenceMeters ? { geofenceMeters: opts.geofenceMeters } : {}),
    },
  });
}

export async function actorFor(userId: string): Promise<Actor> {
  return (await loadActor(prisma, userId))!;
}

export async function makeActor(opts: Parameters<typeof makeUser>[0] = {}) {
  const u = await makeUser(opts);
  return actorFor(u.id);
}

/** ~20m from VENUE_POS: inside the 100m geofence. */
export const INSIDE = { lat: VENUE_POS.lat + 0.00018, lng: VENUE_POS.lng };
/** ~1.1km away: outside the geofence. */
export const OUTSIDE = { lat: VENUE_POS.lat + 0.01, lng: VENUE_POS.lng };

/** Verifies an account the only way the database allows: a log entry, then the badge, in one transaction. */
export async function verify(userId: string, note = "Test verification") {
  await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId, action: "GRANT", note } });
    await tx.user.update({ where: { id: userId }, data: { verifiedAt: new Date() } });
  });
}

import { PNG } from "pngjs";
import { createStamp } from "@/server/services/stamps";

/** A tiny valid PNG (what a phone would send, minus the size). */
export function pngBytes(fill = 120) {
  const p = new PNG({ width: 4, height: 4 });
  p.data.fill(fill);
  return PNG.sync.write(p);
}

/** Stamps a place as `actor`, inside the geofence unless `at` says otherwise. Defaults to a photo and a caption. */
export function makeStamp(
  actor: Actor,
  venueId: string,
  opts: { at?: { lat: number; lng: number }; caption?: string; photo?: boolean; meta?: Record<string, unknown>; now?: Date } = {},
) {
  const at = opts.at ?? INSIDE;
  return createStamp(
    prisma,
    actor,
    venueId,
    { bytes: opts.photo === false ? null : pngBytes(), meta: { caption: opts.caption ?? "hello", lat: String(at.lat), lng: String(at.lng), ...opts.meta } },
    opts.now,
  );
}


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/global-setup.ts
====================================================================================================

import { execSync } from "node:child_process";
import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
import { TEST_DATABASE_URL } from "./env";

const DIRECT = process.env.DIRECT_URL ?? TEST_DATABASE_URL;

/**
 * Creates the separate test database on first run (no init-script bind mount needed, which is fragile
 * on Docker VMs that only share $HOME), then applies pending migrations, including the hand-written triggers.
 * `migrate deploy` is non-destructive; each test truncates tables in setup.ts instead.
 */
export default async function setup() {
  const url = new URL(DIRECT);
  const dbName = url.pathname.replace(/^\//, "");
  if (!/^[a-z0-9_]+$/.test(dbName)) throw new Error(`Unexpected test database name: ${dbName}`);
  const admin = new URL(DIRECT);
  admin.pathname = "/postgres";
  const client = new PrismaClient({ datasourceUrl: admin.toString() });
  try {
    const rows = await client.$queryRaw<{ exists: boolean }[]>`SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname = ${dbName}) AS exists`;
    if (!rows[0]?.exists) await client.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE DATABASE "${dbName}"`);
  } finally {
    await client.$disconnect();
  }
  execSync("pnpm exec prisma migrate deploy", {
    stdio: "pipe",
    env: { ...process.env, DATABASE_URL: DIRECT, DIRECT_URL: DIRECT, PRISMA_HIDE_UPDATE_MESSAGE: "1" },
  });
}


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/invariants.test.ts
====================================================================================================

// The rules that must never break (CLAUDE.md section "Rules that must never break"). One test per rule, named
// "rule N: ...". scripts/gate.sh fails if this file is missing, skipped, or if a required rule below has no passing test.
// Required rules are listed in scripts/invariants-required.txt.
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import sharp from "sharp";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { processImage } from "@/server/services/image";
import { createStamp, getStamp, listFeed, listUserPlaces, listUserStamps, removeStamp } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { decideFollowRequest, follow, getProfile } from "@/server/services/people";
import { blockUser } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { likeStamp } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { getMe } from "@/server/services/me";
import { listActivity } from "@/server/services/activity";
import { signup } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { actorFor, makeActor, makeStamp, makeUser, makeVenue, verify, INSIDE, OUTSIDE } from "./factories";

installMemoryStorage();

const wire = (x: unknown) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)) as unknown;

describe("invariants", () => {
  it("rule 1: a stamp is created only inside the geofence; exempt accounts are the only exception and see only themselves", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { at: OUTSIDE })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    // Even a request that lies about nothing but is far away, and one with coordinates just past the radius.
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { at: { lat: INSIDE.lat + 0.002, lng: INSIDE.lng } })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    expect(await prisma.photo.count({ where: { venueId: venue.id } })).toBe(0);
    await expect(makeStamp(me, venue.id, { at: INSIDE })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    const reviewer = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: reviewer.userId }, data: { geofenceExempt: true } });
    const exempt = { ...reviewer, geofenceExempt: true };
    const stamp = await makeStamp(exempt, venue.id, { at: OUTSIDE });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await makeActor(), stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, exempt, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("rule 2: a private account's stamps and places reach nobody without full access, in any payload", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ handle: "inv_private" });
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id);
    const requester = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, requester, author.handle);
    const follower = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, follower, author.handle);
    await decideFollowRequest(prisma, author, follower.handle, { approve: true });
    for (const v of [null, await makeActor(), requester]) {
      await expect(getStamp(prisma, v, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, v, author.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
      await expect(listUserPlaces(prisma, v, author.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
      const profile = JSON.stringify(wire(await getProfile(prisma, author.handle, v)));
      expect(profile).not.toContain(venue.slug);
      expect(profile).not.toContain(stamp.id);
    }
    // The pending requester sees nothing of the author's stamps in the feed or in activity either.
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, requester)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, follower, author.handle)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(listUserPlaces(prisma, follower, author.handle)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, follower)).stamps.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual([stamp.id]);
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, author, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("rule 3: a block in either direction hides everything, except from staff", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id);
    const other = await makeActor();
    await blockUser(prisma, other, author.handle);
    for (const [viewer, target] of [[other, author], [author, other]] as const) {
      await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, viewer, target.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(getProfile(prisma, target.handle, viewer)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    }
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, other, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(likeStamp(prisma, other, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, author, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("rule 4: hidden content is hidden, never deleted; the moderation log is append-only", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id);
    await removeStamp(prisma, author, stamp.id);
    expect(await prisma.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).toMatchObject({ removedAt: expect.any(Date) });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await makeActor(), stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(prisma.photo.delete({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/hide user content/);
    const event = await prisma.moderationEvent.create({ data: { action: "HIDE", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: stamp.id, actorId: author.userId } });
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.update({ where: { id: event.id }, data: { note: "x" } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.delete({ where: { id: event.id } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
  });

  it("rule 5: EXIF and GPS are stripped from every upload", async () => {
    const jpeg = await sharp({ create: { width: 200, height: 100, channels: 3, background: "#ff3d6e" } })
      .jpeg()
      .withExif({ IFD0: { Make: "LeakyPhone" }, IFD3: { GPSLatitudeRef: "N", GPSLatitude: "40/1 43/1 36/1" } })
      .toBuffer();
    expect((await sharp(jpeg).metadata()).exif).toBeTruthy();
    const { bytes } = await processImage(jpeg);
    expect((await sharp(bytes).metadata()).exif).toBeUndefined();
    expect(bytes.toString("latin1")).not.toContain("LeakyPhone");
    // And through the real stamp path: what lands in storage is clean.
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const stamp = await createStamp(prisma, me, venue.id, { bytes: jpeg, meta: { caption: "", lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng) } });
    const row = await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } });
    const stored = await (await import("@/lib/storage")).getStorage().get(row.storageKey!);
    expect(stored).not.toBeNull();
    expect((await sharp(stored!).metadata()).exif).toBeUndefined();
    expect(stored!.toString("latin1")).not.toContain("LeakyPhone");
  });

  it("rule 6: no user coordinates, storage keys or user ids reach a viewer who should not get them", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    // A stamper's own position, distinct from the place's (the place's location is public, the stamper's never is).
    const at = { lat: INSIDE.lat + 0.00031, lng: INSIDE.lng + 0.00027 };
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id, { at });
    const viewer = await makeActor();
    await likeStamp(prisma, viewer, stamp.id);
    await follow(prisma, viewer, author.handle);
    const payloads = [
      wire(await getStamp(prisma, viewer, stamp.id)), wire(await listUserStamps(prisma, viewer, author.handle)),
      wire(await getProfile(prisma, author.handle, viewer)), wire(await listFeed(prisma, viewer)),
      wire(await listActivity(prisma, await actorFor(author.userId))),
    ];
    const places = wire(await listUserPlaces(prisma, viewer, author.handle));
    const json = JSON.stringify([...payloads, places]);
    // Media is reachable only through a signed URL, never as a raw storage key field.
    expect(json).not.toMatch(/"(storageKey|storage_key|userId|user_id)"/);
    for (const m of json.matchAll(/"(?:url|photoUrl)":"([^"]+)"/g)) expect(m[1]).toMatch(/[?&]sig=/);
    expect(json).not.toContain(author.userId);
    expect(json).not.toContain(viewer.userId);
    // The stamper's coordinates appear nowhere. Only the places list carries lat and lng, and those are the place's own.
    expect(json).not.toContain(String(at.lat));
    expect(json).not.toContain(String(at.lng));
    expect(JSON.stringify(payloads)).not.toMatch(/"(lat|lng)"/);
    expect(JSON.stringify(places)).toContain(String(venue.lat));
  });

  it("rule 7: reputation is visible only to the user and moderators", async () => {
    const subject = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false, reputation: 0.77 });
    const stranger = await makeActor();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const seen = async (v: typeof stranger) => {
      const p = await getProfile(prisma, subject.handle, v);
      return p.access === "full" ? p.user.reputation : null;
    };
    expect(await seen(stranger)).toBeNull();
    expect(await seen(subject)).toBe(0.77);
    expect(await seen(mod)).toBe(0.77);
    expect(JSON.stringify(wire(await getMe(prisma, stranger)))).not.toContain("0.77");
    // It never appears on a stamp, in the feed or in a list.
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const stamp = await makeStamp(subject, venue.id);
    await follow(prisma, stranger, subject.handle);
    expect(JSON.stringify([wire(await getStamp(prisma, stranger, stamp.id)), wire(await listFeed(prisma, stranger))])).not.toMatch(/reputation|0\.77/);
  });

  it("rule 8: under-21s are blocked at signup and nothing about them is stored", async () => {
    const now = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z");
    const tables = ["app_user", "session", "login_attempt", "rate_event", "flag", "moderation_event", "photo", "follow"];
    const count = async () => Object.fromEntries(await Promise.all(tables.map(async (t) => [t, Number((await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<{ n: bigint }[]>(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM "${t}"`))[0]!.n)])));
    const before = await count();
    const attempt = { email: "u20@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "Twenty", handle: "twenty", acceptTerms: true };
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...attempt, birthdate: "2005-10-03" }, { now })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "underage" });
    expect(await count()).toEqual(before);
    expect(await prisma.user.count({ where: { OR: [{ email: attempt.email }, { handle: attempt.handle }] } })).toBe(0);
    // No column anywhere holds a birthdate, so an adult's isn't kept either.
    const cols = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ column_name: string }[]>`SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'public'`;
    expect(cols.map((c) => c.column_name).filter((c) => /birth|dob/i.test(c))).toEqual([]);
    await expect(signup(prisma, { ...attempt, birthdate: "2005-10-02" }, { now })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("rule 9: the feed is chronological from people you follow, with no ranking or personalization", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const a = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const b = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stranger = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await follow(prisma, me, a.handle);
    await follow(prisma, me, b.handle);
    const t0 = Date.now() - 3 * 3600_000;
    const s1 = await makeStamp(a, venue.id, { caption: "first", now: new Date(t0) });
    const s2 = await makeStamp(b, venue.id, { caption: "second", now: new Date(t0 + 3600_000) });
    const s3 = await makeStamp(a, venue.id, { caption: "third", now: new Date(t0 + 2 * 3600_000) });
    await makeStamp(stranger, venue.id, { caption: "not followed", now: new Date(t0 + 2.5 * 3600_000) });
    // Likes, a verified badge and a higher reputation change nothing about the order.
    const fan = await makeActor();
    await likeStamp(prisma, fan, s1.id);
    await verify(a.userId);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: a.userId }, data: { reputation: 1.4 } });
    const feed = await listFeed(prisma, me);
    expect(feed.stamps.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual([s3.id, s2.id, s1.id]);
    // Same answer for anyone with the same follows: nothing about the viewer's history shapes it.
    const twin = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, twin, a.handle);
    await follow(prisma, twin, b.handle);
    await likeStamp(prisma, twin, s2.id);
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, twin)).stamps.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual([s3.id, s2.id, s1.id]);
    // Paging is by time, newest first, with no gaps.
    const times = feed.stamps.map((s) => s.createdAt.getTime());
    expect([...times].sort((x, y) => y - x)).toEqual(times);
  });

  it("rule 11: with social off, nothing of another person's passport reaches anyone but its owner", async () => {
    vi.stubEnv("SOCIAL_ENABLED", "0");
    try {
      const venue = await makeVenue();
      const owner = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false, handle: "inv_owner11" });
      const stamp = await makeStamp(owner, venue.id, { caption: "mine alone" });
      const viewers = [null, await makeActor(), await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" })];
      for (const v of viewers) {
        await expect(getStamp(prisma, v, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
        await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, v, owner.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
        await expect(listUserPlaces(prisma, v, owner.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
        await expect(getProfile(prisma, owner.handle, v)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      }
      await expect(listFeed(prisma, viewers[1]!)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(getStamp(prisma, owner, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    } finally {
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it("rule 10: likes and badges never affect anything else", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id);
    const fan = await makeActor();
    const before = await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: author.userId } });
    const rowBefore = await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } });
    await likeStamp(prisma, fan, stamp.id);
    await verify(author.userId);
    const after = await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: author.userId } });
    const rowAfter = await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } });
    expect(after.reputation).toBe(before.reputation);
    expect(after.isPrivate).toBe(before.isPrivate);
    expect(rowAfter).toEqual(rowBefore);
    // A verified private account is still private.
    const priv = await makeUser({ isPrivate: true });
    await verify(priv.id);
    await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, await makeActor(), priv.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/mem-storage.ts
====================================================================================================

import { LocalDiskStorage, setStorageForTests, type BlobStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

/** In-memory BlobStorage for tests; signs URLs exactly like the local driver. */
export function installMemoryStorage() {
  const mem = new Map<string, Buffer>();
  const signer = new LocalDiskStorage("/nonexistent");
  const storage: BlobStorage = {
    put: async (k, d) => void mem.set(k, d),
    get: async (k) => mem.get(k) ?? null,
    delete: async (k) => void mem.delete(k),
    signedUrl: (k, now) => signer.signedUrl(k, now),
  };
  setStorageForTests(storage);
  return mem;
}


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/moderation.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { flagContent, moderate, moderationQueue, recentlyHidden } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { getStamp, listUserStamps } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { follow } from "@/server/services/people";
import { POST as modRoute } from "@/app/api/mod/actions/route";
import { POST as flagRoute } from "@/app/api/flags/route";
import { createSession, SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, makeStamp, makeVenue } from "./factories";

installMemoryStorage();

/** A stamp by a public author, so any signed-in person can see (and so report) it. */
async function publicStamp() {
  const venue = await makeVenue();
  const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
  const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id, { caption: "crowd" });
  return { venue, author, stamp };
}

describe("flags", () => {
  it("three flags from distinct eligible users auto-hide a stamp pending review (logged as system)", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const [a, b, c] = [await makeActor(), await makeActor(), await makeActor()];
    expect((await flagContent(prisma, a, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" })).autoHidden).toBe(false);
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, a, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake again" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
    expect((await flagContent(prisma, b, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" })).autoHidden).toBe(false);
    expect((await flagContent(prisma, c, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" })).autoHidden).toBe(true);
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.moderationEvent.findFirst({ where: { action: "AUTO_HIDE", targetId: stamp.id } })).toMatchObject({ actorId: null });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await makeActor(), stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
  });

  it("concurrent flags still auto-hide exactly once", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const flaggers = await Promise.all([1, 2, 3, 4].map(() => makeActor()));
    await Promise.all(flaggers.map((f) => flagContent(prisma, f, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" }).catch(() => null)));
    expect(await prisma.moderationEvent.count({ where: { action: "AUTO_HIDE", targetId: stamp.id } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it("staff, new accounts and low-reputation accounts can flag but never count toward the auto-hide", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "hmm" });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor({ ageDays: 1 }), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "new account" });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor({ reputation: 0.3 }), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "low rep" });
    expect((await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" })).autoHidden).toBe(false);
  });

  it("you can't flag your own stamp, and signed-out people can't flag", async () => {
    const { author, stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, author, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "oops" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, null, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "anon" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("you can only report a stamp you can see: a private account's stamp is a 404 to strangers and requesters", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const priv = await makeActor();
    const stamp = await makeStamp(priv, venue.id);
    const stranger = await makeActor();
    const requester = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, requester, priv.handle);
    for (const v of [stranger, requester]) {
      await expect(flagContent(prisma, v, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "probing" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    }
    expect(await prisma.flag.count({ where: { targetId: stamp.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("reports carry a category (default OTHER): underage, stalking and not-at-this-place are first-class", async () => {
    const { stamp, author } = await publicStamp();
    const flaggers = [await makeActor(), await makeActor(), await makeActor(), await makeActor()];
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[0]!, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[1]!, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "just wrong" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[2]!, { targetType: "USER", targetId: author.handle, reason: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[3]!, { targetType: "USER", targetId: author.handle, reason: "Stalking or unwanted contact", category: "STALKING" });
    expect((await prisma.flag.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } })).map((f) => f.category)).toEqual(["NOT_AT_PLACE", "OTHER", "UNDERAGE", "STALKING"]);
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "bad category", category: "BORING" })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("the queue puts underage and stalking reports first, whatever their age", async () => {
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const old = await publicStamp();
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: old.stamp.id, reason: "spam spam", category: "SPAM" });
    const urgent = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "USER", targetId: urgent.handle, reason: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" });
    const queue = await moderationQueue(prisma, mod);
    expect(queue.map((q) => q.targetType)).toEqual(["USER", "STAMP"]);
    expect(queue[0]!.flags[0]).toMatchObject({ category: "UNDERAGE" });
    expect(queue[1]!.flags[0]).toMatchObject({ category: "SPAM" });
  });

  it("the old content types are not accepted", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    for (const targetType of ["PHOTO", "COMMENT", "REPORT"]) {
      await expect(flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType, targetId: stamp.id, reason: "nope nope" })).rejects.toThrow();
    }
  });

  it("the hourly flag limit holds under parallel requests", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const flagger = await makeActor();
    const stamps = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 25 }, async () => makeStamp(await makeActor({ isPrivate: false }), venue.id)));
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(stamps.map((s) => flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: s.id, reason: "spam!" })));
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled")).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(await prisma.flag.count({ where: { flaggerId: flagger.userId } })).toBe(20);
  });

  it("flags over HTTP: a signed-in person can report a visible stamp", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, (await makeActor()).userId);
    const res = await flagRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/flags", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "not at this place" }) }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
  });
});

describe("rule 4: hidden content is hidden, never deleted; the log is append-only", () => {
  it("moderator hide keeps the row, resolves flags and logs; restore brings it back", async () => {
    const { stamp, author } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "abusive" });

    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE", note: "abusive" });
    const hidden = await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } });
    expect(hidden.hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(hidden.caption).toBe("crowd");
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, await makeActor(), author.handle)).stamps).toHaveLength(0);
    // Staff can still open it.
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, mod, stamp.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ stamp: { hidden: true } });
    expect(await prisma.flag.count({ where: { targetId: stamp.id, status: "OPEN" } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await moderationQueue(prisma, mod)).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await recentlyHidden(prisma, mod)).map((i) => i.targetId)).toContain(stamp.id);

    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "RESTORE", note: "context" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
    const log = await prisma.moderationEvent.findMany({ where: { targetId: stamp.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } });
    expect(log.map((e) => e.action)).toEqual(["FLAGGED", "HIDE", "RESTORE"]);
    expect(log[1]).toMatchObject({ actorId: mod.userId, note: "abusive" });
  });

  it("dismissing flags on an auto-hidden stamp restores it, and the whole history is kept", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" });
    const queue = await moderationQueue(prisma, mod);
    expect(queue[0]).toMatchObject({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id });
    expect(queue[0]!.content?.hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
    const actions = (await prisma.moderationEvent.findMany({ where: { targetId: stamp.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } })).map((e) => e.action);
    expect(actions).toEqual(["FLAGGED", "FLAGGED", "FLAGGED", "AUTO_HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE"]);
  });

  it("DISMISS does not undo a moderator's own HIDE; RESTORE does", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "RESTORE" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it("after a dismissal the same user can flag the stamp again", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const flagger = await makeActor();
    await flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "meh!" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "still bad" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("the moderation log can't be edited or deleted, even directly", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "spam!" });
    const ev = await prisma.moderationEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { targetId: stamp.id } });
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.update({ where: { id: ev.id }, data: { note: "nothing happened" } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.deleteMany({})).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
  });

  it("only moderators can see the queue or act; over HTTP too", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await expect(moderationQueue(prisma, await makeActor())).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(moderationQueue(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" }))).resolves.toEqual([]);
    await expect(moderate(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, (await makeActor()).userId);
    const res = await modRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/mod/actions", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" }) }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/neighborhoods.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { pointInRing } from "@/lib/geo";
import { overpassQuery, parseOverpass, placeKind, PLACE_KINDS, type OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";
import { assignNeighborhoods, importNeighborhoods, loadNeighborhoods, locate, MIN_SET, neighborhoodIndex } from "@/server/services/neighborhoods";
import { importOsmVenues } from "@/server/services/osm-import";
import { createStampWithProgress, removeStamp } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, pngBytes } from "./factories";

installMemoryStorage();

const square = (lng0: number, lat0: number, lng1: number, lat1: number): [number, number][] => [[lng0, lat0], [lng1, lat0], [lng1, lat1], [lng0, lat1], [lng0, lat0]];
const HOODS = [
  { code: "T001", name: "Testville", borough: "Manhattan", rings: [square(-74.0, 40.70, -73.96, 40.74)] },
  { code: "T002", name: "Otherton", borough: "Brooklyn", rings: [square(-73.96, 40.70, -73.92, 40.74)] },
];
const at = (i: number) => ({ lat: 40.71 + (i % 5) * 0.004, lng: -73.99 + Math.floor(i / 5) * 0.001 });

async function place(i: number, lat = at(i).lat, lng = at(i).lng) {
  const v = await prisma.venue.create({ data: { slug: `nb-${i}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`, name: `Place ${i}`, address: "", neighborhood: "x", lat, lng } });
  return v;
}
async function stamp(actor: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof makeActor>>, v: { id: string; lat: number; lng: number }, now = new Date()) {
  return createStampWithProgress(prisma, actor, v.id, { photos: [pngBytes()], meta: { caption: "x", lat: String(v.lat), lng: String(v.lng) } }, now);
}

describe("geometry", () => {
  it("point in ring", () => {
    const r = square(0, 0, 2, 2);
    expect(pointInRing({ lat: 1, lng: 1 }, r)).toBe(true);
    expect(pointInRing({ lat: 3, lng: 1 }, r)).toBe(false);
    expect(pointInRing({ lat: 1, lng: -0.1 }, r)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("the real NYC boundaries", () => {
  it("load, and the East Village's landmark parks fall where they should", async () => {
    const n = await importNeighborhoods(prisma);
    expect(n).toBeGreaterThan(150);
    expect(await importNeighborhoods(prisma)).toBe(n); // idempotent
    const list = await loadNeighborhoods(prisma);
    expect(locate(list, 40.72654, -73.98179)?.name).toBe("East Village"); // Tompkins Square Park
    expect(locate(list, 40.7185, -73.988)?.name).toBe("Lower East Side");
    expect(locate(list, 40.0, -70.0)).toBeNull(); // the ocean
    const file = JSON.parse(readFileSync("prisma/data/neighborhoods.json", "utf8")) as unknown[];
    expect(file.length).toBe(n);
    expect(readFileSync("prisma/data/neighborhoods.json").length).toBeLessThan(1_000_000);
  });

  it("files places under the neighborhood that contains them", async () => {
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const v = await place(1, 40.72654, -73.98179);
    expect(await assignNeighborhoods(prisma)).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: v.id } })).toMatchObject({ neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: expect.any(String) });
    expect(await assignNeighborhoods(prisma)).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("the importer covers more than bars", () => {
  const el = (id: number, tags: Record<string, string>, lat = 40.72, lng = -73.98): OverpassElement => ({ type: "node", id, lat, lon: lng, tags: { name: `N${id}`, ...tags } });
  it("keeps restaurants, cafes, parks, museums and bookstores, and drops what it doesn't know", async () => {
    for (const [key, vals] of Object.entries(PLACE_KINDS)) {
      expect(overpassQuery()).toContain(`"${key}"`);
      expect(vals.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
    expect(overpassQuery()).toMatch(/restaurant/);
    expect(overpassQuery()).toMatch(/park/);
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma, HOODS);
    const hoods = await loadNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const parsed = parseOverpass(
      [el(1, { amenity: "bar" }), el(2, { amenity: "restaurant" }), el(3, { amenity: "cafe" }), el(4, { leisure: "park" }), el(5, { tourism: "museum" }), el(6, { shop: "books" }),
        el(7, { amenity: "parking" }), el(8, { shop: "convenience" }), el(9, { amenity: "bar" }, 41.9, -73.0) /* outside every neighborhood */],
      (lat, lng) => locate(hoods, lat, lng),
    );
    expect(parsed.map((p) => [p.osmId, p.kind, p.neighborhoodId])).toEqual([
      ["node/1", "bar", "T001"], ["node/2", "restaurant", "T001"], ["node/3", "cafe", "T001"], ["node/4", "park", "T001"], ["node/5", "museum", "T001"], ["node/6", "books", "T001"],
    ]);
    expect(placeKind({ amenity: "parking" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("the import files each place under its neighborhood", async () => {
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma, HOODS);
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, [{ type: "node", id: 1, lat: 40.72, lon: -73.98, tags: { name: "Cafe One", amenity: "cafe" } }, { type: "node", id: 2, lat: 40.72, lon: -73.94, tags: { name: "Park Two", leisure: "park" } }]);
    const rows = await prisma.venue.findMany({ orderBy: { name: "asc" } });
    expect(rows.map((r) => [r.name, r.neighborhood, r.neighborhoodId])).toEqual([["Cafe One", "Testville", "T001"], ["Park Two", "Otherton", "T002"]]);
  });
});

describe("collections", () => {
  it("counts visited places over total, earns a completion once, and never takes it back", async () => {
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma, HOODS);
    const me = await makeActor();
    const places = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) places.push(await place(i));
    await place(20, 40.72, -73.94); // in the other neighborhood
    await assignNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const t0 = Date.now() - 5 * 3600_000;
    const first = await stamp(me, places[0]!, new Date(t0));
    expect(first.progress).toMatchObject({ neighborhoodId: "T001", visited: 1, total: 4, complete: false, justCompleted: false, newPlace: true });
    // A second stamp at the same place later doesn't add a place.
    expect((await stamp(me, places[0]!, new Date(t0 + 3600_000))).progress).toMatchObject({ visited: 1, newPlace: false });
    await stamp(me, places[1]!, new Date(t0 + 2 * 3600_000));
    expect((await stamp(me, places[2]!, new Date(t0 + 3 * 3600_000))).progress).toMatchObject({ visited: 3, total: 4, complete: false });
    const done = await stamp(me, places[3]!, new Date(t0 + 4 * 3600_000));
    expect(done.progress).toMatchObject({ visited: 4, total: 4, complete: true, justCompleted: true });
    expect(await prisma.neighborhoodCompletion.count({ where: { userId: me.userId } })).toBe(1);
    // A new place opens in the neighborhood: the earned marker stays.
    await place(5);
    await assignNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const idx = await neighborhoodIndex(prisma, me);
    expect(idx.neighborhoods.find((n) => n.neighborhoodId === "T001")).toMatchObject({ visited: 4, total: 5, complete: true, percent: 80, completedAt: expect.any(Date) });
    expect(idx.totals).toEqual({ neighborhoodsStarted: 1, neighborhoodsComplete: 1 });
    // Removing a stamp lowers visited but the marker is earned for good.
    await removeStamp(prisma, me, done.stamp.id);
    expect((await neighborhoodIndex(prisma, me)).neighborhoods.find((n) => n.neighborhoodId === "T001")).toMatchObject({ visited: 3, complete: true });
  });

  it("a set needs at least 3 places, the index lists started neighborhoods first, and it is owner only", async () => {
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma, HOODS);
    expect(MIN_SET).toBe(3);
    const me = await makeActor();
    const other = await makeActor();
    const a = await place(0), b = await place(1);
    const c = await place(30, 40.72, -73.94);
    await assignNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const r = await stamp(me, a);
    expect(r.progress).toMatchObject({ visited: 1, total: 2, complete: false });
    await stamp(me, b, new Date(Date.now() + 3600_000));
    const two = (await neighborhoodIndex(prisma, me)).neighborhoods;
    expect(two.map((n) => n.neighborhoodId)).toEqual(["T001", "T002"]);
    expect(two[0]).toMatchObject({ visited: 2, total: 2, complete: false }); // 2 places is not a set
    expect((await neighborhoodIndex(prisma, other)).neighborhoods.every((n) => n.visited === 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(c.neighborhoodId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("App Review stamps never count, and a place with no neighborhood has no progress", async () => {
    await importNeighborhoods(prisma, HOODS);
    const reviewer = await makeActor();
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: reviewer.userId }, data: { geofenceExempt: true } });
    const v = await place(0);
    const lone = await place(40, 10, 10);
    await assignNeighborhoods(prisma);
    const out = await createStampWithProgress(prisma, { ...reviewer, geofenceExempt: true }, v.id, { photos: [pngBytes()], meta: { caption: "x", lat: "0", lng: "0" } });
    expect(out.progress).toBeNull();
    expect((await neighborhoodIndex(prisma, reviewer)).neighborhoods.every((n) => n.visited === 0)).toBe(true);
    const me = await makeActor();
    expect((await stamp(me, lone)).progress).toBeNull();
    expect((await stamp(me, v)).stamp.place.neighborhoodId).toBe("T001");
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/osm-import.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import type { OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";
import { importOsmVenues } from "@/server/services/osm-import";
import { GET as placesRoute } from "@/app/api/places/route";

const sample = (JSON.parse(readFileSync("tests/fixtures/overpass-sample.json", "utf8")) as { elements: OverpassElement[] }).elements;

describe("venues:import", () => {
  it("creates places with the default geofence, and is idempotent", async () => {
    expect(await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample)).toMatchObject({ fetched: 5, created: 5, updated: 0 });
    expect(await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample)).toMatchObject({ created: 0, updated: 0, unchanged: 5 });
    const v = await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } });
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ slug: "test-tavern-n1001", geofenceMeters: 100 });
    expect((await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "way/2001" } })).slug).toBe("test-tavern-w2001");
  });

  it("slugs don't change on re-import even if names change; coordinates and names follow OSM", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const renamed = sample.map((e) => (e.id === 1001 ? { ...e, lat: 40.7266, tags: { ...e.tags, name: "Renamed In OSM" } } : e.id === 1002 ? { ...e, tags: { ...e.tags, name: "Basement Club NYC" } } : e));
    const r = await importOsmVenues(prisma, renamed);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ updated: 2 });
    expect(await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } })).toMatchObject({ name: "Renamed In OSM", slug: "test-tavern-n1001", lat: 40.7266 });
    expect(await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1002" } })).toMatchObject({ name: "Basement Club NYC", slug: "basement-club" });
  });

  it("adds ODbL attribution to the places API", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const body = await (await placesRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), undefined as never)).json();
    expect(body.attribution).toEqual({ text: "© OpenStreetMap contributors", url: "https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright", license: "ODbL-1.0" });
    expect(body.places.every((v: { fromOsm: boolean }) => v.fromOsm)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("recomputes the neighborhood when coordinates move, and rolls back on failure", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const moved = sample.map((e) => (e.id === 1001 ? { ...e, lat: 40.7185, lon: -73.988 } : e));
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, moved);
    expect((await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } })).neighborhood).toBe("Lower East Side");
    // A slug clash with an existing place makes the whole import fail with nothing written.
    await prisma.venue.create({ data: { slug: "new-bar-n7777", name: "Squatter", address: "", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 } });
    const before = await prisma.venue.count();
    const clash: OverpassElement[] = [...sample, { type: "node", id: 7777, lat: 40.7262, lon: -73.9848, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "New Bar" } }, { type: "node", id: 7778, lat: 40.7263, lon: -73.9849, tags: { amenity: "pub", name: "Brand New" } }, { type: "node", id: 7779, lat: 40.7201, lon: -73.9880, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "New Bar" } }];
    await expect(importOsmVenues(prisma, clash)).rejects.toThrow(/Slug collision/);
    expect(await prisma.venue.count()).toBe(before);
  });

  it("no attribution when no place came from OSM", async () => {
    await prisma.venue.create({ data: { slug: "manual", name: "Manual", address: "1 St", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 } });
    const body = await (await placesRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), undefined as never)).json();
    expect(body.attribution).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/personal-mode.test.ts
====================================================================================================

// SOCIAL_ENABLED off (the default for Nostos v1): strictly personal. Every social route is a 404 and a passport is
// visible only to its owner. (The rest of the suite runs with the flag on: see vitest.config.ts.)
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { socialEnabled } from "@/server/social";
import { getStamp, listUserStamps } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { getProfile } from "@/server/services/people";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, makeStamp, makeVenue } from "./factories";

installMemoryStorage();

import * as feed from "@/app/api/feed/route";
import * as flags from "@/app/api/flags/route";
import * as activity from "@/app/api/me/activity/route";
import * as meBlocks from "@/app/api/me/blocks/route";
import * as reqs from "@/app/api/me/follow-requests/route";
import * as reqOne from "@/app/api/me/follow-requests/[handle]/route";
import * as approveAll from "@/app/api/me/follow-requests/approve-all/route";
import * as meFollower from "@/app/api/me/followers/[handle]/route";
import * as meFollowing from "@/app/api/me/following/route";
import * as like from "@/app/api/stamps/[id]/like/route";
import * as search from "@/app/api/users/route";
import * as block from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/block/route";
import * as follow from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/follow/route";
import * as followers from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/followers/route";
import * as following from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/following/route";
import * as userR from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/route";
import * as userStamps from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/stamps/route";
import * as userPlaces from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/places/route";
import * as stampR from "@/app/api/stamps/[id]/route";

type H = (req: Request, ctx: never) => Promise<Response>;
beforeEach(() => vi.stubEnv("SOCIAL_ENABLED", "0"));
afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllEnvs());

async function call(h: H, method: string, token: string, params: Record<string, string> = {}, query = "") {
  const res = await h(new Request(`http://localhost/x${query}`, { method, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "content-type": "application/json" }, body: method === "GET" ? undefined : "{}" }), { params: Promise.resolve(params) } as never);
  return res;
}

describe("the flag", () => {
