    expect(new Set(all).size).toBe(STAMPS_PAGE + 5);
    expect([...all].sort()).toEqual([...ids].sort());
    const times = [...first.stamps, ...second.stamps].map((s) => s.createdAt.getTime());
    expect([...times].sort((a, b) => b - a)).toEqual(times);
    await expect(listFeed(prisma, me, "garbage")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    await expect(listFeed(prisma, null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("only shows people you follow: not yourself, not strangers, not people you unfollowed", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const a = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stranger = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "mine" });
    await makeStamp(stranger, venue.id, { caption: "stranger" });
    await makeStamp(a, venue.id, { caption: "a" });
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    await follow(prisma, me, a.handle);
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["a"]);
    await prisma.follow.deleteMany({});
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("the passport grid and places view count and group stamps by place", async () => {
    const [a, b] = [await makeVenue(), await makeVenue()];
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const t = Date.now() - 3 * 3600_000;
    await makeStamp(me, a.id, { caption: "first at a", now: new Date(t) });
    await makeStamp(me, a.id, { caption: "second at a", now: new Date(t + 3600_000) });
    const last = await makeStamp(me, b.id, { caption: "at b", now: new Date(t + 2 * 3600_000) });
    const viewer = await makeActor();
    const grid = await listUserStamps(prisma, viewer, me.handle);
    expect(grid.stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["first at a", "second at a", "at b"]);
    expect(grid.stamps.map((s) => [s.page, s.slot])).toEqual([[1, 1], [1, 2], [1, 3]]);
    const { places } = await listUserPlaces(prisma, viewer, me.handle);
    expect(places.map((p) => [p.place.slug, p.stamps])).toEqual([[b.slug, 1], [a.slug, 2]]);
    expect(places[0]!.latestStamp.id).toBe(last.id);
    expect(places[1]!.latestStamp.caption).toBe("second at a");
  });
});

describe("places", () => {
  it("lists places near a position, nearest first, flagging the ones whose geofence you're inside", async () => {
    const here = await makeVenue({ lat: INSIDE.lat, lng: INSIDE.lng });
    const close = await makeVenue({ lat: INSIDE.lat + 0.002, lng: INSIDE.lng });
    await makeVenue({ lat: OUTSIDE.lat + 0.1, lng: OUTSIDE.lng });
    const near = await nearbyPlaces(prisma, INSIDE);
    expect(near.map((p) => p.slug)).toEqual([here.slug, close.slug]);
    expect(near.map((p) => p.inside)).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect(near[0]!.distanceM).toBe(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify(near)).not.toMatch(/osmId/);
  });
});

describe("pages and slots", () => {
  it("numbers stamps oldest first, three to a page, from one shared function", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const t = Date.now() - 10 * 3600_000;
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) ids.push((await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id, { caption: `n${i}`, now: new Date(t + i * 3600_000) })).id);
    const grid = await listUserStamps(prisma, me, me.handle);
    expect(grid.stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["n0", "n1", "n2", "n3", "n4", "n5", "n6"]);
    expect(grid.stamps.map((s) => `${s.page}.${s.slot}`)).toEqual(["1.1", "1.2", "1.3", "2.1", "2.2", "2.3", "3.1"]);
    // The same numbers appear on the single-stamp response.
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, me, ids[4]!)).stamp).toMatchObject({ page: 2, slot: 2 });
    // The oldest stays on page 1 when newer ones come; removing one closes the gap behind it.
    await removeStamp(prisma, me, ids[1]!);
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, me, ids[0]!)).stamp).toMatchObject({ page: 1, slot: 1 });
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, me, ids[2]!)).stamp).toMatchObject({ page: 1, slot: 2 });
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, me, ids[6]!)).stamp).toMatchObject({ page: 2, slot: 3 });
    // Staff looking at a removed stamp see no page.
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), ids[1]!)).stamp).toMatchObject({ page: null, slot: null });
  });

  it("pages the passport oldest first with an after cursor and no gaps", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const base = Date.now() - 90 * 60_000;
    for (let i = 0; i < STAMPS_PAGE + 4; i++) await prisma.photo.create({ data: { userId: me.userId, venueId: venue.id, caption: `s${i}`, ...INSIDE, createdAt: new Date(base + i * 1000) } });
    const first = await listUserStamps(prisma, me, me.handle);
    const second = await listUserStamps(prisma, me, me.handle, first.nextAfter);
    expect(first.stamps).toHaveLength(STAMPS_PAGE);
    expect([...first.stamps, ...second.stamps].map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(Array.from({ length: STAMPS_PAGE + 4 }, (_, i) => `s${i}`));
    expect(second.nextAfter).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("my place page", () => {
  it("stacks my stamps at a place by night, oldest first, and says whether I'm inside right now", async () => {
    const { getMyPlacePage, stampDay } = await import("@/server/services/stamps");
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const other = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const d1 = new Date("2026-09-20T23:30:00Z"), d2 = new Date("2026-09-20T23:50:00Z"), d3 = new Date("2026-09-27T23:30:00Z");
    await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "a", now: d1 });
    await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "b", now: d2 });
    await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "c", now: d3 });
    await makeStamp(other, v.id, { caption: "not mine" });
    await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id, { caption: "elsewhere" });
    const page = await getMyPlacePage(prisma, me, v.slug, INSIDE);
    expect(page.stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
    expect(page.visits.map((x) => [x.day, x.stamps.length])).toEqual([[stampDay(d1), 2], [stampDay(d3), 1]]);
    expect(page.inside).toBe(true);
    expect(page.distanceM).toBeLessThanOrEqual(v.geofenceMeters);
    expect((await getMyPlacePage(prisma, me, v.id, OUTSIDE)).inside).toBe(false);
    expect(await getMyPlacePage(prisma, me, v.id)).toMatchObject({ inside: null, distanceM: null });
    expect(JSON.stringify(page)).not.toContain("not mine");
    await expect(getMyPlacePage(prisma, null, v.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    await expect(getMyPlacePage(prisma, me, "nope")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/token-auth.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { signup } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { hashToken, SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { POST as loginRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/login/route";
import { POST as signupRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/signup/route";
import { POST as logoutRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/logout/route";
import { POST as logoutAllRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/logout-all/route";
import { GET as meRoute } from "@/app/api/me/route";
import { PUT as followRoute } from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/follow/route";
import { makeUser } from "./factories";

const json = (body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  ({ method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }) as RequestInit;
const none = undefined as never;

async function iosLogin(email = "ios@x.dev") {
  await signup(prisma, { email, password: "long enough pw", displayName: "I", handle: email.split("@")[0]!.replace(/\W/g, "_"), acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
  const res = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email, password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
  return { res, body: (await res.json()) as { token: string; expiresAt: string; user: { handle: string } } };
}

describe("bearer tokens for iOS", () => {
  it("login with X-Client: ios returns { token, user } and sets no cookie; the session is stored hashed as IOS", async () => {
    const { res, body } = await iosLogin();
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
    expect(body.token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}$/);
    expect(body.user.handle).toBe("ios");
    const row = await prisma.session.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: hashToken(body.token) } });
    expect(row.client).toBe("IOS");
    expect(await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: body.token } })).toBeNull();
    expect(row.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()).toBeGreaterThan(80 * 86_400_000);
  });

  it("signup with X-Client: ios also returns a token; web signup is unchanged (cookie, no token)", async () => {
    const ios = await signupRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/signup", json({ email: "s1@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "S", handle: "s_one", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
    expect(ios.status).toBe(201);
    expect((await ios.json()).token).toBeTruthy();
    const web = await signupRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/signup", json({ email: "s2@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "S", handle: "s_two", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" })), none);
    expect(web.headers.get("set-cookie")).toMatch(/lc_session=/);
    expect((await web.json()).token).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("GET /api/me works with a bearer token and returns role and settings", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const res = await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${body.token}` } }), none);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ user: { handle: "ios", role: "USER" }, settings: { geofenceExempt: false } });
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me"), none)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("a bad bearer token is unauthenticated even when a valid cookie is also sent", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const res = await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer not-a-real-token-000000000", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}` } }), none);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe("CSRF check applies to cookie auth only", () => {
  it("bearer requests from any Origin are accepted; the same cookie request from a foreign Origin is rejected", async () => {
    const target = await makeUser({ isPrivate: false });
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const ctx = { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: target.handle }) };
    const url = `http://localhost/api/users/${target.handle}/follow`;
    const put = (headers: Record<string, string>) => new Request(url, { method: "PUT", headers });
    const viaBearer = await followRoute(put({ authorization: `Bearer ${body.token}`, origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" }), ctx);
    expect(viaBearer.status).toBe(200);
    const viaCookie = await followRoute(put({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}`, origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" }), ctx);
    expect(viaCookie.status).toBe(403);
  });
});

describe("logout", () => {
  it("revokes only the current session; logout-all revokes every session", async () => {
    const { body: a } = await iosLogin();
    const second = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
    const b = (await second.json()) as { token: string };
    const auth = (t: string) => ({ authorization: `Bearer ${t}` });

    expect((await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: auth(a.token) }), none)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(a.token) }), none)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(b.token) }), none)).status).toBe(200);

    const third = (await (await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none)).json()) as object;
    expect(third).not.toHaveProperty("token");
    const before = await prisma.session.count(); // signup's session + b + the web login
    expect(before).toBe(3);
    const res = await logoutAllRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout-all", { method: "POST", headers: auth(b.token) }), none);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true, revoked: before });
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(b.token) }), none)).status).toBe(401);
    expect(await prisma.session.count()).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("token hardening", () => {
  const me = (headers: Record<string, string>) => meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers }), none);

  it("an expired bearer token gets 401 invalid_token with WWW-Authenticate, and the row is deleted", async () => {
    const u = await prisma.user.create({ data: { email: "e@x.dev", handle: "eee", displayName: "e", passwordHash: "x" } });
    const { createSession } = await import("@/lib/auth/session");
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, u.id, { client: "IOS", now: new Date(Date.now() - 91 * 86_400_000) });
    const res = await me({ authorization: `Bearer ${token}` });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ error: "invalid_token" });
    expect(res.headers.get("www-authenticate")).toMatch(/invalid_token/);
    expect(await prisma.session.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("a dead bearer token is rejected on public routes too (no silent anonymous fallback)", async () => {
    const { GET: places } = await import("@/app/api/places/route");
    expect((await places(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), none)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await places(new Request("http://localhost/api/places", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer revoked-token-revoked-token-000" } }), none)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("accepts the auth scheme case-insensitively", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    expect((await me({ authorization: `bearer ${body.token}` })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await me({ authorization: `BEARER   ${body.token}` })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("binds tokens to their channel: iOS tokens aren't cookies and web tokens aren't bearer tokens", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    expect((await me({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}` })).status).toBe(401);
    const web = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none);
    const cookieToken = /lc_session=([^;]+)/.exec(web.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "")![1]!;
    const row = await prisma.session.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: hashToken(cookieToken) } });
    expect(row.client).toBe("WEB");
    const ttlDays = (row.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()) / 86_400_000;
    expect(ttlDays).toBeGreaterThan(29);
    expect(ttlDays).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30);
    expect((await me({ authorization: `Bearer ${cookieToken}` })).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await me({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${cookieToken}` })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("X-Client matching tolerates case and whitespace", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "w@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "W", handle: "www", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const res = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "w@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": " IOS " })), none);
    expect((await res.json()).token).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("an invalid bearer with a foreign Origin gets 401, not a CSRF pass", async () => {
    const target = await makeUser({ isPrivate: false });
    const res = await followRoute(
      new Request(`http://localhost/api/users/${target.handle}/follow`, { method: "PUT", headers: { authorization: "Bearer forged-token-forged-token-000", origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" } }),
      { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: target.handle }) },
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await prisma.follow.count()).toBe(0);
  });

  it("logout: 401 for a dead bearer; cookie logout revokes the cookie session", async () => {
    const dead = await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: "Bearer dead-token-dead-token-dead-00" } }), none);
    expect(dead.status).toBe(401);
    await signup(prisma, { email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "C", handle: "ccc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const web = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none);
    const cookieToken = /lc_session=([^;]+)/.exec(web.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "")![1]!;
    const out = await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${cookieToken}` } }), none);
    expect(out.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: hashToken(cookieToken) } })).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/age.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ageOn, isOldEnough, parseBirthdate } from "@/lib/age";

// 2026-10-02 12:00 in New York is 16:00Z.
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z");

describe("parseBirthdate", () => {
  it("accepts real dates only", () => {
    expect(parseBirthdate("1990-05-05")).toEqual({ y: 1990, m: 5, d: 5 });
    expect(parseBirthdate("2000-02-29")).toEqual({ y: 2000, m: 2, d: 29 });
    for (const bad of ["2001-02-29", "2001-13-01", "2001-00-10", "2001-04-31", "05/05/1990", "1990-5-5", "", "yesterday", "1990-05-05T00:00:00Z"]) {
      expect(parseBirthdate(bad), bad).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe("ageOn", () => {
  it("counts a birthday from its own date", () => {
    const birth = { y: 2005, m: 10, d: 2 };
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 1 })).toBe(20);
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 2 })).toBe(21);
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 3 })).toBe(21);
  });
});

describe("isOldEnough (21+, New York today)", () => {
  it("is true on the 21st birthday and false the day before", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-02", NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-03", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-01", NOW)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("handles leap-day birthdays", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2004-02-29", new Date("2025-03-01T17:00:00Z"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isOldEnough("2008-02-29", new Date("2029-02-28T17:00:00Z"))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("uses the New York date, not UTC: 11pm in New York is already the next day in UTC", () => {
    // 2026-10-02 23:30 in New York (EDT) is 2026-10-03 03:30Z. The 21st birthday on the 3rd is not here yet.
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-03", new Date("2026-10-03T03:30:00Z"))).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-02", new Date("2026-10-03T03:30:00Z"))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("refuses future dates, nonsense and absurd ages", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2030-01-01", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("1800-01-01", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("nope", NOW)).toBe(false);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/content-filter.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { containsObjectionable } from "@/lib/content-filter";
import { assertCleanText } from "@/server/errors";

describe("objectionable content filter", () => {
  it("catches slurs and explicit terms", () => {
    for (const t of ["what a faggot", "total retard", "Kike", "nice pussy", "porn night", "coons", "whores"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("sees through leetspeak, accents, spacing, punctuation and letter stretching", () => {
    for (const t of ["n1gg3r", "f@gg0t", "r3t@rd", "f a g", "f.a.g.g.o.t", "faaaggggot", "what a w h o r e", "s l u t", "ćunt"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("catches unambiguous slurs inside run-together handles", () => {
    for (const t of ["xxniggerxx", "the_faggot_king", "Wetback99", "ilovecumshots", "big_n1gga_energy"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("does not flag innocent words that contain a short term (Scunthorpe problem)", () => {
    for (const t of [
      "Great cocktails tonight", "spicy margaritas", "a raccoon by the bins", "Scunthorpe United fans",
      "fire retardant curtains", "cocktail hour", "cumin in the fries", "Dickens themed trivia", "the grape soda", "Therapist night out",
      "Shitake mushrooms", "this place is fucking packed", "Pakistani food nearby", "cocoon lounge",
      // regressions from review: letter-collapsing and cross-word substrings used to flag these
      "pros and cons", "con artist", "Nigerian food spot", "zucchini gazpacho", "mini garden bar", "drag heads welcome",
      "wet backyard patio", "Cock & Bull", "cum laude", "someone tried to rape my friend here, be careful",
    ]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(false);
  });
  it("handles empty input", () => {
    expect(containsObjectionable("")).toBe(false);
    expect(containsObjectionable(null)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("assertCleanText throws a clear 400 naming the field", () => {
    expect(() => assertCleanText({ caption: "fine", comment: "you retard" })).toThrow(/Your comment contains language we don't allow/);
    try {
      assertCleanText({ handle: "sl_ut" });
    } catch (e) {
      expect(e).toMatchObject({ status: 400, code: "objectionable_content" });
    }
    expect(() => assertCleanText({ comment: "Packed, 20 min line" })).not.toThrow();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/contract-requests.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ZodTypeAny } from "zod";
import * as C from "@/contracts/api";
import { signupSchema } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { flagSchema, moderateSchema } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { deleteAccountSchema } from "@/server/services/account";
import { stampEditSchema } from "@/server/services/stamps";

/** The app validates with the contract's request schemas; they must accept and reject exactly what the server does. */
function agree(contract: ZodTypeAny, server: ZodTypeAny, samples: unknown[]) {
  for (const s of samples) expect(contract.safeParse(s).success, JSON.stringify(s)).toBe(server.safeParse(s).success);
}

describe("contract request schemas agree with server validation", () => {
  it("signup", () =>
    agree(C.SignupRequest, signupSchema, [
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "abc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "short", displayName: "A", handle: "abc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "a", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "ABC_ok", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "abc", acceptTerms: false, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "nope", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "abc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" },
      { email: "a@b.co", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "A", handle: "abc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05", extra: 1 },
    ]));
  it("flags, moderation, deletion", () => {
    agree(C.FlagRequest, flagSchema, [
      { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "p1", reason: "fake photo" }, { targetType: "USER", targetId: "someone", reason: "abuse" },
      { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "p1", reason: "no" }, { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: "p1", reason: "fake photo" }, { targetType: "COMMENT", targetId: "c", reason: "hmm hmm" },
      { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "", reason: "fake" },
      { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "p1", reason: "not here", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE" }, { targetType: "USER", targetId: "u", reason: "too young", category: "UNDERAGE" },
      { targetType: "USER", targetId: "u", reason: "follows me", category: "STALKING" }, { targetType: "USER", targetId: "u", reason: "nope nope", category: "BORING" },
    ]);
    agree(C.ModActionRequest, moderateSchema.innerType(), [
      { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "p", action: "HIDE" }, { targetType: "USER", targetId: "u", action: "SUSPEND", note: "abuse" }, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: "p", action: "DELETE" },
      { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: "p", action: "HIDE" },
    ]);
    agree(C.DeleteAccountRequest, deleteAccountSchema, [{ password: "x" }, { password: "" }, {}]);
  });
  it("stamp edits", () =>
    agree(C.StampEditRequest, stampEditSchema, [
      { caption: "new caption" }, { caption: "x".repeat(280) }, { caption: "x".repeat(281) }, {}, { caption: "ok", extra: 1 },
      { song: null }, { song: { title: "T", artist: "A" } }, { song: { title: "T" } }, { song: { title: "", artist: "A" } },
      { song: { title: "T", artist: "A", artUrl: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/x/300x300bb.jpg", sourceId: "123" } },
      { song: { title: "T", artist: "A", artUrl: "https://evil.example/pixel.gif" } }, { song: { title: "T", artist: "A", artUrl: "http://a.mzstatic.com/x.jpg" } },
      { song: { title: "T", artist: "A", artUrl: null } },
    ]));
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/layout.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { LAYOUT_VECTORS, nextSlot, pageAndSlot, spreadOfPage } from "@/lib/layout";

describe("passport layout", () => {
  it.each(LAYOUT_VECTORS)("index %i is page %i slot %i", (index, page, slot) => expect(pageAndSlot(index)).toEqual({ page, slot }));

  it("fills three slots a page, in order, never skipping", () => {
    const seen = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => pageAndSlot(i));
    expect(seen.map((s) => s.slot)).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 1, 2, 3, 1, 2, 3, 1, 2, 3]);
    expect(seen.map((s) => s.page)).toEqual([1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3, 3, 4, 4, 4]);
  });

  it("the first stamps stay on page 1 however many come later", () => {
    for (const total of [3, 10, 500]) expect(Array.from({ length: Math.min(total, 3) }, (_, i) => pageAndSlot(i).page)).toEqual(Array(Math.min(total, 3)).fill(1));
  });

  it("two pages to a spread", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(spreadOfPage)).toEqual([1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3]);
  });

  it("the next stamp lands in the next empty slot", () => {
    expect(nextSlot(0)).toEqual({ page: 1, slot: 1 });
    expect(nextSlot(5)).toEqual({ page: 2, slot: 3 });
    expect(nextSlot(6)).toEqual({ page: 3, slot: 1 });
  });

  it("refuses nonsense", () => {
    for (const bad of [-1, 1.5, Number.NaN]) expect(() => pageAndSlot(bad)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/ops.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isAuthorizedCron } from "@/server/cron";
import { clientIp } from "@/server/http";
import { assertSeedAllowed } from "../../prisma/seed-guard";

describe("cron auth", () => {
  const req = (h?: string) => new Request("http://x/api/cron/reputation", { headers: h ? { authorization: h } : {} });
  const secret = "s3cret-s3cret-s3cret";
  it("requires the exact bearer secret", () => {
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req(`Bearer ${secret}`), secret)).toBe(true);
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req(`Bearer ${secret}x`), secret)).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req(secret), secret)).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req(), secret)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("is closed when no (or a weak) secret is configured", () => {
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req("Bearer "), "")).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthorizedCron(req("Bearer short"), "short")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("client IP", () => {
  it("uses the first X-Forwarded-For hop, then X-Real-IP", () => {
    expect(clientIp(new Request("http://x", { headers: { "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.7, 10.0.0.1" } }))).toBe("203.0.113.7");
    expect(clientIp(new Request("http://x", { headers: { "x-real-ip": "198.51.100.2" } }))).toBe("198.51.100.2");
    expect(clientIp(new Request("http://x"))).toBe("unknown");
  });
});

describe("production seed guard", () => {
  const local = "postgresql://u:p@localhost:5435/nostos";
  const neon = "postgresql://u:p@ep-cool-123.us-east-2.aws.neon.tech/neondb";
  it("refuses production however it is signalled", () => {
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "production", DATABASE_URL: local })).toThrow(/production/);
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "Production", DATABASE_URL: local })).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "preview", VERCEL_ENV: "production", DATABASE_URL: neon })).toThrow(/VERCEL_ENV/);
  });
  it("fails safe when APP_ENV is missing and the database is remote", () => {
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ DATABASE_URL: neon })).toThrow(/not local/);
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ DATABASE_URL: local, DIRECT_URL: neon })).toThrow(/not local/);
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "staging", DATABASE_URL: local })).toThrow(/unknown/);
  });
  it("allows local development and explicit preview", () => {
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ DATABASE_URL: local })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "development", DATABASE_URL: local })).not.toThrow();
    const strong = { DEV_SEED_PASSWORD: "a-long-preview-password", APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD: "review-pass-123" };
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "preview", VERCEL_ENV: "preview", DATABASE_URL: neon, ...strong })).not.toThrow();
  });
  it("refuses a preview seed with the public dev password or no App Review password", () => {
    const neonUrl = "postgresql://u:p@ep-cool-123.neon.tech/neondb";
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "preview", DATABASE_URL: neonUrl })).toThrow(/DEV_SEED_PASSWORD/);
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "preview", DATABASE_URL: neonUrl, DEV_SEED_PASSWORD: "lastcall-dev" })).toThrow(/DEV_SEED_PASSWORD/);
    expect(() => assertSeedAllowed({ APP_ENV: "preview", DATABASE_URL: neonUrl, DEV_SEED_PASSWORD: "a-long-preview-password" })).toThrow(/APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD/);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/osm.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { assignSlugs, neighborhoodOf, overpassQuery, parseOverpass, slugify, type OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";

const sample = (JSON.parse(readFileSync("tests/fixtures/overpass-sample.json", "utf8")) as { elements: OverpassElement[] }).elements;

describe("OSM parsing", () => {
  it("queries bars, restaurants, cafes, parks, museums and bookstores in a box covering both neighborhoods", () => {
    const q = overpassQuery();
    expect(q).toContain('"amenity"~"^(bar|pub|nightclub|restaurant|cafe|');
    expect(q).toContain('"leisure"~"^(park|garden|playground)$"');
    expect(q).toContain('"tourism"~');
    expect(q).toContain('"shop"~"^(books)$"');
    expect(q).toContain("40.7115,-73.9945,40.7345,-73.972");
    expect(q).toContain("out center tags");
  });

  it("keeps named places (bars, restaurants...) inside EV/LES, uses way centers, blank address when unmapped", () => {
    const venues = parseOverpass(sample);
    expect(venues.map((v) => v.osmId)).toEqual(["node/1001", "node/1002", "node/1005", "node/1006", "way/2001"]);
    expect(venues.find((v) => v.osmId === "way/2001")).toMatchObject({ lat: 40.721, lng: -73.986, kind: "pub" });
    expect(venues.find((v) => v.osmId === "node/1001")).toMatchObject({ address: "12 Avenue A", neighborhood: "East Village" });
    expect(venues.find((v) => v.osmId === "node/1002")).toMatchObject({ address: "", neighborhood: "Lower East Side" });
  });

  it("classifies real venues from a live Overpass pull (2026-09-24)", () => {
    const cases: [string, number, number, string | null][] = [
      ["Beauty Bar, 231 E 14th St", 40.7329, -73.9858, "East Village"],
      ["Webster Hall, 125 E 11th St", 40.7318, -73.9891, "East Village"],
      ["Yawning Cobra, 356 Bowery", 40.7269, -73.9919, "East Village"],
      ["Swift Hibernian Lounge, 34 E 4th St", 40.7273, -73.9923, "East Village"],
      ["Nuyorican Poets Cafe, E 3rd St", 40.7219, -73.9832, "East Village"],
      ["Home Sweet Home, 131 Chrystie St", 40.7195, -73.9934, "Lower East Side"],
      ["David's Bar, 76 Forsyth St", 40.7172, -73.9933, "Lower East Side"],
      ["The Highlight Room, 145 Bowery", 40.7189, -73.9941, "Lower East Side"],
      ["169 Bar, 169 East Broadway", 40.7139, -73.9898, "Lower East Side"],
      ["Reservoir, 70 University Pl", 40.7332, -73.9936, null],
      ["Joe's Pub, 425 Lafayette St", 40.7292, -73.9916, null],
      ["Great Jones Distilling, 686 Broadway", 40.7279, -73.9942, null],
      ["Jac's on Bond, 26 Bond St", 40.7265, -73.9936, null],
      ["Mr. Fong's, 40 Market St", 40.7124, -73.9943, null],
      ["Sake Bar Asoko, 127 East Broadway", 40.7138, -73.992, null],
    ];
    for (const [name, lat, lng, want] of cases) expect(neighborhoodOf(lat, lng), name).toBe(want);
  });

  it("uses real neighborhood outlines: NoHo/Village and Chinatown are out, and Houston splits EV/LES", () => {
    expect(neighborhoodOf(40.7219, -73.9832)).toBe("East Village"); // E 3rd St between Aves B & C
    expect(neighborhoodOf(40.7185, -73.988)).toBe("Lower East Side"); // Delancey & Essex
    expect(neighborhoodOf(40.7337, -73.9934)).toBeNull(); // University Pl (Village)
    expect(neighborhoodOf(40.7263, -73.9942)).toBeNull(); // Lafayette / Bond (NoHo)
    expect(neighborhoodOf(40.7137, -73.9937)).toBeNull(); // East Broadway (Chinatown)
    const outside: OverpassElement = { type: "node", id: 9, lat: 40.7337, lon: -73.9934, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "Village Spot" } };
    expect(parseOverpass([outside])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("collapses the same venue mapped twice (node + building) deterministically", () => {
    const dup: OverpassElement[] = [
      { type: "way", id: 247, center: { lat: 40.7266, lon: -73.9846 }, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "Yawning Cobra", "addr:housenumber": "1", "addr:street": "Avenue A" } },
      { type: "node", id: 1233, lat: 40.7265, lon: -73.9845, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "yawning  cobra" } },
      { type: "node", id: 555, lat: 40.7201, lon: -73.9888, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "Yawning Cobra" } }, // far away: a different bar
    ];
    const out = parseOverpass(dup);
    expect(out.map((v) => v.osmId).sort()).toEqual(["node/555", "way/247"]); // the one with an address wins
    expect(parseOverpass([...dup].reverse())).toEqual(out);
  });

  it("makes deterministic slugs from OSM data alone", () => {
    expect(slugify("Café Ñandú & Friends")).toBe("cafe-nandu-and-friends");
    expect(slugify("McSorley's Old Ale House")).toBe("mcsorleys-old-ale-house");
    expect(slugify("!!!")).toBe("venue");
    const venues = parseOverpass(sample);
    const slugs = assignSlugs(venues);
    // Two venues named "Test Tavern": both get suffixes, independent of import order or DB state.
    expect(slugs.get("node/1001")).toBe("test-tavern-n1001");
    expect(slugs.get("way/2001")).toBe("test-tavern-w2001");
    expect(slugs.get("node/1002")).toBe("basement-club");
    expect(assignSlugs([...venues].reverse())).toEqual(slugs);
    expect(assignSlugs(venues, new Set(["basement-club"])).get("node/1002")).toBe("basement-club-n1002");
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/password.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword, SCRYPT_PARAMS } from "@/lib/auth/password";

describe("password hashing (scrypt)", () => {
  it("round-trips and encodes parameters", async () => {
    const hash = await hashPassword("correct horse battery");
    expect(hash.startsWith(`scrypt$${SCRYPT_PARAMS.N}$${SCRYPT_PARAMS.r}$${SCRYPT_PARAMS.p}$`)).toBe(true);
    expect(await verifyPassword("correct horse battery", hash)).toBe(true);
    expect(await verifyPassword("correct horse batterY", hash)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("salts every hash", async () => {
    expect(await hashPassword("same")).not.toEqual(await hashPassword("same"));
  });

  it("rejects malformed stored hashes", async () => {
    expect(await verifyPassword("x", "plaintext")).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyPassword("x", "scrypt$abc$8$1$AA$AA")).toBe(false);
  });
});

====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/privacy.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { canSeeStamp, personView, profileAccess, viewerOf, type StampSubject, type Viewer } from "@/server/privacy";

const SUBJECT_ID = "subject";

/** Every kind of viewer. A pending follow request grants nothing, so "requester" behaves like a stranger. */
const viewers: Record<string, { viewer: Viewer; follows: boolean }> = {
  anonymous: { viewer: null, follows: false },
  self: { viewer: { userId: SUBJECT_ID, staff: false }, follows: false },
  follower: { viewer: { userId: "follower", staff: false }, follows: true },
  requester: { viewer: { userId: "requester", staff: false }, follows: false },
  stranger: { viewer: { userId: "stranger", staff: false }, follows: false },
  moderator: { viewer: { userId: "mod", staff: true }, follows: false },
};

type Kind = keyof typeof viewers;
const FULL_FOR_PRIVATE: Kind[] = ["self", "follower", "moderator"];

describe("profileAccess: every viewer x every account state", () => {
  for (const [kind, { viewer, follows }] of Object.entries(viewers)) {
    it(`${kind} sees a public account in full`, () => {
      expect(profileAccess(viewer, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: false, deleted: false }, follows)).toBe("full");
    });
    it(`${kind} sees a private account ${FULL_FOR_PRIVATE.includes(kind as Kind) ? "in full" : "limited"}`, () => {
      expect(profileAccess(viewer, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: true, deleted: false }, follows)).toBe(FULL_FOR_PRIVATE.includes(kind as Kind) ? "full" : "limited");
    });
    it(`${kind} never sees a deleted account in full`, () => {
      expect(profileAccess(viewer, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: false, deleted: true }, follows)).toBe("limited");
      expect(profileAccess(viewer, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: true, deleted: true }, follows)).toBe("limited");
    });
  }

  it("a block (either direction) means limited, even for a follower of a public account; staff and self are unaffected", () => {
    for (const isPrivate of [false, true]) {
      const s = { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate, deleted: false };
      expect(profileAccess(viewers.stranger!.viewer, s, false, true)).toBe("limited");
      expect(profileAccess(viewers.follower!.viewer, s, true, true)).toBe("limited");
      expect(profileAccess(viewers.moderator!.viewer, s, false, true)).toBe("full");
      expect(profileAccess(viewers.self!.viewer, s, false, true)).toBe("full");
    }
  });

  it("a signed-out viewer can't be a follower even if the flag says so", () => {
    expect(profileAccess(null, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: true, deleted: false }, true)).toBe("limited");
  });

  it("a suspended account is limited to everyone but staff and itself", () => {
    const s = { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate: false, deleted: false, suspended: true };
    expect(profileAccess(viewers.stranger!.viewer, s, false)).toBe("limited");
    expect(profileAccess(viewers.follower!.viewer, s, true)).toBe("limited");
    expect(profileAccess(viewers.moderator!.viewer, s, false)).toBe("full");
  });
});

describe("personView: a limited view shows the handle and badge only", () => {
  const person = { handle: "maya", displayName: "Maya", verified: true };
  it("full shows everything, limited hides the name and photo", () => {
    expect(personView("full", person, "https://x/a.jpg")).toEqual({ handle: "maya", displayName: "Maya", avatarUrl: "https://x/a.jpg", verified: true });
    expect(personView("limited", person, "https://x/a.jpg")).toEqual({ handle: "maya", displayName: null, avatarUrl: null, verified: true });
  });
});

describe("canSeeStamp: every viewer x every stamp state", () => {
  const base: StampSubject = { authorId: SUBJECT_ID, hidden: false, removed: false, countable: true };
  const self = viewers.self!.viewer;
  const mod = viewers.moderator!.viewer;
  const stranger = viewers.stranger!.viewer;

  for (const [kind, { viewer }] of Object.entries(viewers)) {
    it(`${kind} sees a stamp exactly when they may see its author in full`, () => {
      for (const isPrivate of [false, true]) {
        const follows = viewers[kind]!.follows;
        const access = profileAccess(viewer, { id: SUBJECT_ID, isPrivate, deleted: false }, follows);
        expect(canSeeStamp(viewer, base, access)).toBe(access === "full");
      }
    });
  }

  it("hidden and removed stamps are for staff only, not even the author", () => {
    for (const state of [{ hidden: true }, { removed: true }]) {
      const s = { ...base, ...state };
      expect(canSeeStamp(mod, s, "full")).toBe(true);
      expect(canSeeStamp(self, s, "full")).toBe(false);
      expect(canSeeStamp(viewers.follower!.viewer, s, "full")).toBe(false);
      expect(canSeeStamp(stranger, s, "full")).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it("a block hides the stamp from the other side; the author still sees it; staff always do", () => {
    expect(canSeeStamp(stranger, base, "limited", true)).toBe(false);
    expect(canSeeStamp(viewers.follower!.viewer, base, "full", true)).toBe(false);
    expect(canSeeStamp(mod, base, "full", true)).toBe(true);
    expect(canSeeStamp(self, base, "full", true)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("App Review stamps open for their author and staff only", () => {
    const exempt = { ...base, countable: false };
    expect(canSeeStamp(self, exempt, "full")).toBe(true);
    expect(canSeeStamp(mod, exempt, "full")).toBe(true);
    expect(canSeeStamp(stranger, exempt, "full")).toBe(false);
    expect(canSeeStamp(viewers.follower!.viewer, exempt, "full")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("signed-out viewers see a stamp only of a public author", () => {
    expect(canSeeStamp(null, base, "full")).toBe(true);
    expect(canSeeStamp(null, base, "limited")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("viewerOf", () => {
  const actor = (role: "USER" | "MODERATOR" | "ADMIN") => ({ userId: "u", role, handle: "u", displayName: "U", geofenceExempt: false });
  it("staff are moderators and admins only", () => {
    expect(viewerOf(actor("MODERATOR"))?.staff).toBe(true);
    expect(viewerOf(actor("ADMIN"))?.staff).toBe(true);
    expect(viewerOf(actor("USER"))?.staff).toBe(false);
    expect(viewerOf(null)).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/storage.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { LocalDiskStorage, R2Storage, SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S, storageFromEnv, verifyLocalSignature } from "@/lib/storage";

const NOW = new Date("2026-09-26T03:32:10Z");

describe("local signed URLs", () => {
  const s = new LocalDiskStorage("/tmp/none");
  const parse = (u: string) => {
    const url = new URL(u, "http://x");
    return { key: url.pathname.replace("/api/uploads/", ""), exp: url.searchParams.get("exp"), sig: url.searchParams.get("sig") };
  };

  it("are valid for at least 15 minutes and at most 20", async () => {
    const { key, exp, sig } = parse(await s.signedUrl("photos/abc.png", NOW));
    const ttl = Number(exp) - NOW.getTime() / 1000;
    expect(ttl).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S);
    expect(ttl).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20 * 60);
    expect(verifyLocalSignature(key, exp, sig, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(verifyLocalSignature(key, exp, sig, new Date(Number(exp) * 1000))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("are stable within a 5-minute bucket (cacheable) and change across buckets", async () => {
    const a = await s.signedUrl("photos/abc.png", NOW);
    expect(await s.signedUrl("photos/abc.png", new Date(NOW.getTime() + 60_000))).toBe(a);
    expect(await s.signedUrl("photos/abc.png", new Date(NOW.getTime() + 5 * 60_000))).not.toBe(a);
  });

  it("can't be reused for another key or a longer expiry", async () => {
    const { exp, sig } = parse(await s.signedUrl("photos/abc.png", NOW));
    expect(verifyLocalSignature("photos/other.png", exp, sig, NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyLocalSignature("photos/abc.png", String(Number(exp) + 3600), sig, NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyLocalSignature("photos/abc.png", exp, null, NOW)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("R2 storage", () => {
  const cfg = { accountId: "acct123", accessKeyId: "AKIDTEST", secretAccessKey: "secret", bucket: "nostos-photos" };

  it("presigns private GET URLs against the R2 endpoint with a 20-minute expiry", async () => {
    const url = new URL(await new R2Storage(cfg).signedUrl("photos/abc.jpg", NOW));
    expect(url.host).toBe("nostos-photos.acct123.r2.cloudflarestorage.com");
    expect(url.pathname).toBe("/photos/abc.jpg");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("X-Amz-Expires")).toBe("1200");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("X-Amz-Signature")).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
    expect(url.searchParams.get("X-Amz-Date")).toBe("20260926T033000Z"); // floored to the 5-minute bucket
  });

  it("uploads with the right bucket, key and content type", async () => {
    const sent: { input: Record<string, unknown> }[] = [];
    const fake = { send: async (cmd: { input: Record<string, unknown> }) => void sent.push(cmd) } as unknown as S3Client;
    await new R2Storage(cfg, fake).put("photos/abc.jpg", Buffer.from("x"), "image/jpeg");
    expect(sent[0]!.input).toMatchObject({ Bucket: "nostos-photos", Key: "photos/abc.jpg", ContentType: "image/jpeg" });
  });

  it("rejects invalid keys", async () => {
    await expect(new R2Storage(cfg).signedUrl("../etc/passwd", NOW)).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

describe("storage driver selection", () => {
  it("defaults to local, picks R2 when configured, and fails loudly when misconfigured", () => {
    expect(storageFromEnv({})).toBeInstanceOf(LocalDiskStorage);
    expect(storageFromEnv({ STORAGE_DRIVER: "r2", R2_ACCOUNT_ID: "a", R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID: "b", R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: "c", R2_BUCKET: "d" })).toBeInstanceOf(R2Storage);
    expect(() => storageFromEnv({ STORAGE_DRIVER: "r2", R2_BUCKET: "d" })).toThrow(/R2_ACCOUNT_ID/);
    expect(() => storageFromEnv({ VERCEL: "1" })).toThrow(/Vercel/);
    expect(() => storageFromEnv({ STORAGE_DRIVER: "gcs" })).toThrow();
  });
});

== tests/unit/time.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { nightBounds, nightKey, zonedTimeToUtc } from "@/lib/time";

describe("night rollover in America/New_York (5:00am)", () => {
  it("assigns late-night hours to the previous evening's night", () => {
    // Sat 2026-09-26 02:30 EDT = 06:30Z -> Friday's night
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-09-26T06:30:00Z"))).toBe("2026-09-25");
    // Sat 04:59:59 EDT still Friday night
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-09-26T08:59:59Z"))).toBe("2026-09-25");
    // Sat 05:00:00 EDT starts Saturday's night
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-09-26T09:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-09-26");
  });

  it("uses local wall time, not UTC date", () => {
    // Fri 23:00 EDT = Sat 03:00Z; UTC date is Saturday but the night is Friday.
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-09-26T03:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-09-25");
  });

  it("rolls at 5am local across DST changes", () => {
    // Spring forward: Sun 2026-03-08. 04:59 EDT = 08:59Z -> night of Sat 03-07. 05:00 EDT = 09:00Z -> 03-08.
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-03-08T08:59:00Z"))).toBe("2026-03-07");
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-03-08T09:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-03-08");
    // Fall back: Sun 2026-11-01. 04:59 EST = 09:59Z -> 10-31. 05:00 EST = 10:00Z -> 11-01.
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-11-01T09:59:00Z"))).toBe("2026-10-31");
    expect(nightKey(new Date("2026-11-01T10:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-01");
  });

  it("computes night bounds, including the 23h/25h DST nights", () => {
    const fri = nightBounds("2026-09-25");
    expect(fri.start.toISOString()).toBe("2026-09-25T09:00:00.000Z");
    expect(fri.end.toISOString()).toBe("2026-09-26T09:00:00.000Z");
    const spring = nightBounds("2026-03-07");
    expect(spring.end.getTime() - spring.start.getTime()).toBe(23 * 3600_000);
    const fall = nightBounds("2026-10-31");
    expect(fall.end.getTime() - fall.start.getTime()).toBe(25 * 3600_000);
  });

  it("zonedTimeToUtc handles EST and EDT", () => {
    expect(zonedTimeToUtc(2026, 1, 15, 22).toISOString()).toBe("2026-01-16T03:00:00.000Z");
    expect(zonedTimeToUtc(2026, 7, 15, 22).toISOString()).toBe("2026-07-16T02:00:00.000Z");
  });
});

# Handoff

Nostos is an iPhone app (Expo, iOS only) for New York: you go to a place and get a stamp. Your profile is a passport book you open
and flip through; stamps appear on its pages. This version is strictly personal: no feed, no likes, no follows, no sharing. Only you
can see your passport. The look is black and brass.

Read first: `CLAUDE.md`, `ENGINE.md`, `NOSTOS-BUILD-PROMPT.md`, `NOSTOS-BUILD-PROMPT-2.md`, `DESIGN.md`. The only definition of green is
`scripts/gate.sh`. `LEDGER.md` is written by the gate only. Current combined checkout: `/Users/charlesjinn/nostos-codex`, branch `build-codex`; never push.
Claude's committed `build` work at e639860 has been imported into this working tree alongside Codex HEAD 42a6c0d.
The user requested an uncommitted merge and uncommitted fixes; do not commit without renewed authorization.

## What exists
- **Backend** (`backend/`: Next.js 15, Prisma, Postgres): accounts and sessions, the geofence check on every stamp, up to six photos
  per stamp with EXIF and GPS stripped, songs as metadata (iTunes Search proxy), neighborhoods and completion, pinned stamps (up to
  four), the place page, oldest-first page and slot numbering, moderation, the age gate, account deletion. The social layer is still
  there behind `SOCIAL_ENABLED` (default off), and 404s when off.
- **App** (`mobile/`): Passport (the book), Stamp (the stamp flow), Map, and Settings behind a gear. A development-only Design gallery.
- **Rules that must never break:** `CLAUDE.md` lists eleven; each has a test in `backend/tests/integration/invariants.test.ts`.

## Run it
```
cd /Users/charlesjinn/nostos-codex
(cd backend && docker compose up -d db)    # nostos-codex, Postgres 5438 only
(cd backend && pnpm db:seed && pnpm dev)   # nostos_codex, API on :3001
(cd mobile && npx expo start --go --port 8082) # scan the QR code in Expo Go
scripts/gate.sh                            # everything: typecheck, lint, tests, invariants, dependency ledger
```
Demo sign-in after the seed: `avaa@lastcall.dev` (or `maya_r@lastcall.dev`), password `lastcall-dev`. The phone and the Mac must be on
the same network (or use `npx expo start --tunnel`). See `mobile/README.md`.

## Try each thing on the phone
- **The book:** Passport tab. Tap the cover: it swings open to the bearer page. Drag from the outer edge of a page, or tap the edge,
  to turn it. "Index" under the book lists neighborhoods; tap one to jump to its first page.
- **Stamp a place:** the large center button. The list says "Here" or "Nearby" with distances. Take photos with the in-app camera (up
  to six), add a caption or a song, tap Stamp, and watch it land. To try it away from a place in a development build, long-press the
  Map to pretend you are there (the server still checks the geofence).
- **A stamp:** tap one on a page. The photos are on the right; drag them about (it saves), tap one for full screen and pinch.
  The ellipsis menu pins it, edits it or deletes it from your profile.
- **Map:** places you have stamped glow; tap a pin for its sheet and the place page.
- **The Design gallery (development builds only):** on the Welcome screen tap "Design gallery (development)", or in Settings under
  Development, or open the link `nostos://design` (in Expo Go: `exp://<your Mac's address>:8082/--/design`). Every piece of the design
  is there on made-up data with no account and no server.

## What needs your decision (`OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`)
- **L6:** the server saves the phone's position with each stamp (to audit the geofence check) and never shows it. The stamp screen
  says so. Is that wording right, and should the position be dropped after the check?
- **L7:** the privacy policy and terms still describe the social layer. Only recolored this session; the text needs a lawyer.
- L1 to L5 (songs terms, age gate, SAFE for Kids Act, geolocation disclosures, error reporting) are unchanged.
- Product questions 1 to 8 (ratings, place catalog, stamp frequency and others) are unchanged.

## What has not been run
**Nothing in session 2 has run on a phone.** The app type-checks, lints, and passes Jest, which tests the math and that screens render,
but the leather and foil shaders, the cover swing, the page flip, the stamp landing, the typewriter, the pinch zoom, the camera, haptics,
Reduce Motion, VoiceOver and the map have never been seen. `mobile/DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md` lists every effect to check. Expect to fix
things on first contact with a real device. Skia, `react-native-svg`, `expo-camera` and `expo-sensors` are claimed to work in Expo Go
from the Expo SDK 57 docs, not from a run.

## Where things are
`backend/src/app/api` (routes), `backend/src/server/services` (logic), `backend/src/contracts/api.ts` (the API contract, copied to
`mobile/src/api/contracts.ts` by `node scripts/sync-contracts.mjs`), `mobile/src/app` (screens), `mobile/src/features/book` (the
book), `mobile/src/theme` (Midnight), `reviews/` (one file per session), `DEPENDENCIES.md` (one line per dependency).

# Real-phone checklist: book-page fix

Use `/Users/charlesjinn/nostos-codex` only. Mac and iPhone should be on the same Wi-Fi. Start the isolated backend with `(cd backend && pnpm dev)` on port 3001, then `(cd mobile && npx expo start --go --port 8082)` and scan the QR code in Expo Go. If overriding the API URL, use the Mac's LAN address on port 3001, not the phone's localhost. Do not reset or reseed an existing database just for this checklist.

- [ ] **Backend connection:** sign in, open your passport and load nearby places. Confirm there is no connection error and requests reach the backend on 3001.
- [ ] **Camera:** open Stamp, take a photo inside the app, confirm the preview, remove one and take it again. Capture up to six; a seventh must be blocked. Return to the composer and confirm the photos remain.
- [ ] **Stamp creation:** at a place marked Here, add a caption or song and submit with photos. Confirm the landing, then reopen the passport and confirm the new stamp persists.
- [ ] **Every book page:** use an account with at least four visible stamps. Open the book: inside cover on the left, bearer page on the right. Turn through every spread forwards and backwards, using buttons and edge gestures; check both faces each time. On the final spread, page 2 (and later final right pages for a larger passport) must render. Next must stop at the end.
- [ ] **Open stamps:** tap a stamp on the first stamp page and the fourth stamp on page 2. Each must open the correct stamp with its caption/song/photos; return and repeat from the final spread.
- [ ] **Reduce Motion:** enable Settings > Accessibility > Motion > Reduce Motion on iPhone, relaunch Expo Go, and repeat the first/last-spread and stamp-opening checks. Both faces must remain visible and tappable during the reduced-motion transition.

Record iPhone model, iOS/Expo Go versions, Reduce Motion setting, and pass/fail for each item. Screenshots or a short recording of the first and final spreads help review failures. No device result has been recorded yet.
